File: mobile\src\api\client.ts

// AttendSure - Secure Mobile API Client with Automated Failover
// File: mobile/src/api/client.ts

import axios, { AxiosError, InternalAxiosRequestConfig } from 'axios';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import {
  getServerBaseUrl,
  resolveFastestServer,
  invalidateServerCache,
  cleanUrl,
} from '../services/configService';

export const AUTH_TOKEN_KEY = 'attendsure_auth_token';
const LEGACY_TOKEN_KEYS = ['user_token', 'attendsure_token'];

export const apiClient = axios.create({
  timeout: 8000,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    Accept: 'application/json',
  },
});

apiClient.interceptors.request.use(
  async (config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {
    // 1. Resolve and sanitize base URL (guarantees no trailing slashes or /api)
    const rawBaseUrl = await getServerBaseUrl();
    config.baseURL = cleanUrl(rawBaseUrl);

    // 2. Prevent duplicate /api/ prefixes and path traversal
    let requestPath = config.url || '';
    if (!requestPath.startsWith('http://') && !requestPath.startsWith('https://')) {
      const sanitized = requestPath
        .replace(/^\/+/, '')
        .replace(/\.\.\//g, '')
        .replace(/^(api\/)+/i, '');
      config.url = `/api/${sanitized}`;
    }

    // 3. Resolve stored authentication token
    let token = await AsyncStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
    if (!token) {
      for (const legacyKey of LEGACY_TOKEN_KEYS) {
        const legacyToken = await AsyncStorage.getItem(legacyKey);
        if (legacyToken) {
          token = legacyToken;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    // 4. Attach token with CRLF injection protection
    if (token && config.headers && !config.headers.Authorization) {
      const sanitizedToken = token.trim().replace(/[\r\n]/g, '');
      config.headers.Authorization = `Token ${sanitizedToken}`;
    }

    return config;
  },
  (error) => Promise.reject(error)
);

apiClient.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  async (error: AxiosError) => {
    const originalRequest = error.config as InternalAxiosRequestConfig & {
      _retryCount?: number;
    };

    // Clear stored credentials on 401 Unauthorized
    if (error.response?.status === 401) {
      await AsyncStorage.multiRemove([
        AUTH_TOKEN_KEY,
        'user_token',
        'attendsure_token',
        'attendsure_user',
        'attendsure_role',
      ]);
      return Promise.reject(error);
    }

    // Automated failover if network times out or drops
    const isNetworkError =
      !error.response ||
      error.code === 'ECONNABORTED' ||
      error.message?.includes('Network Error') ||
      error.message?.includes('timeout');

    if (isNetworkError && originalRequest) {
      originalRequest._retryCount = originalRequest._retryCount || 0;

      if (originalRequest._retryCount < 1) {
        originalRequest._retryCount += 1;
        invalidateServerCache();

        try {
          const newBaseUrl = await resolveFastestServer();
          originalRequest.baseURL = cleanUrl(newBaseUrl);
          return apiClient(originalRequest);
        } catch (discoveryError) {
          return Promise.reject(discoveryError);
        }
      }
    }

    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

export default apiClient;

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\branding\AttendSureBadge.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, Animated } from 'react-native';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';

const LOGO_SOURCE = require('../../assets/attendsure-logo.svg');

interface AttendSureBadgeProps {
  size?: number;
  animate?: boolean;
}

export const AttendSureBadge: React.FC<AttendSureBadgeProps> = ({
  size = 96,
  animate = false,
}) => {
  const scaleAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;
  const iconSize = Math.round(size * 0.62);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!animate) return;

    const breatheAnimation = Animated.loop(
      Animated.sequence([
        Animated.timing(scaleAnim, {
          toValue: 1.05,
          duration: 1200,
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
        Animated.timing(scaleAnim, {
          toValue: 1,
          duration: 1200,
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
      ])
    );

    breatheAnimation.start();
    return () => breatheAnimation.stop();
  }, [animate, scaleAnim]);

  return (
    <Animated.View
      style={[
        styles.badgeContainer,
        {
          width: size,
          height: size,
          borderRadius: size / 2,
          transform: [{ scale: scaleAnim }],
        },
      ]}
    >
      <Image
        source={LOGO_SOURCE}
        style={{ width: iconSize, height: iconSize }}
        contentFit="contain"
      />
    </Animated.View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  badgeContainer: {
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    borderWidth: 2,
    borderColor: '#e2e8f0',
    shadowColor: '#004b87',
    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 },
    shadowOpacity: 0.18,
    shadowRadius: 10,
    elevation: 6,
    overflow: 'hidden',
  },
});

export default AttendSureBadge;

import React from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { theme } from '../../constants/theme';

interface BadgeProps {
  label: string;
  variant?: 'success' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'info' | 'neutral';
}

export function Badge({ label, variant = 'neutral' }: BadgeProps) {
  const stylesMap = {
    success: { bg: theme.colors.successLight, text: theme.colors.successDark },
    warning: { bg: theme.colors.warningLight, text: theme.colors.warningDark },
    danger: { bg: theme.colors.dangerLight, text: theme.colors.dangerDark },
    info: { bg: theme.colors.primaryLight, text: theme.colors.primaryHover },
    neutral: { bg: theme.colors.surfaceSubtle, text: theme.colors.textSecondary },
  };

  const current = stylesMap[variant];

  return (
    <View style={[styles.badge, { backgroundColor: current.bg }]}>
      <Text style={[styles.text, { color: current.text }]}>{label}</Text>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  badge: {
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    paddingVertical: 3,
    borderRadius: theme.borderRadius.sm,
    alignSelf: 'flex-start',
  },
  text: {
    fontSize: 11,
    fontWeight: '700',
    letterSpacing: 0.3,
  },
});

File: mobile\src\components\common\Button.tsx
========================================================
import React from 'react';
import {
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ActivityIndicator,
  ViewStyle,
} from 'react-native';
import { theme } from '../../constants/theme';

interface ButtonProps {
  title: string;
  onPress: () => void;
  variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'danger' | 'outline';
  loading?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  style?: ViewStyle;
}

export function Button({
  title,
  onPress,
  variant = 'primary',
  loading = false,
  disabled = false,
  style,
}: ButtonProps) {
  const getVariantStyles = () => {
    switch (variant) {
      case 'danger':
        return { bg: theme.colors.danger, text: '#ffffff' };
      case 'secondary':
        return { bg: theme.colors.surfaceSubtle, text: theme.colors.textPrimary };
      case 'outline':
        return {
          bg: 'transparent',
          text: theme.colors.textSecondary,
          border: theme.colors.borderStrong,
        };
      case 'primary':
      default:
        return { bg: theme.colors.primary, text: '#ffffff' };
    }
  };

  const config = getVariantStyles();

  return (
    <TouchableOpacity
      style={[
        styles.button,
        { backgroundColor: config.bg, borderColor: config.border || 'transparent' },
        config.border ? styles.border : null,
        disabled && styles.disabled,
        style,
      ]}
      onPress={onPress}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      activeOpacity={0.8}
    >
      {loading ? (
        <ActivityIndicator color={config.text} size="small" />
      ) : (
        <Text style={[styles.text, { color: config.text }]}>{title}</Text>
      )}
    </TouchableOpacity>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  button: {
    height: 46,
    borderRadius: theme.borderRadius.md,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    paddingHorizontal: theme.spacing.md,
  },
  border: {
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
  disabled: {
    opacity: 0.5,
  },
  text: {
    fontSize: 14,
    fontWeight: '600',
  },
});

import React from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, Text, View, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { theme } from '../../constants/theme';

interface CardProps {
  title?: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  rightAction?: React.ReactNode;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  style?: ViewStyle;
}

export function Card({ title, subtitle, rightAction, children, style }: CardProps) {
  return (
    <View style={[styles.card, style]}>
      {(title || rightAction) && (
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <View style={styles.titleContainer}>
            {title && <Text style={styles.title}>{title}</Text>}
            {subtitle && <Text style={styles.subtitle}>{subtitle}</Text>}
          </View>
          {rightAction && <View>{rightAction}</View>}
        </View>
      )}
      <View style={styles.body}>{children}</View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  card: {
    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
    borderRadius: theme.borderRadius.lg,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: theme.colors.border,
    padding: theme.spacing.md,
    marginBottom: theme.spacing.md,
  },
  header: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    marginBottom: theme.spacing.sm,
    paddingBottom: theme.spacing.xs,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderBottomColor: theme.colors.surfaceSubtle,
  },
  titleContainer: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  title: {
    fontSize: 12,
    fontWeight: '700',
    color: theme.colors.textSecondary,
    textTransform: 'uppercase',
    letterSpacing: 0.5,
  },
  subtitle: {
    fontSize: 12,
    color: theme.colors.textMuted,
    marginTop: 2,
  },
  body: {
    marginTop: theme.spacing.xs,
  },
});

import React from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, View, TextInputProps } from 'react-native';
import { theme } from '../../constants/theme';

interface InputProps extends TextInputProps {
  label: string;
  error?: string;
}

export function Input({ label, error, style, ...rest }: InputProps) {
  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <Text style={styles.label}>{label}</Text>
      <TextInput
        style={[
          styles.input,
          error ? styles.inputError : null,
          style,
        ]}
        placeholderTextColor={theme.colors.textMuted}
        {...rest}
      />
      {error && <Text style={styles.errorText}>{error}</Text>}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    marginBottom: theme.spacing.md,
  },
  label: {
    fontSize: 13,
    fontWeight: '600',
    color: theme.colors.textPrimary,
    marginBottom: theme.spacing.xs,
  },
  input: {
    height: 46,
    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceSubtle,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: theme.colors.borderStrong,
    borderRadius: theme.borderRadius.md,
    paddingHorizontal: theme.spacing.md,
    fontSize: 14,
    color: theme.colors.textPrimary,
  },
  inputError: {
    borderColor: theme.colors.danger,
    backgroundColor: theme.colors.dangerLight,
  },
  errorText: {
    fontSize: 11,
    color: theme.colors.danger,
    marginTop: 4,
  },
});

/**
 * AttendSure - DepEd Manual of Style (DMOS) Mobile Header & Footer
 * File: mobile/src/components/reports/DepEdReportHeaderFooter.tsx
 */

import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, Platform } from 'react-native';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';

interface DepEdHeaderProps {
  leftLogoUrl?: string | null;  
  rightLogoUrl?: string | null; 
  region?: string;
  division?: string;
  formTitle: string;
  formSubtitle?: string;
}

interface DepEdFooterProps {
  schoolLogoUrl?: string | null; 
  address?: string;
  contactNumber?: string;
  email?: string;
}

const sanitizeImageUrl = (url?: string | null): string | null => {
  if (!url) return null;
  const clean = url.trim();
  if (
    clean.startsWith('http://') ||
    clean.startsWith('https://') ||
    clean.startsWith('data:image/') ||
    clean.startsWith('file://')
  ) {
    return clean;
  }
  return null;
};

const cleanText = (val?: string | null): string => {
  if (!val) return '';
  return val.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, ' ').trim();
};

export const DepEdReportHeader: React.FC<DepEdHeaderProps> = ({
  leftLogoUrl,
  rightLogoUrl,
  region,
  division,
  formTitle,
  formSubtitle,
}) => {
  const safeKagawaranLogo = sanitizeImageUrl(leftLogoUrl);
  const safeDepEdLogo = sanitizeImageUrl(rightLogoUrl);

  const cleanRegion = cleanText(region);
  const cleanDivision = cleanText(division);
  const cleanTitle = cleanText(formTitle);
  const cleanSub = cleanText(formSubtitle);

  return (
    <View style={styles.headerContainer}>
      <View style={styles.sealBox}>
        {safeKagawaranLogo ? (
          <Image
            source={{ uri: safeKagawaranLogo }}
            style={styles.sealImage}
            contentFit="contain"
            cachePolicy="memory-disk"
            transition={100}
          />
        ) : null}
      </View>

      <View style={styles.headerCenter}>
        <Text style={styles.republicText}>Republic of the Philippines</Text>
        <Text style={styles.depedText}>Department of Education</Text>

        {cleanRegion ? (
          <Text style={styles.officeText}>{cleanRegion.toUpperCase()}</Text>
        ) : null}

        {cleanDivision ? (
          <Text style={styles.officeText}>{cleanDivision.toUpperCase()}</Text>
        ) : null}

        <Text style={styles.formTitleText}>{cleanTitle}</Text>
        {cleanSub ? <Text style={styles.formSubText}>{cleanSub}</Text> : null}
      </View>

      <View style={styles.sealBox}>
        {safeDepEdLogo ? (
          <Image
            source={{ uri: safeDepEdLogo }}
            style={styles.sealImage}
            contentFit="contain"
            cachePolicy="memory-disk"
            transition={100}
          />
        ) : null}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

export const DepEdReportFooter: React.FC<DepEdFooterProps> = ({
  schoolLogoUrl,
  address,
  contactNumber,
  email,
}) => {
  const safeSchoolLogo = sanitizeImageUrl(schoolLogoUrl);

  const displayAddress = cleanText(address) || '—';
  const displayContact = cleanText(contactNumber) || '—';
  const displayEmail = cleanText(email) || '—';

  return (
    <View style={styles.footerContainer}>
      <View style={styles.sealBox}>
        {safeSchoolLogo ? (
          <Image
            source={{ uri: safeSchoolLogo }}
            style={styles.sealImage}
            contentFit="contain"
            cachePolicy="memory-disk"
            transition={100}
          />
        ) : null}
      </View>

      <View style={styles.footerDetailsCol}>
        <Text style={styles.footerDetailsText}>
          <Text style={styles.footerLabel}>Address: </Text>
          {displayAddress}
          {'\n'}
          <Text style={styles.footerLabel}>Contact No.: </Text>
          {displayContact} &bull; <Text style={styles.footerLabel}>Email: </Text>
          {displayEmail}
        </Text>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  headerContainer: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'flex-start',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    width: '100%',
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  sealBox: {
    width: 73,
    height: 73,
    minWidth: 73,
    minHeight: 73,
    flexShrink: 0,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  sealImage: {
    width: 73,
    height: 73,
  },
  headerCenter: {
    flex: 1,
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
  },
  republicText: {
    fontSize: 12,
    fontWeight: 'bold',
    color: '#000000',
    textAlign: 'center',
    fontFamily: Platform.select({
      ios: 'American Typewriter',
      android: 'serif',
      web: '"Old English Text MT", "Cloister Black", serif',
    }),
  },
  depedText: {
    fontSize: 18,
    fontWeight: 'bold',
    color: '#000000',
    textAlign: 'center',
    marginTop: 1,
    fontFamily: Platform.select({
      ios: 'American Typewriter',
      android: 'serif',
      web: '"Old English Text MT", "Cloister Black", serif',
    }),
  },
  officeText: {
    fontSize: 10,
    fontWeight: 'bold',
    color: '#000000',
    textAlign: 'center',
    marginTop: 1,
    fontFamily: Platform.select({
      ios: 'Tahoma',
      android: 'sans-serif',
      web: 'Tahoma, Arial, sans-serif',
    }),
  },
  formTitleText: {
    fontSize: 12.5,
    fontWeight: '900',
    color: '#000000',
    textAlign: 'center',
    marginTop: 4,
    textTransform: 'uppercase',
  },

    borderTopWidth: 1,
    borderTopColor: '#000000',
  },

    flex: 1,

    fontWeight: '700',
  },

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\components\reports\DTRReportView.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  TextInput,
  Modal,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { apiClient } from '../../api/client';

export interface DailyDTRRow {
  day: number;
  date_str: string;
  day_of_week: string;
  is_weekend: boolean;
  am_arrival: string;
  am_departure: string;
  pm_arrival: string;
  pm_departure: string;
  undertime_hours: number | string;
  undertime_minutes: number | string;
  is_loafing?: boolean;
  loafing_excused?: boolean;
  loafing_remarks?: string;
}

export interface DTRReportData {
  faculty_id: number;
  faculty_name: string;
  employee_id: string;
  department?: string;
  month: string;
  month_number: number;
  year: number;
  regular_days_hours: string;
  saturdays_hours: string;
  school_head: string;
  school_head_title?: string;
  dtr_submitted?: boolean;
  dept_head_approved: boolean;
  approved_by_dept_head_at?: string | null;
  user_role?: string;
  can_approve_dept?: boolean;
  can_clear_loafing?: boolean;
  rows: DailyDTRRow[];
  total_undertime_hours: number;
  total_undertime_minutes: number;
  days_present: number;
}

export interface DTRReportViewProps {
  data: DTRReportData | null;
  selectedMonth?: number;
  selectedYear?: number;
  onMonthChange?: (month: number, year: number) => void;
  submitting?: boolean;
  onSubmit?: () => void;
  onRefreshNeeded?: () => void;
}

const MONTH_NAMES = [
  'January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June',
  'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December',
];

export const DTRReportView: React.FC<DTRReportViewProps> = ({
  data,
  selectedMonth = new Date().getMonth() + 1,
  selectedYear = new Date().getFullYear(),
  onMonthChange,
  submitting = false,
  onSubmit,
  onRefreshNeeded,
}) => {
  if (!data) return null;

  const [viewSide, setViewSide] = useState<'FRONT' | 'BACK' | 'BOTH'>('FRONT');
  const [selectedViolationRow, setSelectedViolationRow] = useState<DailyDTRRow | null>(null);
  const [pardonRemarks, setPardonRemarks] = useState<string>('');
  const [actionProcessing, setActionProcessing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [showDatePickerModal, setShowDatePickerModal] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const isApproved = Boolean(data.dept_head_approved);
  const isSubmitted = Boolean(data.dtr_submitted);
  const resolvedHead = (data.school_head || 'School Head / In-Charge').trim();
  const resolvedTitle = (data.school_head_title || 'In-Charge').trim();

  // Normalize 31 rows
  const rawRows = Array.isArray(data.rows) ? data.rows : [];
  const rows31: DailyDTRRow[] = [];

  for (let dayNum = 1; dayNum <= 31; dayNum++) {
    const found = rawRows.find((r) => r.day === dayNum);
    if (found) {
      rows31.push(found);
    } else {
      rows31.push({
        day: dayNum,
        date_str: '',
        day_of_week: '',
        is_weekend: false,
        am_arrival: '',
        am_departure: '',
        pm_arrival: '',
        pm_departure: '',
        undertime_hours: '',
        undertime_minutes: '',
        is_loafing: false,
        loafing_excused: false,
      });
    }
  }

  // Month navigation handlers
  const handlePrevMonth = () => {
    if (!onMonthChange) return;
    if (selectedMonth === 1) {
      onMonthChange(12, selectedYear - 1);
    } else {
      onMonthChange(selectedMonth - 1, selectedYear);
    }
  };

  const handleNextMonth = () => {
    if (!onMonthChange) return;
    if (selectedMonth === 12) {
      onMonthChange(1, selectedYear + 1);
    } else {
      onMonthChange(selectedMonth + 1, selectedYear);
    }
  };

  // Excuse loafing
  const handleExcuseLoafing = async () => {
    if (!selectedViolationRow) return;

    const cleanRemarks = pardonRemarks.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, ' ').trim();
    if (!cleanRemarks) {
      Alert.alert('Required', 'Please enter official remarks or justification.');
      return;
    }

    setActionProcessing(true);
    try {
      await apiClient.post(`/reports/dtr/${data.faculty_id}/`, {
        action: 'EXCUSE_LOAFING',
        faculty_id: data.faculty_id,
        date_str: selectedViolationRow.date_str,
        remarks: cleanRemarks,
      });

      Alert.alert('Excused', `Departure on ${selectedViolationRow.date_str} excused.`);
      setSelectedViolationRow(null);
      setPardonRemarks('');
      if (onRefreshNeeded) onRefreshNeeded();
    } catch (err: any) {
      Alert.alert('Denied', err?.response?.data?.error || 'Could not excuse record.');
    } finally {
      setActionProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  // Front DTR Sheet
  const renderFrontSheet = () => (
    <View style={styles.sheetContainer}>
      {/* Draft Watermark */}
      {!isApproved && (
        <View style={styles.watermarkOverlay} pointerEvents="none">
          <View style={styles.watermarkRotate}>
            <View style={styles.watermarkBorder}>
              <Text style={styles.watermarkText}>DRAFT</Text>
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.watermarkSub}>NOT APPROVED BY DEPT HEAD</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      )}

      {/* Date Navigation Bar on Top of Form */}
      <View style={styles.topDateNavCard}>
        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.dateNavArrow} onPress={handlePrevMonth}>
          <Ionicons name="chevron-back" size={16} color="#004b87" />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.dateDisplayBtn}
          onPress={() => setShowDatePickerModal(true)}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
        >
          <Ionicons name="calendar-outline" size={14} color="#004b87" />
          <Text style={styles.dateDisplayText}>
            {MONTH_NAMES[selectedMonth - 1]} {selectedYear}
          </Text>
          <Ionicons name="caret-down-outline" size={11} color="#64748b" />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.dateNavArrow} onPress={handleNextMonth}>
          <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={16} color="#004b87" />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {/* Statutory Header */}
      <Text style={styles.formMeta}>Civil Service Form No. 48</Text>
      <Text style={styles.formTitle}>DAILY TIME RECORD</Text>
      <Text style={styles.formDivider}>----- o0o -----</Text>

      {/* Name Line */}
      <View style={styles.nameBlock}>
        <Text style={styles.nameText}>{data.faculty_name.toUpperCase()}</Text>
        <View style={styles.blackUnderline} />
        <Text style={styles.captionText}>(Name)</Text>
        {data.employee_id ? (
          <Text style={styles.empIdCaption}>Employee ID: {data.employee_id}</Text>
        ) : null}
      </View>

      {/* Month & Prescribed Hours */}
      <View style={styles.hoursBlock}>
        <Text style={styles.monthCoverText}>
          For the month of <Text style={styles.boldUnderline}>{MONTH_NAMES[selectedMonth - 1]}</Text>, 20
          <Text style={styles.boldUnderline}>{String(selectedYear).slice(-2)}</Text>
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.hoursSpecRow}>
          <Text style={styles.hoursSpecLabel}>
            Official hours of arrival{'\n'}and departure
          </Text>
          <View style={styles.hoursSpecBox}>
            <Text style={styles.hoursText}>
              Regular Days: <Text style={styles.underlineVal}>{data.regular_days_hours || '8:00 AM - 12:00 PM / 1:00 PM - 5:00 PM'}</Text>
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.hoursText}>
              Saturdays: <Text style={styles.underlineVal}>{data.saturdays_hours || 'As Required'}</Text>
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* 31-Day Grid Table */}
      <View style={styles.table}>
        <View style={styles.tableRowHeader}>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colDay, { borderBottomWidth: 0 }]}>
            <Text style={styles.thBold}>Days</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colHalf, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thBold}>A. M.</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colHalf, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thBold}>P. M.</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUt, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thBold}>UNDERTIME</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={[styles.tableRowHeader, { borderBottomWidth: 1.2 }]}>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colDay]} />
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thSmall}>ARRIVAL</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thSmall}>DEPARTURE</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thSmall}>ARRIVAL</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thSmall}>DEPARTURE</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUtPart, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thSmall}>Hours</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUtPart, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thSmall}>Minutes</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {rows31.map((row) => {
          const isViolating = Boolean(row.is_loafing && !row.loafing_excused);
          const isWeekend = Boolean(row.is_weekend);

          return (
            <View
              key={String(row.day)}
              style={[
                styles.tableRow,
                isViolating
                  ? { backgroundColor: '#fee2e2' }
                  : isWeekend
                  ? { backgroundColor: '#fafafa' }
                  : null,
              ]}
            >
              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colDay]}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => {
                    if (data.can_clear_loafing && row.is_loafing) {
                      setSelectedViolationRow(row);
                    }
                  }}
                  disabled={!data.can_clear_loafing || !row.is_loafing}
                  style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' }}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.dayText}>{row.day}</Text>
                  {row.is_loafing ? (
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        fontSize: 8,
                        fontWeight: '800',
                        marginLeft: 1,
                        color: isViolating ? '#dc2626' : '#059669',
                      }}
                    >
                      {isViolating ? '!' : '✓'}
                    </Text>
                  ) : null}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tdText, isViolating && styles.redText]}>
                  {row.am_arrival}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tdText, isViolating && styles.redText]}>
                  {row.am_departure}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tdText, isViolating && styles.redText]}>
                  {row.pm_arrival}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tdText, isViolating && styles.redText]}>
                  {row.pm_departure}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUtPart, styles.borderLeft]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tdText, row.undertime_hours ? styles.redText : null]}>
                  {row.undertime_hours}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUtPart, styles.borderLeft]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tdText, row.undertime_minutes ? styles.redText : null]}>
                  {row.undertime_minutes}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          );
        })}

        {/* Totals */}
        <View style={styles.totalRow}>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTotalSpan]}>
            <Text style={styles.totalThText}>TOTAL</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUtPart, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.totalValText}>
              {data.total_undertime_hours > 0 ? String(data.total_undertime_hours) : ''}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUtPart, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.totalValText}>
              {data.total_undertime_minutes > 0 ? String(data.total_undertime_minutes) : ''}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Certification */}
      <Text style={styles.certBody}>
        I CERTIFY on my honor that the above is a true and correct report of the
        hours of work performed, record of which was made daily at the time of
        arrival and departure from office.
      </Text>

      <View style={styles.sigBox}>
        <Text style={styles.sigName}>{data.faculty_name}</Text>
        <View style={styles.sigLine} />
        <Text style={styles.captionText}>(Signature of Faculty / Employee)</Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.sigBox}>
        <Text style={styles.verifiedText}>Verified as to prescribed office hours:</Text>
        <Text style={styles.sigName}>{resolvedHead}</Text>
        <View style={styles.sigLine} />
        <Text style={styles.captionText}>{resolvedTitle}</Text>
      </View>

      <Text style={styles.backNote}>(See Instructions on back)</Text>

      {/* Submit Button */}
      {!isSubmitted && onSubmit && (
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.submitBtn, submitting && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
          onPress={onSubmit}
          disabled={submitting}
        >
          {submitting ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
          ) : (
            <>
              <Ionicons name="send-outline" size={16} color="#ffffff" />
              <Text style={styles.submitBtnText}>Submit DTR</Text>
            </>
          )}
        </TouchableOpacity>
      )}
    </View>
  );

  // Back Instructions Sheet
  const renderBackSheet = () => (
    <View style={styles.sheetContainer}>
      <Text style={styles.instTitle}>INSTRUCTIONS</Text>
      <View style={styles.instLine} />

      <Text style={styles.instP}>
        Civil Service Form No. 48, after completion, Should be filed in the records
        of the Bureau or Office which submits the monthly report on Civil Service Form
        No. 3 to the Bureau of Civil Service.
      </Text>

      <Text style={styles.instP}>
        In lieu of the above, court interpreters and stenographers who accompany
        the judges of the Court of First Instance will fill out the daily time
        reports on this form in triplicate, after which they should be approved by
        the judge with whom service has been rendered, or by an officer of the
        Department of Justice authorized to do so. The original should be forwarded
        promptly after the end of the month to the Bureau of Civil Service, thru the
        Department of Justice; the duplicate to kept in the department of justice;
        and the triplicate, in the office of the Clerk of Court where service was
        rendered.
      </Text>

      <Text style={styles.instP}>
        In the space provided for the purpose on the other side will be indicated the
        office hours the employee is required to observe, as for example, "Regular
        days, 8:00 to 12:00 and 1:00 to 4:00; Saturdays 8:00 to 1:00."
      </Text>

      <Text style={styles.instP}>
        Attention is invited to paragraph 3, Civil Service Rule XV, Executive Order
        No. 5, series of 1909, which reads as follows:
      </Text>

      <View style={styles.instQuote}>
        <Text style={styles.instQuoteText}>
          "Each chief of a Bureau or Office shall require a daily record of
          attendance of all the officers and employees under him entitled to leave or
          absence or vacation (including faculty) to be kept on the proper form and
          also a systematic office record showing for each day all absences from
          duty from any cause whatever. At the beginning of each month he shall
          report to the Commissioner on the proper form of all absences from any cause
          whatever, including the exact amount of undertime of each person for each
          day. Officers or employees serving in the field or on the water need not be
          required to keep a daily record, but all absences of such employees must
          be included in the monthly report of changes and absences. Falsification of
          time records will render the offending officers or employee liable to
          summary removal from the service and criminal prosecution."
        </Text>
      </View>

      <Text style={styles.instNote}>
        <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>(NOTE: </Text>A record made from memory
        at sometime subsequent to the occurrence of an event is not reliable. Non
        observance of office hours deprives the employee of the leave privileges
        although he may have rendered overtime service. Where service rendered
        outside of the Office for the whole morning or afternoon, notation to that
        effect should be made clearly.)
      </Text>
    </View>
  );

  return (
    <View style={{ gap: 10 }}>
      {/* Side Toggle Bar */}
      <View style={styles.sideToggleRow}>
        {(['FRONT', 'BACK', 'BOTH'] as const).map((side) => {
          const active = viewSide === side;
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={side}
              style={[styles.sideToggleBtn, active && styles.sideToggleBtnActive]}
              onPress={() => setViewSide(side)}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.sideToggleText, active && styles.sideToggleTextActive]}>
                {side === 'FRONT' ? 'Front (DTR)' : side === 'BACK' ? 'Back (Instructions)' : 'Full View'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </View>

      {/* Sheet Output */}
      {(viewSide === 'BOTH' || viewSide === 'FRONT') && renderFrontSheet()}
      {(viewSide === 'BOTH' || viewSide === 'BACK') && renderBackSheet()}

      {/* Month & Year Picker Modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={showDatePickerModal}
        transparent={true}
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setShowDatePickerModal(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <Ionicons name="calendar" size={20} color="#004b87" />
              <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>Select Month & Year</Text>
            </View>

            {/* Year Selector */}
            <Text style={styles.pickerSectionLabel}>YEAR</Text>
            <View style={styles.yearGrid}>
              {[2024, 2025, 2026, 2027].map((y) => (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={y}
                  style={[styles.yearChip, selectedYear === y && styles.yearChipActive]}
                  onPress={() => onMonthChange && onMonthChange(selectedMonth, y)}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.yearChipText, selectedYear === y && styles.yearChipTextActive]}>
                    {y}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ))}
            </View>

            {/* Months 3x4 Grid */}
            <Text style={styles.pickerSectionLabel}>MONTH</Text>
            <View style={styles.monthsGrid}>
              {MONTH_NAMES.map((m, idx) => {
                const mNum = idx + 1;
                const active = selectedMonth === mNum;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={m}
                    style={[styles.monthChip, active && styles.monthChipActive]}
                    onPress={() => {
                      if (onMonthChange) onMonthChange(mNum, selectedYear);
                      setShowDatePickerModal(false);
                    }}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.monthChipText, active && styles.monthChipTextActive]}>
                      {m.slice(0, 3)}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.modalCloseBtn}
              onPress={() => setShowDatePickerModal(false)}
            >
              <Text style={styles.modalCloseBtnText}>Close</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* Loafing Review Modal */}
      {selectedViolationRow && (
        <Modal
          visible={Boolean(selectedViolationRow)}
          transparent={true}
          animationType="fade"
          onRequestClose={() => setSelectedViolationRow(null)}
        >
          <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
            <View style={styles.modalCard}>
              <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
                <Ionicons name="warning" size={24} color="#dc2626" />
                <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>Loafing Violation Review</Text>
              </View>

              <Text style={styles.modalDesc}>
                An unexcused departure was recorded for{' '}
                <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>{data.faculty_name}</Text> on{' '}
                <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>{selectedViolationRow.date_str}</Text>.
                If not excused, this day incurs a salary deduction.
              </Text>

              <Text style={styles.gateNote}>
                Gate Note: {selectedViolationRow.loafing_remarks || 'None'}
              </Text>

              <Text style={styles.modalInputLabel}>Official Remarks / Justification:</Text>
              <TextInput
                style={styles.modalInput}
                value={pardonRemarks}
                onChangeText={setPardonRemarks}
                placeholder="Enter justification for excusing departure..."
                placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
              />

              <View style={styles.modalActions}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.modalBtnCancel}
                  onPress={() => setSelectedViolationRow(null)}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.modalBtnCancelText}>Cancel</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.modalBtnConfirm, actionProcessing && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
                  onPress={handleExcuseLoafing}
                  disabled={actionProcessing}
                >
                  {actionProcessing ? (
                    <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Ionicons name="checkmark-done" size={15} color="#ffffff" />
                      <Text style={styles.modalBtnConfirmText}>Pardon &amp; Clear</Text>
                    </>
                  )}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  sideToggleRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderRadius: 8,
    padding: 3,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#cbd5e1',
    gap: 4,
  },
  sideToggleBtn: { flex: 1, paddingVertical: 6, alignItems: 'center', borderRadius: 6 },
  sideToggleBtnActive: { backgroundColor: '#004b87' },
  sideToggleText: { fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '800', color: '#64748b' },
  sideToggleTextActive: { color: '#ffffff' },

  sheetContainer: {
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderWidth: 1.5,
    borderColor: '#0f172a',
    borderRadius: 4,
    padding: 10,
    position: 'relative',
    overflow: 'hidden',
  },
  topDateNavCard: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#cbd5e1',
    borderRadius: 6,
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    paddingVertical: 5,
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  dateNavArrow: { padding: 4 },
  dateDisplayBtn: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, paddingVertical: 2 },
  dateDisplayText: { fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800', color: '#004b87' },

  watermarkOverlay: {
    position: 'absolute',
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
    right: 0,
    bottom: 0,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    zIndex: 10,
  },
  watermarkRotate: {
    transform: [{ rotate: '-32deg' }],
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  watermarkBorder: {
    borderWidth: 3,
    borderColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.22)',
    borderRadius: 8,
    paddingVertical: 2,
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    borderStyle: 'dashed',
  },
  watermarkText: {
    fontSize: 34,
    fontWeight: '900',
    color: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.22)',
    letterSpacing: 6,
  },
  watermarkSub: {
    fontSize: 8,
    fontWeight: '800',
    color: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.32)',
    letterSpacing: 1,
    marginTop: 2,
  },

  formMeta: { fontSize: 9, fontStyle: 'italic', color: '#0f172a' },
  formTitle: {
    fontSize: 15,
    fontWeight: '900',
    color: '#0f172a',
    textAlign: 'center',
    letterSpacing: 1,
    marginTop: 1,
  },
  formDivider: { fontSize: 9, textAlign: 'center', color: '#475569', marginBottom: 4 },
  nameBlock: { alignItems: 'center', marginVertical: 3 },
  nameText: { fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a' },
  blackUnderline: { width: '80%', height: 1, backgroundColor: '#0f172a', marginTop: 1 },
  captionText: { fontSize: 8, color: '#475569', marginTop: 1 },
  empIdCaption: { fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: '700', color: '#004b87', marginTop: 1 },
  hoursBlock: { marginVertical: 4 },
  monthCoverText: { fontSize: 10.5, color: '#0f172a', marginBottom: 4 },
  boldUnderline: { fontWeight: '800', textDecorationLine: 'underline' },
  hoursSpecRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' },
  hoursSpecLabel: { fontSize: 9, color: '#0f172a', width: 110, lineHeight: 12 },
  hoursSpecBox: { flex: 1, borderLeftWidth: 1.5, borderLeftColor: '#0f172a', paddingLeft: 6 },
  hoursText: { fontSize: 8.5, color: '#0f172a', marginVertical: 0.5 },
  underlineVal: { textDecorationLine: 'underline', fontWeight: '700' },

  table: { borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: '#0f172a', backgroundColor: '#ffffff', marginTop: 4 },
  tableRowHeader: { flexDirection: 'row', backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', borderBottomWidth: 1, borderColor: '#0f172a' },
  tableRow: { flexDirection: 'row', borderBottomWidth: 1, borderColor: '#cbd5e1', minHeight: 18, alignItems: 'center' },
  totalRow: { flexDirection: 'row', backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9', minHeight: 20, alignItems: 'center' },
  cell: { paddingVertical: 1, paddingHorizontal: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  borderLeft: { borderLeftWidth: 1, borderLeftColor: '#0f172a' },
  colDay: { width: 30 },
  colHalf: { flex: 2 },
  colTime: { flex: 1 },
  colUt: { flex: 1.2 },
  colUtPart: { flex: 0.6 },
  colTotalSpan: { width: 30 + 4 * 56 },
  thBold: { fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a', textAlign: 'center' },
  thSmall: { fontSize: 6.8, fontWeight: '800', color: '#334155', textAlign: 'center' },
  dayText: { fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a' },
  tdText: { fontSize: 8.5, color: '#334155', textAlign: 'center' },
  redText: { color: '#dc2626', fontWeight: '800' },
  totalThText: { fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '900', color: '#0f172a', textAlign: 'right', paddingRight: 6 },
  totalValText: { fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '900', color: '#dc2626', textAlign: 'center' },
  certBody: { fontSize: 8.5, color: '#0f172a', textAlign: 'justify', lineHeight: 12, marginTop: 8 },
  sigBox: { alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 12 },

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\components\reports\SF1ReportView.tsx

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  ScrollView,
  TouchableOpacity,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { DepEdReportHeader, DepEdReportFooter } from './DepEdReportHeaderFooter';

export interface Learner {
  id: number;
  lrn: string;
  name: string;
  sex: string;
  birthdate: string;
  age: number | string;
  birth_place?: string;
  mother_tongue: string;
  ethnic_group: string;
  religion: string;
  house_street: string;
  barangay: string;
  municipality_city: string;
  province: string;
  father_name: string;
  mother_maiden_name: string;
  guardian_name: string;
  guardian_relationship: string;
  parent_contact: string;
  remarks: string;
}

export interface SF1ReportViewProps {
  data: any;
  learners: Learner[];
}

export const SF1ReportView: React.FC<SF1ReportViewProps> = ({ data, learners }) => {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  if (!data) return null;

  const [groupingMode, setGroupingMode] = useState<'COMBINED' | 'SEPARATED'>('COMBINED');

  // Dynamic Adviser Resolution:
  // If the active logged-in employee is an assigned Faculty/Faculty, prioritize their verified name
  // to avoid showing placeholder/mock database seeds.
  const currentUser = user as Record<string, any> | null;
  const activeUserFullName =
    currentUser?.full_name ||
    `${currentUser?.first_name || ''} ${currentUser?.last_name || ''}`.trim() ||
    currentUser?.faculty_name ||
    currentUser?.username ||
    '';

  const userRole = String(
    currentUser?.role || currentUser?.system_role || 'EMPLOYEE'
  ).trim().toUpperCase();

  const isTeachingRole = ['FACULTY', 'FACULTY', 'EMPLOYEE'].includes(userRole);

  const resolvedAdviserName =
    (isTeachingRole && activeUserFullName.length > 0
      ? activeUserFullName
      : null) ||
    data.adviser_name ||
    activeUserFullName ||
    'Class Adviser';

  const resolvedSchoolHead = String(data.school_head || 'School Head').trim();

  // 1. Memoized Combined Learner List (Null-safe sorting)
  const sortedCombinedLearners = useMemo(() => {
    if (data.has_enrolled_students === false) return [];
    const raw: Learner[] = data.students || data.learners || learners || [];
    return [...raw].sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || ''));
  }, [data, learners]);

  // 2. Memoized Gender Breakdown (Single declaration to resolve TS2451)
  const { maleLearners, femaleLearners } = useMemo(() => {
    if (data.has_enrolled_students === false) {
      return { maleLearners: [], femaleLearners: [] };
    }
    const raw: Learner[] = data.students || data.learners || learners || [];
    const m = raw.filter((l: Learner) => l.sex === 'M' || l.sex === 'Male');
    const f = raw.filter((l: Learner) => l.sex === 'F' || l.sex === 'Female');
    return {
      maleLearners: [...m].sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '')),
      femaleLearners: [...f].sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '')),
    };
  }, [data, learners]);

  const maleCount = data.total_male ?? maleLearners.length;
  const femaleCount = data.total_female ?? femaleLearners.length;
  const totalCount = data.total_combined ?? (maleCount + femaleCount);

  const activeStudentList =
    groupingMode === 'COMBINED' ? sortedCombinedLearners : [...maleLearners, ...femaleLearners];
  const isListEmpty = activeStudentList.length === 0;

  // Sanitized guardian parser to prevent text overflow & injection
  const parseGuardian = (name: string, rel: string) => {
    const commonRel = ['MOTHER', 'FATHER', 'AUNT', 'UNCLE', 'GRANDMOTHER', 'GRANDFATHER', 'GUARDIAN'];
    const cleanName = (name || '').replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, ' ').trim();
    const cleanRel = (rel || '').replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, ' ').trim();
    if (commonRel.includes(cleanName.toUpperCase()) && !commonRel.includes(cleanRel.toUpperCase())) {
      return { gName: cleanRel, gRel: cleanName };
    }
    return { gName: cleanName, gRel: cleanRel.toLowerCase() === 'mather' ? 'Mother' : cleanRel };
  };

  const leftLogoUrl = data.left_logo || null;
  const rightLogoUrl = data.right_logo || null;

  return (
    <View style={styles.sheetContainer}>
      {/* Layout Mode Control Bar */}
      <View style={styles.controlBar}>
        <Text style={styles.controlLabel}>Class List Layout:</Text>
        <View style={styles.toggleRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.toggleBtn, groupingMode === 'COMBINED' && styles.toggleBtnActive]}
            onPress={() => setGroupingMode('COMBINED')}
          >
            <Ionicons
              name="people-outline"
              size={13}
              color={groupingMode === 'COMBINED' ? '#ffffff' : '#64748b'}
            />
            <Text
              style={[styles.toggleText, groupingMode === 'COMBINED' && styles.toggleTextActive]}
            >
              One List
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.toggleBtn, groupingMode === 'SEPARATED' && styles.toggleBtnActive]}
            onPress={() => setGroupingMode('SEPARATED')}
          >
            <Ionicons
              name="git-branch-outline"
              size={13}
              color={groupingMode === 'SEPARATED' ? '#ffffff' : '#64748b'}
            />
            <Text
              style={[styles.toggleText, groupingMode === 'SEPARATED' && styles.toggleTextActive]}
            >
              By Sex
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Full Sheet Document Horizontal Scroll (960px Canvas) */}
      <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={true}>
        <View style={styles.officialDocumentCanvas}>
          {/* ========================================================
              1. DYNAMIC DEPED DMOS HEADER (FROM DATABASE)
             ======================================================== */}
          <DepEdReportHeader
            leftLogoUrl={leftLogoUrl}
            rightLogoUrl={rightLogoUrl}
            region={data.region}
            division={data.division}
            formTitle="School Form 1 (SF 1) School Register"
            formSubtitle="(This replaces Form 1, Master List & STS Form 2)"
          />

          {/* ========================================================
              2. METADATA GRID (FROM DATABASE)
             ======================================================== */}
          <View style={styles.metaGrid}>
            <View style={styles.metaRow}>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>School ID: </Text>
                {data.school_id || '—'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>Region: </Text>
                {data.region || '—'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>Division: </Text>
                {data.division || '—'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>District: </Text>
                {data.district || '—'}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.metaRow}>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>School Name: </Text>
                {data.school_name || '—'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>School Year: </Text>
                {data.academic_year || '—'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>Grade Level: </Text>
                {data.grade_level || '—'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>Section: </Text>
                {data.section_name || '—'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* ========================================================
              3. SF1 OFFICIAL TABLE
             ======================================================== */}
          <View style={styles.table}>
            <View style={styles.thRow}>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 32 }]}>#</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 95 }]}>LRN</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 170 }]}>
                LEARNER'S NAME{'\n'}(Last, First, Middle)
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 38 }]}>SEX</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 75 }]}>BIRTH DATE</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 36 }]}>AGE</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 85 }]}>MOTHER TONGUE</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 75 }]}>IP GROUP</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 75 }]}>RELIGION</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 140 }]}>ADDRESS</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 120 }]}>FATHER'S NAME</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 120 }]}>MOTHER'S MAIDEN</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 110 }]}>GUARDIAN</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>CONTACT</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 80 }]}>REMARKS</Text>
            </View>

            {isListEmpty ? (
              <View style={{ padding: 24, alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#64748b' }}>
                  {data.notice || `No students enrolled in Section ${data.section_name || 'this class'}.`}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ) : groupingMode === 'COMBINED' ? (
              activeStudentList.map((learner: Learner, idx: number) => {
                const { gName } = parseGuardian(
                  learner.guardian_name,
                  learner.guardian_relationship
                );
                return (
                  <View key={String(learner.id || idx)} style={styles.tdRow}>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 32 }]}>{idx + 1}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 95, fontWeight: '700' }]}>{learner.lrn}</Text>
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.td,
                        { width: 170, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4, fontWeight: '700' },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {learner.name}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 38 }]}>{learner.sex}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 75 }]}>{learner.birthdate}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 36 }]}>{learner.age}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 85 }]}>{learner.mother_tongue}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 75 }]}>{learner.ethnic_group || 'N/A'}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 75 }]}>{learner.religion || 'N/A'}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 140, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                      {learner.barangay || learner.house_street || ''}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 120, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                      {learner.father_name || 'N/A'}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 120, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                      {learner.mother_maiden_name || 'N/A'}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 110, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                      {gName || 'N/A'}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 90 }]}>{learner.parent_contact || 'N/A'}</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 80 }]}>{learner.remarks || ''}</Text>
                  </View>
                );
              })
            ) : (
              <>
                <View style={styles.genderHeader}>
                  <Text style={styles.genderHeaderText}>MALE ({maleLearners.length})</Text>
                </View>
                {maleLearners.map((learner: Learner, idx: number) => {
                  const { gName } = parseGuardian(
                    learner.guardian_name,
                    learner.guardian_relationship
                  );
                  return (
                    <View key={String(learner.id || idx)} style={styles.tdRow}>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 32 }]}>{idx + 1}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 95, fontWeight: '700' }]}>{learner.lrn}</Text>
                      <Text
                        style={[
                          styles.td,
                          { width: 170, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4, fontWeight: '700' },
                        ]}
                      >
                        {learner.name}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 38 }]}>M</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 75 }]}>{learner.birthdate}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 36 }]}>{learner.age}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 85 }]}>{learner.mother_tongue}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 75 }]}>{learner.ethnic_group || 'N/A'}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 75 }]}>{learner.religion || 'N/A'}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 140, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                        {learner.barangay || ''}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 120, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                        {learner.father_name || 'N/A'}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 120, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                        {learner.mother_maiden_name || 'N/A'}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 110, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                        {gName || 'N/A'}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 90 }]}>{learner.parent_contact || 'N/A'}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 80 }]}>{learner.remarks || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                  );
                })}

                <View style={[styles.genderHeader, { backgroundColor: '#fdf2f8' }]}>
                  <Text style={[styles.genderHeaderText, { color: '#db2777' }]}>
                    FEMALE ({femaleLearners.length})
                  </Text>
                </View>
                {femaleLearners.map((learner: Learner, idx: number) => {
                  const { gName } = parseGuardian(
                    learner.guardian_name,
                    learner.guardian_relationship
                  );
                  return (
                    <View key={String(learner.id || idx)} style={styles.tdRow}>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 32 }]}>{idx + 1}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 95, fontWeight: '700' }]}>{learner.lrn}</Text>
                      <Text
                        style={[
                          styles.td,
                          { width: 170, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4, fontWeight: '700' },
                        ]}
                      >
                        {learner.name}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 38 }]}>F</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 75 }]}>{learner.birthdate}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 36 }]}>{learner.age}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 85 }]}>{learner.mother_tongue}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 75 }]}>{learner.ethnic_group || 'N/A'}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 75 }]}>{learner.religion || 'N/A'}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 140, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                        {learner.barangay || ''}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 120, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                        {learner.father_name || 'N/A'}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 120, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                        {learner.mother_maiden_name || 'N/A'}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 110, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                        {gName || 'N/A'}
                      </Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 90 }]}>{learner.parent_contact || 'N/A'}</Text>
                      <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 80 }]}>{learner.remarks || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                  );
                })}
              </>
            )}
          </View>

          {/* ========================================================
              4. OFFICIAL DEPED INDICATORS & REMARKS GUIDE BOX
             ======================================================== */}
          <View style={styles.remarksGuideContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.remarksGuideTitle}>
              List and Code of Indicators under REMARKS column:
            </Text>
            <View style={styles.remarksGrid}>
              <View style={styles.remarksCol}>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Transferred Out (T/O): </Text>Name of Public (P) / Private (PR) School &amp; Effectivity Date
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Transferred In (T/I): </Text>Name of Public (P) / Private (PR) School &amp; Effectivity Date
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>CCT Recipient: </Text>CCT C/H ID Number &amp; Effectivity Date
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Balik-Aral (B/A): </Text>Name of school last attended &amp; Year
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={styles.remarksCol}>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Dropped Out (DRP): </Text>Reason and Effectivity Date
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Late Enrollment (LE): </Text>Reason for enrollment beyond 1st Friday of June
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Learner With Disability (LWD): </Text>Specify condition
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Accelerated (ACL): </Text>Specify Level &amp; Effectivity Date
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* ========================================================
              5. SUMMARY BOX & DYNAMIC SIGNATURES
             ======================================================== */}
          <View style={styles.summaryBox}>
            <View style={styles.summaryInfoCol}>
              <Text style={styles.summaryTitle}>Registered Learners Summary</Text>
              <Text style={styles.summaryText}>
                Male: <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>{maleCount}</Text>
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.summaryText}>
                Female: <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>{femaleCount}</Text>
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.summaryText}>
                Total Enrolled: <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>{totalCount}</Text>
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.signCol}>
              {/* Prepared by: Class Adviser (Dynamic current user) */}
              <View style={styles.signBlock}>
                <Text style={styles.signName}>{resolvedAdviserName}</Text>
                <View style={styles.signLine} />
                <Text style={styles.signCaption}>Prepared by (Class Adviser)</Text>
              </View>

              {/* Certified Correct: School Head */}
              <View style={styles.signBlock}>
                <Text style={styles.signName}>{resolvedSchoolHead}</Text>
                <View style={styles.signLine} />
                <Text style={styles.signCaption}>Certified Correct (School Head)</Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* ========================================================
              6. DYNAMIC DEPED DMOS FOOTER (FROM DATABASE)
             ======================================================== */}
          <DepEdReportFooter
            schoolLogoUrl={data.school_logo || data.right_logo || null}
            address={data.school_address || data.address}
            contactNumber={data.school_contact || data.contact_number}
            email={data.school_email || data.email}
          />
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  sheetContainer: {
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderWidth: 1.5,
    borderColor: '#0f172a',
