// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\components\reports\SF2ReportView.tsx

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  ScrollView,
} from 'react-native';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { DepEdReportHeader, DepEdReportFooter } from './DepEdReportHeaderFooter';

export interface SchoolDay {
  dateNumber: number;
  dayOfWeek: 'M' | 'T' | 'W' | 'TH' | 'F';
  fullDate?: string;
}

export interface SF2Learner {
  id: number;
  lrn: string;
  name: string;
  sex: 'M' | 'F';
  attendance: { [dayNumber: number]: string | number };
  total_absent: number;
  total_tardy: number;
  remarks?: string;
}

export interface SF2ReportViewProps {
  data: any;
  learners?: any[];
}

export const SF2ReportView: React.FC<SF2ReportViewProps> = ({ data }) => {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  if (!data) return null;

  // 1. Dynamic Faculty Signatory Resolution
  const currentUser = user as Record<string, any> | null;
  const activeUserFullName =
    currentUser?.full_name ||
    `${currentUser?.first_name || ''} ${currentUser?.last_name || ''}`.trim() ||
    currentUser?.faculty_name ||
    currentUser?.username ||
    '';

  const userRole = String(
    currentUser?.role || currentUser?.system_role || 'EMPLOYEE'
  ).trim().toUpperCase();

  const isTeachingRole = ['FACULTY', 'FACULTY', 'EMPLOYEE'].includes(userRole);

  const resolvedFacultyName =
    (isTeachingRole && activeUserFullName.length > 0
      ? activeUserFullName
      : null) ||
    data.adviser_name ||
    activeUserFullName ||
    'Class Adviser';

  const resolvedSchoolHead = String(data.school_head || 'School Head / Principal').trim();

  // 2. Safe Array Normalization
  const schoolDays: SchoolDay[] = Array.isArray(data.school_days) ? data.school_days : [];
  const males: SF2Learner[] = Array.isArray(data.males) ? data.males : [];
  const females: SF2Learner[] = Array.isArray(data.females) ? data.females : [];

  const standardSchoolDays = useMemo(() => {
    const list: SchoolDay[] = [];
    const maxDays = 25;
    const daysCycle: Array<'M' | 'T' | 'W' | 'TH' | 'F'> = ['M', 'T', 'W', 'TH', 'F'];

    for (let i = 0; i < maxDays; i++) {
      if (schoolDays[i]) {
        list.push(schoolDays[i]);
      } else {
        list.push({
          dateNumber: i + 1,
          dayOfWeek: daysCycle[i % 5],
        });
      }
    }
    return list;
  }, [schoolDays]);

  // 3. Totals Summary Cached with useMemo
  const totalsSummary = useMemo(() => {
    const maleAbsent = males.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (Number(cur.total_absent) || 0), 0);
    const maleTardy = males.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (Number(cur.total_tardy) || 0), 0);
    const femaleAbsent = females.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (Number(cur.total_absent) || 0), 0);
    const femaleTardy = females.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (Number(cur.total_tardy) || 0), 0);

    return {
      maleAbsent,
      maleTardy,
      femaleAbsent,
      femaleTardy,
      combinedAbsent: maleAbsent + femaleAbsent,
      combinedTardy: maleTardy + femaleTardy,
    };
  }, [males, females]);

  // 4. Daily Column Tallies
  const dailyAttendanceTotals = useMemo(() => {
    const maxCols = 25;
    const malesCount: number[] = new Array(maxCols).fill(0);
    const femalesCount: number[] = new Array(maxCols).fill(0);
    const combinedCount: number[] = new Array(maxCols).fill(0);

    standardSchoolDays.forEach((day, idx) => {
      if (idx >= maxCols) return;
      let mPresent = 0;
      let fPresent = 0;

      males.forEach((m) => {
        const raw = String(m.attendance?.[day.dateNumber] ?? '').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (
          raw === 'present' ||
          raw === 'tardy' ||
          raw === 'late' ||
          raw === 'cutting' ||
          raw === '0' ||
          raw === ''
        ) {
          mPresent++;
        }
      });

      females.forEach((f) => {
        const raw = String(f.attendance?.[day.dateNumber] ?? '').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (
          raw === 'present' ||
          raw === 'tardy' ||
          raw === 'late' ||
          raw === 'cutting' ||
          raw === '0' ||
          raw === ''
        ) {
          fPresent++;
        }
      });

      malesCount[idx] = mPresent;
      femalesCount[idx] = fPresent;
      combinedCount[idx] = mPresent + fPresent;
    });

    return { males: malesCount, females: femalesCount, combined: combinedCount };
  }, [standardSchoolDays, males, females]);

  // 5. Official DepEd Cell Notation Renderer
  const renderAttendanceCell = (status?: string | number | null) => {
    const s = String(status ?? '').toLowerCase().trim();

    if (s === 'absent' || s === 'x' || s === '1' || s === 'a') {
      return (
        <View style={styles.attCell}>
          <Text style={styles.textAbsent}>(x)</Text>
        </View>
      );
    }

    if (s === 'tardy' || s === 'late' || s === '2' || s === 't') {
      return (
        <View style={styles.attCell}>
          <View style={styles.triangleUpperShaded} />
        </View>
      );
    }

    if (s === 'cutting' || s === 'cc' || s === '3' || s === 'c') {
      return (
        <View style={styles.attCell}>
          <View style={styles.triangleLowerShaded} />
        </View>
      );
    }

    return <View style={styles.attCell} />;
  };

  const isListEmpty = males.length === 0 && females.length === 0;

  const metrics = data.metrics || {};
  const maleEnrolmentJune = metrics?.enrolment_june?.m ?? males.length;
  const femaleEnrolmentJune = metrics?.enrolment_june?.f ?? females.length;
  const totalEnrolmentJune = maleEnrolmentJune + femaleEnrolmentJune;

  const maleRegisteredEnd = males.length;
  const femaleRegisteredEnd = females.length;
  const totalRegisteredEnd = maleRegisteredEnd + femaleRegisteredEnd;

  const percentageEnrolment =
    totalEnrolmentJune > 0 ? ((totalRegisteredEnd / totalEnrolmentJune) * 100).toFixed(2) : '100.00';

  const adaM = Number(metrics?.average_daily_attendance?.m ?? 0).toFixed(1);
  const adaF = Number(metrics?.average_daily_attendance?.f ?? 0).toFixed(1);
  const adaTotal = Number(metrics?.average_daily_attendance?.total ?? (Number(adaM) + Number(adaF))).toFixed(1);

  const percAttM = Number(metrics?.percentage_attendance?.m ?? 0).toFixed(1);
  const percAttF = Number(metrics?.percentage_attendance?.f ?? 0).toFixed(1);
  const percAttTotal = Number(metrics?.percentage_attendance?.total ?? 0).toFixed(1);

  const leftLogoUrl = data.left_logo || null;
  const rightLogoUrl = data.right_logo || null;

  return (
    <View style={styles.sheetOuterWrapper}>
      <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={true}>
        <View style={styles.officialDocumentCanvas}>
          <DepEdReportHeader
            leftLogoUrl={leftLogoUrl}
            rightLogoUrl={rightLogoUrl}
            region={data.region}
            division={data.division}
            formTitle="School Form 2 (SF2) Daily Attendance Report of Learners"
            formSubtitle="(This replaced Form 1, Form 2 & STS Form 4- Absenteeism and Dropout Profile)"
          />

          <View style={styles.metaTable}>
            <View style={styles.metaRow}>
              <View style={[styles.metaCol, { flex: 1.2 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>School ID </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.metaValue}>{data.school_id || '—'}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.metaCol, { flex: 1.2 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>School Year </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.metaValue}>{data.academic_year || data.school_year || '—'}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.metaCol, { flex: 1.6 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Report for the Month of </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.metaValue}>{data.month || '—'} {data.year || ''}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={[styles.metaRow, { borderTopWidth: 1, borderTopColor: '#000000' }]}>
              <View style={[styles.metaCol, { flex: 1.8 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Name of School </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.metaValue}>{data.school_name || '—'}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.metaCol, { flex: 1 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Grade Level </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.metaValue}>{data.grade_level || '—'}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.metaCol, { flex: 1.2 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Section </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.metaValue}>{data.section_name || '—'}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.matrixTable}>
            <View style={styles.tableRowHeader}>
              <View style={[styles.thCell, { width: 26 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.thText}>#</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.thCell, { width: 175, paddingHorizontal: 3 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.thText}>LEARNER'S NAME</Text>
                <Text style={styles.thSubText}>(Last Name, First Name, Middle Name)</Text>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.daysContainer}>
                <View style={styles.daysTopBanner}>
                  <Text style={styles.daysBannerText}>
                    (1st row for date, 2nd row for Day: M,T,W,TH,F)
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.daysRowNumbers}>
                  {standardSchoolDays.map((sd, colIdx) => (
                    <View key={`dnum-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayColCell}>
                      <Text style={styles.dayNumText}>{sd.dateNumber}</Text>
                    </View>
                  ))}
                </View>
                <View style={styles.daysRowLetters}>
                  {standardSchoolDays.map((sd, colIdx) => (
                    <View key={`dday-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayColCell}>
                      <Text style={styles.dayLetterText}>{sd.dayOfWeek}</Text>
                    </View>
                  ))}
                </View>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.thCell, { width: 70 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.thText}>Total for the{'\n'}Month</Text>
                <View style={styles.absTarSubRow}>
                  <View style={[styles.absTarCell, { borderRightWidth: 1, borderColor: '#000000' }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.thSmall}>ABSENT</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={styles.absTarCell}>
                    <Text style={styles.thSmall}>TARDY</Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.thCell, { width: 155, paddingHorizontal: 4 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.thText}>
                  REMARK/S (If DROPPED OUT, state reason, please refer to legend number 2.{'\n'}
                  If TRANSFERRED IN/OUT, write the name of School.)
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            {isListEmpty ? (
              <View style={styles.emptyBox}>
                <Text style={styles.emptyText}>
                  {data.notice || `No student attendance records found for Section ${data.section_name}.`}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ) : (
              <>
                {males.map((m, rowIdx) => (
                  <View key={`male-row-${rowIdx}-${m.id || rowIdx}`} style={styles.tableRowData}>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 26 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdText}>{rowIdx + 1}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 175, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdLearnerName} numberOfLines={1}>{m.name}</Text>
                    </View>

                    {standardSchoolDays.map((sd, colIdx) => {
                      const status = m.attendance?.[sd.dateNumber];
                      return (
                        <View key={`m-${m.id || rowIdx}-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayDataCell}>
                          {renderAttendanceCell(status)}
                        </View>
                      );
                    })}

                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdTotalAbsent}>{m.total_absent || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdTotalTardy}>{m.total_tardy || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 155, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdRemarks} numberOfLines={1}>{m.remarks || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                  </View>
                ))}

                <View style={styles.tallyRow}>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 201, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 6 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.tallyLabel}>&larr; MALE | TOTAL Per Day &rarr;</Text>
                  </View>
                  {standardSchoolDays.map((_, colIdx) => (
                    <View key={`m-tot-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayDataCell}>
                      <Text style={styles.tallyCountText}>{dailyAttendanceTotals.males[colIdx] || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                  ))}
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.tallyCountText}>{totalsSummary.maleAbsent || ''}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.tallyCountText}>{totalsSummary.maleTardy || ''}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 155 }]} />
                </View>

                {females.map((f, rowIdx) => (
                  <View key={`female-row-${rowIdx}-${f.id || rowIdx}`} style={styles.tableRowData}>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 26 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdText}>{rowIdx + 1}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 175, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdLearnerName} numberOfLines={1}>{f.name}</Text>
                    </View>

                    {standardSchoolDays.map((sd, colIdx) => {
                      const status = f.attendance?.[sd.dateNumber];
                      return (
                        <View key={`f-${f.id || rowIdx}-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayDataCell}>
                          {renderAttendanceCell(status)}
                        </View>
                      );
                    })}

                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdTotalAbsent}>{f.total_absent || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdTotalTardy}>{f.total_tardy || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 155, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdRemarks} numberOfLines={1}>{f.remarks || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                  </View>
                ))}

                <View style={styles.tallyRow}>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 201, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 6 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.tallyLabel}>&larr; FEMALE | TOTAL Per Day &rarr;</Text>
                  </View>
                  {standardSchoolDays.map((_, colIdx) => (
                    <View key={`f-tot-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayDataCell}>
                      <Text style={styles.tallyCountText}>{dailyAttendanceTotals.females[colIdx] || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                  ))}
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.tallyCountText}>{totalsSummary.femaleAbsent || ''}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.tallyCountText}>{totalsSummary.femaleTardy || ''}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 155 }]} />
                </View>

                <View style={[styles.tallyRow, { backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9' }]}>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 201, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 6 }]}>
                    <Text style={[styles.tallyLabel, { fontWeight: '900' }]}>Combined TOTAL PER DAY</Text>
                  </View>
                  {standardSchoolDays.map((_, colIdx) => (
                    <View key={`c-tot-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayDataCell}>
                      <Text style={[styles.tallyCountText, { fontWeight: '900' }]}>
                        {dailyAttendanceTotals.combined[colIdx] || ''}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  ))}
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                    <Text style={[styles.tallyCountText, { fontWeight: '900' }]}>
                      {totalsSummary.combinedAbsent || ''}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                    <Text style={[styles.tallyCountText, { fontWeight: '900' }]}>
                      {totalsSummary.combinedTardy || ''}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 155 }]} />
                </View>
              </>
            )}
          </View>

          <View style={styles.bottomBlockContainer}>
            <View style={styles.guidelinesCol}>
              <Text style={styles.blockHeading}>GUIDELINES:</Text>
              <Text style={styles.guidelineP}>
                1. The attendance shall be accomplished daily. Refer to the codes for checking learners' attendance.
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.guidelineP}>
                2. Dates shall be written in the preceding columns beside Learner's Name.
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.guidelineP}>3. To compute the following:</Text>

              <View style={styles.formulaRow}>
                <Text style={styles.formulaLabel}>a. Percentage of Enrolment =</Text>
                <View style={styles.fractionWrap}>
                  <Text style={styles.fractionNumerator}>Registered Learner as of End of the Month</Text>
                  <View style={styles.fractionBar} />
                  <Text style={styles.fractionDenominator}>Enrolment as of 1st Friday of June</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.multiplyText}>x 100</Text>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.formulaRow}>
                <Text style={styles.formulaLabel}>b. Average Daily Attendance =</Text>
                <View style={styles.fractionWrap}>
                  <Text style={styles.fractionNumerator}>Total Daily Attendance</Text>
                  <View style={styles.fractionBar} />
                  <Text style={styles.fractionDenominator}>Number of School Days in reporting month</Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.formulaRow}>
                <Text style={styles.formulaLabel}>c. Percentage of Attendance for the month =</Text>
                <View style={styles.fractionWrap}>
                  <Text style={styles.fractionNumerator}>Average daily attendance</Text>
                  <View style={styles.fractionBar} />
                  <Text style={styles.fractionDenominator}>Registered Learner as of End of the month</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.multiplyText}>x 100</Text>
              </View>

              <Text style={styles.guidelineP}>
                4. Every End of the month, the class adviser will submit this form to the office of the principal for recording of summary table into the School Form 4. Once signed by the principal, this form should be returned to the adviser.
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.guidelineP}>
                5. The adviser will extend necessary intervention including but not limited to home visitation to learner/s that committed 5 consecutive days of absences or those with potentials of dropping out.
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.guidelineP}>
                6. Attendance performance of learner is expected to reflect in Form 137 and Form 138 every grading period.
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.guidelineFooter}>
                Beginning of School Year cut-off report is every 1st Friday of School Calendar Days
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.codesAndReasonsCol}>
              <Text style={styles.blockHeading}>1. CODES FOR CHECKING ATTENDANCE</Text>
              <Text style={styles.codeText}>
                <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>blank </Text>- Present
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.codeText}>
                <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>(x) </Text>- Absent
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.codeText}>
                <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>Tardy </Text>(half shaded Upper for Late Comer, Lower for Cutting Classes)
              </Text>

              <View style={styles.legendVisualRow}>
                <View style={styles.legendBox}>
                  <View style={styles.triangleUpperShadedLegend} />
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.legendLabel}>Late Comer</Text>

                <View style={[styles.legendBox, { marginLeft: 12 }]}>
                  <View style={styles.triangleLowerShadedLegend} />
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.legendLabel}>Cutting Classes</Text>
              </View>

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\components\reports\SF4ReportView.tsx

import React from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  ScrollView,
} from 'react-native';
import { DepEdReportHeader, DepEdReportFooter } from './DepEdReportHeaderFooter';

export interface MetricTriplet {
  m: number;
  f: number;
  total: number;
}

export interface SF4SectionRecord {
  section_id: number | string;
  section_name: string;
  grade_level: string;
  adviser_name: string;
  registered_end: MetricTriplet;
  daily_average: MetricTriplet;
  attendance_percentage: MetricTriplet;
  dropped_out_prev: MetricTriplet;
  dropped_out_month: MetricTriplet;
  dropped_out_cumulative: MetricTriplet;
  transferred_out_prev: MetricTriplet;
  transferred_out_month: MetricTriplet;
  transferred_out_cumulative: MetricTriplet;
  transferred_in_prev: MetricTriplet;
  transferred_in_month: MetricTriplet;
  transferred_in_cumulative: MetricTriplet;
}

export interface SF4GradeGroup {
  grade_level: string;
  sections: SF4SectionRecord[];
  subtotal: SF4SectionRecord;
}

interface SF4ReportViewProps {
  data: any;
  gradeGroups: SF4GradeGroup[];
}

const parseGradeLevelAndSection = (gradeLevelInput: string, existingSectionName?: string) => {
  const cleanGrade = (gradeLevelInput || '').trim();
  const cleanSection = (existingSectionName || '').trim();

  if (cleanSection) {
    return { grade: cleanGrade, section: cleanSection };
  }

  if (cleanGrade.includes(' - ')) {
    const parts = cleanGrade.split(' - ');
    return {
      grade: parts[0].trim(),
      section: parts.slice(1).join(' - ').trim(),
    };
  }

  if (cleanGrade.includes('-') && !cleanGrade.toLowerCase().startsWith('non-')) {
    const parts = cleanGrade.split('-');
    if (parts.length === 2 && parts[0].toLowerCase().includes('grade')) {
      return {
        grade: parts[0].trim(),
        section: parts[1].trim(),
      };
    }
  }

  return { grade: cleanGrade, section: '' };
};

export const SF4ReportView: React.FC<SF4ReportViewProps> = ({ data, gradeGroups }) => {
  if (!data) return null;

  const leftLogoUrl = data.left_logo || null;
  const rightLogoUrl = data.right_logo || null;

  const safeGroups = Array.isArray(gradeGroups) ? gradeGroups : [];

  const renderMetricCells = (triplet?: MetricTriplet) => (
    <>
      <Text style={styles.tdCenter}>{triplet ? triplet.m : 0}</Text>
      <Text style={styles.tdCenter}>{triplet ? triplet.f : 0}</Text>
      <Text style={[styles.tdCenter, { fontWeight: '700' }]}>{triplet ? triplet.total : 0}</Text>
    </>
  );

  return (
    <View style={styles.sheetContainer}>
      <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={true}>
        <View style={styles.officialDocumentCanvas}>
          {/* ========================================================
              1. DYNAMIC DEPED DMOS HEADER (FROM DATABASE)
             ======================================================== */}
          <DepEdReportHeader
            leftLogoUrl={leftLogoUrl}
            rightLogoUrl={rightLogoUrl}
            region={data.region}
            division={data.division}
            formTitle="School Form 4 (SF 4) Monthly Learner's Movement and Attendance"
            formSubtitle="(Consolidated Learner Movement and Monthly Attendance Summary)"
          />

          {/* ========================================================
              2. METADATA GRID (FROM DATABASE)
             ======================================================== */}
          <View style={styles.metaGrid}>
            <View style={styles.metaRow}>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}><Text style={styles.metaKey}>School ID: </Text>{data.school_id || '—'}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}><Text style={styles.metaKey}>Region: </Text>{data.region || '—'}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}><Text style={styles.metaKey}>Division: </Text>{data.division || '—'}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}><Text style={styles.metaKey}>District: </Text>{data.district || '—'}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.metaRow}>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}><Text style={styles.metaKey}>School Name: </Text>{data.school_name || '—'}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}><Text style={styles.metaKey}>School Year: </Text>{data.school_year || data.academic_year || '—'}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}><Text style={styles.metaKey}>Report Month: </Text>{data.month || '—'} {data.year || ''}</Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* ========================================================
              3. 39-COLUMN SF4 MATRIX TABLE
             ======================================================== */}
          <View style={styles.table}>
            <View style={styles.thRow1}>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 95 }]}>NAME OF ADVISER</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 75 }]}>GRADE LEVEL</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 70 }]}>SECTION</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>REGISTERED{'\n'}(End of Month)</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>ATTENDANCE{'\n'}(Daily Average)</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>ATTENDANCE{'\n'}(Percentage)</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>DROPPED OUT{'\n'}(Prev Month)</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>DROPPED OUT{'\n'}(For Month)</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>DROPPED OUT{'\n'}(Cumulative)</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>TRANSFERRED OUT{'\n'}(Prev Month)</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>TRANSFERRED OUT{'\n'}(For Month)</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>TRANSFERRED OUT{'\n'}(Cumulative)</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>TRANSFERRED IN{'\n'}(Prev Month)</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>TRANSFERRED IN{'\n'}(For Month)</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: 90 }]}>TRANSFERRED IN{'\n'}(Cumulative)</Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.thRow2}>
              <View style={{ width: 95 + 75 + 70 }} />
              {Array.from({ length: 12 }).map((_, i) => (
                <View key={`mft-th-${i}`} style={styles.mftGroup}>
                  <Text style={styles.mftTh}>M</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.mftTh}>F</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.mftTh, { fontWeight: '800' }]}>T</Text>
                </View>
              ))}
            </View>

            <View style={styles.rowCategory}>
              <Text style={styles.categoryText}>ELEMENTARY/SECONDARY:</Text>
            </View>

            {safeGroups.map((group) => {
              const { grade: groupGrade, section: groupSection } = parseGradeLevelAndSection(group.grade_level);
              return (
                <React.Fragment key={`group-${group.grade_level}`}>
                  {group.sections && group.sections.length > 0 ? (
                    <>
                      {group.sections.map((sec) => {
                        const { grade: secGrade, section: secName } = parseGradeLevelAndSection(sec.grade_level, sec.section_name);

                            <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(sec.daily_average)}</View>
                            <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(sec.attendance_percentage)}</View>
                            <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(sec.dropped_out_prev)}</View>
                            <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(sec.dropped_out_month)}</View>
                            <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(sec.dropped_out_cumulative)}</View>
                            <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(sec.transferred_out_prev)}</View>
                            <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(sec.transferred_out_month)}</View>
                            <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(sec.transferred_out_cumulative)}</View>
                            <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(sec.transferred_in_prev)}</View>
                            <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(sec.transferred_in_month)}</View>
                            <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(sec.transferred_in_cumulative)}</View>
                          </View>
                        );
                      })}
                      <View style={styles.subtotalRow}>
                        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: 240, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 6, fontWeight: '800' }]}>
                          TOTAL FOR {groupGrade.toUpperCase()}
                        </Text>
                        <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(group.subtotal?.registered_end)}</View>
                        <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(group.subtotal?.daily_average)}</View>
                        <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(group.subtotal?.attendance_percentage)}</View>

                <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(data.non_graded_summary.dropped_out_month)}</View>
                <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(data.non_graded_summary.dropped_out_cumulative)}</View>
                <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(data.non_graded_summary.transferred_out_prev)}</View>
                <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(data.non_graded_summary.transferred_out_month)}</View>
                <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(data.non_graded_summary.transferred_out_cumulative)}</View>
                <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(data.non_graded_summary.transferred_in_prev)}</View>
                <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(data.non_graded_summary.transferred_in_month)}</View>
                <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(data.non_graded_summary.transferred_in_cumulative)}</View>

            {data.total_summary && (

                </Text>

                <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(data.total_summary.dropped_out_month)}</View>

                <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(data.total_summary.transferred_in_prev)}</View>
                <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(data.total_summary.transferred_in_month)}</View>
                <View style={styles.mftGroup}>{renderMetricCells(data.total_summary.transferred_in_cumulative)}</View>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>

          {/* ========================================================
              4. GUIDELINES & SIGNATURES
             ======================================================== */}
          <View style={styles.summaryBox}>
            <View style={styles.summaryInfoCol}>
              <Text style={styles.summaryTitle}>GUIDELINES:</Text>
              <Text style={styles.summaryText}>1. This form shall be accomplished every end of the month.</Text>

            <View style={styles.signCol}>

                <Text style={styles.signName}>{data.school_head || 'School Head'}</Text>
                <View style={styles.signLine} />
                <Text style={styles.signCaption}>Prepared and Submitted by: (School Head)</Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* ========================================================
              5. DYNAMIC DEPED DMOS FOOTER (FROM DATABASE)
             ======================================================== */}

          <DepEdReportFooter
            schoolLogoUrl={data.school_logo || data.right_logo || null}
            address={data.school_address || data.address}
            contactNumber={data.school_contact || data.contact_number}
            email={data.school_email || data.email}
          />
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
};

File: mobile\src\components\ServerConfigModal.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Modal, StyleSheet, Text, View, TextInput, Alert } from 'react-native';
import { getServerBaseUrl, setServerBaseUrl, resetServerBaseUrl } from '../services/configService';
import { Button } from './common/Button';
import { theme } from '../constants/theme';

interface ServerConfigModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function ServerConfigModal({ visible, onClose }: ServerConfigModalProps) {
  const [url, setUrl] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (visible) {
      getServerBaseUrl().then(setUrl);
    }
  }, [visible]);

  const handleSave = async () => {
    if (!url.startsWith('http://') && !url.startsWith('https://')) {
      Alert.alert('Invalid URL', 'Address must begin with http:// or https://');
      return;
    }
    await setServerBaseUrl(url);
    Alert.alert('Saved', 'Server address updated successfully.');
    onClose();
  };

  const handleReset = async () => {
    await resetServerBaseUrl();
    const defaultUrl = await getServerBaseUrl();
    setUrl(defaultUrl);
    Alert.alert('Reset', 'Restored default server address.');
  };

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="fade">
      <View style={styles.overlay}>
        <View style={styles.modalBox}>
          <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>Server Connection Settings</Text>
          <Text style={styles.modalDesc}>
            Configure the AttendSure backend endpoint dynamically for local development or remote campus deployment.
          </Text>

          <Text style={styles.label}>Host Base URL</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={url}
            onChangeText={setUrl}
            placeholder="http://10.16.115.49:8000"
            autoCapitalize="none"
            autoCorrect={false}
          />

          <View style={styles.buttonRow}>
            <Button title="Reset Default" variant="outline" onPress={handleReset} style={styles.btnFlex} />
            <Button title="Save & Apply" variant="primary" onPress={handleSave} style={styles.btnFlex} />
          </View>
          <Button title="Cancel" variant="secondary" onPress={onClose} style={styles.cancelBtn} />
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  overlay: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.6)',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    padding: theme.spacing.lg,
  },
  modalBox: {
    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
    borderRadius: theme.borderRadius.lg,
    padding: theme.spacing.lg,
  },
  modalTitle: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: '700',
    color: theme.colors.textPrimary,
  },
  modalDesc: {
    fontSize: 12,
    color: theme.colors.textSecondary,
    marginVertical: theme.spacing.xs,
    lineHeight: 18,
  },
  label: {
    fontSize: 12,
    fontWeight: '600',
    color: theme.colors.textPrimary,
    marginTop: theme.spacing.sm,
    marginBottom: 4,
  },
  input: {
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: theme.colors.borderStrong,
    borderRadius: theme.borderRadius.md,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 8,
    fontSize: 13,
    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceSubtle,
    marginBottom: theme.spacing.md,
  },
  buttonRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    gap: 8,
  },
  btnFlex: {
    flex: 1,
    height: 40,
  },
  cancelBtn: {
    marginTop: 8,
    height: 38,
  },
});

File: mobile\src\components\SplashScreen.tsx

/**
 * AttendSure - Mobile Splash Screen
 * Uses vector SVG logo for crisp, zero-latency rendering.
 * File: mobile/src/components/SplashScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  Animated,
  Platform,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Modal,
  TextInput,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import * as NativeSplashScreen from 'expo-splash-screen';
import { getServerBaseUrl, setServerBaseUrl } from '../services/configService';
import { AttendSureLogo } from '../branding/AttendSureLogo';

interface SplashScreenProps {
  loading?: boolean;
  minDurationMs?: number;
  onFinish?: () => void;
}

// Cybersecurity: Prevent script or unsafe protocol injection into server address
const isValidServerUrl = (url: string): boolean => {
  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return false;
  const clean = url.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (
    clean.startsWith('javascript:') ||
    clean.startsWith('data:') ||
    clean.startsWith('file:') ||
    clean.startsWith('vbscript:')
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  return clean.startsWith('http://') || clean.startsWith('https://');
};

export const SplashScreen: React.FC<SplashScreenProps> = ({
  loading = false,
  minDurationMs = 800,
  onFinish,
}) => {
  const [canExit, setCanExit] = useState(false);
  const [hidden, setHidden] = useState(false);

  const opacityAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;

  // Network Tools Modal State
  const [showToolsModal, setShowToolsModal] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [customServerUrl, setCustomServerUrl] = useState<string>('');
  const isHoldingSplashRef = useRef<boolean>(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
      NativeSplashScreen.hideAsync().catch(() => {});
    }

    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      setCanExit(true);
    }, minDurationMs);

    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [minDurationMs]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && canExit && !isHoldingSplashRef.current) {
      Animated.timing(opacityAnim, {
        toValue: 0,
        duration: 350,
        useNativeDriver: true,
      }).start(() => {
        setHidden(true);
        if (onFinish) onFinish();
      });
    }
  }, [loading, canExit, onFinish, opacityAnim]);

  const openTools = async () => {
    isHoldingSplashRef.current = true;
    const activeUrl = await getServerBaseUrl();
    setCustomServerUrl(activeUrl);
    setShowToolsModal(true);
  };

  const saveServerAddress = async () => {
    const cleanUrl = customServerUrl.trim();
    if (!isValidServerUrl(cleanUrl)) {
      Alert.alert(
        'Invalid Address',
        'Please enter a valid URL starting with http:// or https://'
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      await setServerBaseUrl(cleanUrl);
      Alert.alert('Saved', 'School server address updated successfully.');
      setShowToolsModal(false);
      isHoldingSplashRef.current = false;
      if (onFinish) onFinish();
    } catch (e: any) {
      Alert.alert('Invalid URL', e.message || 'Please check the server address format.');
    }
  };

  if (hidden) return null;

  return (
    <Animated.View style={[styles.container, { opacity: opacityAnim }]}>
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeContainer} edges={['top', 'bottom']}>
        <StatusBar style="dark" />

        {/* Top-Left Network Tools Button */}
        <View style={styles.topBar}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.toolsButton}
            onPress={openTools}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
            accessibilityLabel="Configure server endpoint"
          >
            <View style={styles.toolsIconHalo}>
              <View style={styles.toolsIconCircle}>
                <Ionicons name="settings-sharp" size={16} color="#ffffff" />
              </View>
            </View>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {/* Center Product Emblem: Uses AttendSure SVG Logo */}
        <View style={styles.centerSection}>
          <View style={styles.emblemContainer}>
            <AttendSureLogo size={56} />
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.brandTitle}>AttendSure</Text>
        </View>

        {/* Developer Corporate Signature at Bottom */}
        <View style={styles.footerSection}>
          <Text style={styles.signatureText}>System by TechBlazer</Text>
        </View>

        {/* Network & Server Tools Modal */}
        <Modal
          visible={showToolsModal}
          transparent
          animationType="fade"
          onRequestClose={() => {
            setShowToolsModal(false);
            isHoldingSplashRef.current = false;
            if (onFinish) onFinish();
          }}
        >
          <View style={styles.modalBackdrop}>
            <View style={styles.modalCard}>
              <View style={styles.modalHeaderRow}>
                <Ionicons name="server-outline" size={20} color="#004b87" />
                <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>Network &amp; Server Tools</Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={styles.modalSubtitle}>
                Configure local or cloud school server address
              </Text>

              <TextInput
                style={styles.modalInput}
                value={customServerUrl}
                onChangeText={setCustomServerUrl}
                placeholder="http://192.168.1.100:8000"
                placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
                autoCapitalize="none"
                autoCorrect={false}
              />

              <View style={styles.modalActions}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.modalCancelBtn}
                  onPress={() => {
                    setShowToolsModal(false);
                    isHoldingSplashRef.current = false;
                    if (onFinish) onFinish();
                  }}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.modalCancelText}>Cancel</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.modalSaveBtn}
                  onPress={saveServerAddress}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.modalSaveText}>Save &amp; Connect</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </Modal>
      </SafeAreaView>
    </Animated.View>
  );
};

// mobile/src/context/AuthContext.tsx

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { apiClient, AUTH_TOKEN_KEY } from '../api/client';

const USER_CACHE_KEY = 'attendsure_user_profile';

export interface AuthenticatedUser {
  id: number;
  username: string;
  first_name: string;
  last_name: string;
  middle_name?: string;
  suffix?: string;
  full_name: string;
  faculty_name: string;
  faculty_id: string;
  employee_id?: string;
  email: string;
  role: string;
  system_role: string;
  position?: string;
  department?: string | null;
  division?: string | null;
  contact_number?: string;
  phone?: string;
  photo?: string | null;
  avatar?: string | null;
  school_name?: string;
  qr_token?: string | null;
  bound_device_id?: string | null;
  is_superuser: boolean;
  is_staff: boolean;
}

interface DeviceIdentity {
  deviceId?: string;
}

interface AuthContextType {
  user: AuthenticatedUser | null;
  token: string | null;
  loading: boolean;
  isLoading: boolean;
  deviceIdentity: DeviceIdentity | null;
  login: (username: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshUser: () => Promise<void>;
  updateUser: (updatedFields: Partial<AuthenticatedUser>) => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

const saveTokenSafely = async (token: string): Promise<void> => {
  await AsyncStorage.setItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, token);
  if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
    try {
      await SecureStore.setItemAsync(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, token);
    } catch {
      // Ignore keychain write error
    }
  }
};

const clearTokenSafely = async (): Promise<void> => {
  await AsyncStorage.multiRemove([AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, USER_CACHE_KEY]);
  if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
    try {
      await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
    } catch {
      // Ignore keychain delete error
    }
  }
};

export const AuthProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<AuthenticatedUser | null>(null);
  const [token, setToken] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [deviceIdentity] = useState<DeviceIdentity | null>({ deviceId: 'WEB-CLIENT-UUID' });
  const isRefreshingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);

  const refreshUser = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isRefreshingRef.current) return;
    isRefreshingRef.current = true;

    try {
      const storedToken = await AsyncStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
      if (!storedToken) {
        setUser(null);
        setToken(null);
        return;
      }

      const res = await apiClient.get('/api/me/');
      if (res.data && res.data.id) {
        setUser(res.data);
        setToken(storedToken);
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(USER_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.data));
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      const status = err?.response?.status;
      if (status === 401 || status === 403) {
        await clearTokenSafely();
        setUser(null);
        setToken(null);
      }
    } finally {
      isRefreshingRef.current = false;
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const restoreSession = async () => {
      try {
        const storedToken = await AsyncStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
        const cachedUserStr = await AsyncStorage.getItem(USER_CACHE_KEY);

        if (storedToken && cachedUserStr) {
          setToken(storedToken);
          setUser(JSON.parse(cachedUserStr));
        }

        if (storedToken) {
          await refreshUser();
        }
      } catch {
        await clearTokenSafely();
        setUser(null);
        setToken(null);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    restoreSession();
  }, [refreshUser]);

const login = async (username: string, password: string): Promise<void> => {
    const cleanUsername = username.trim();
    const cleanPassword = password.trim();

    if (!cleanUsername || !cleanPassword) {
      throw new Error('Username and password are required.');
    }

    const res = await apiClient.post('/api/auth/login/', {
      username: cleanUsername,
      password: cleanPassword,
    });

    const receivedToken: string | undefined = res.data?.token;
    const receivedUser: AuthenticatedUser | undefined = res.data?.user;

    if (!receivedToken || !receivedUser) {
      throw new Error('Invalid response received from authentication server.');
    }

    await saveTokenSafely(receivedToken);
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(USER_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(receivedUser));

    setToken(receivedToken);
    setUser(receivedUser);

    // Refresh profile in background without blocking successful login
    refreshUser().catch(() => {});
  };

  const logout = async (): Promise<void> => {
    await clearTokenSafely();
    setToken(null);
    setUser(null);
  };

  const updateUser = async (updatedFields: Partial<AuthenticatedUser>): Promise<void> => {
    if (!user) return;
    const nextUser = { ...user, ...updatedFields };
    setUser(nextUser);
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(USER_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(nextUser));
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        token,
        loading,
        isLoading: loading,
        deviceIdentity,
        login,
        logout,
        refreshUser,
        updateUser,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = (): AuthContextType => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\navigation\AppNavigator.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { View, ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { createNativeStackNavigator } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';

import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import LoginScreen from '../screens/LoginScreen';
import MainTabNavigator from './MainTabNavigator';
import ProfileSettingsScreen from '../screens/ProfileSettingsScreen';
import ReportsScreen from '../screens/ReportsScreen';
import { COLORS } from '../constants/theme';

export type RootStackParamList = {
  Login: undefined;
  Main: undefined;
  ProfileSettings: undefined;
  Reports: undefined;
};

const Stack = createNativeStackNavigator<RootStackParamList>();

export const AppNavigator: React.FC = () => {
  const auth = useAuth() as any;
  const user = auth?.user;
  const isCheckingSession = Boolean(auth?.isLoading ?? auth?.loading);

  if (isCheckingSession) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.loadingContainer}>
        <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={COLORS.primary} />
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Stack.Navigator screenOptions={{ headerShown: false }}>
      {!user ? (
        <Stack.Screen name="Login" component={LoginScreen} />
      ) : (
        <>
          <Stack.Screen name="Main" component={MainTabNavigator} />
          <Stack.Screen
            name="ProfileSettings"
            component={ProfileSettingsScreen}
            options={{
              headerShown: false,
              animation: 'slide_from_right',
            }}
          />
          <Stack.Screen
            name="Reports"
            component={ReportsScreen}
            options={{
              headerShown: false,
              animation: 'slide_from_right',
            }}
          />
        </>
      )}
    </Stack.Navigator>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  loadingContainer: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
});

export default AppNavigator;

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\navigation\MainTabNavigator.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Text,
  Platform,
  ScrollView,
  RefreshControl,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { COLORS } from '../constants/theme';

// Standalone Screens
import DashboardScreen from '../screens/DashboardScreen';
import CalendarScreen from '../screens/CalendarScreen';
import ScannerScreen from '../screens/ScannerScreen';
import ReportsScreen from '../screens/ReportsScreen';
import MenuScreen from '../screens/MenuScreen';
import SettingsScreen from '../screens/SettingsScreen';
import SmsLogsScreen from '../screens/SmsLogsScreen';
import GatePassScreen from '../screens/GatePassScreen';

// Reports
import DTRReportView, { DTRReportData } from '../components/reports/DTRReportView';

export type TabType =
  | 'home'
  | 'gate_pass'
  | 'scanner'
  | 'reports'
  | 'calendar'
  | 'menu'
  | 'settings'
  | 'sms'
  | 'class_list'
  | 'dtr'
  | 'dept_monitor'
  | 'gate_feed'
  | 'device_approval'
  | 'kiosk_health';

export const MainTabNavigator: React.FC = () => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabType>('home');

  // Direct DTR State
  const [dtrMonth, setDtrMonth] = useState<number>(new Date().getMonth() + 1);
  const [dtrYear, setDtrYear] = useState<number>(new Date().getFullYear());
  const [dtrData, setDtrData] = useState<DTRReportData | null>(null);
  const [dtrLoading, setDtrLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [dtrSubmitting, setDtrSubmitting] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const isMounted = useRef<boolean>(true);

  const fetchDTR = useCallback(async (silent = false) => {
    if (!silent) setDtrLoading(true);
    const cacheKey = `attendsure_dtr_menu_${dtrMonth}_${dtrYear}`;

    try {
      const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(cacheKey);
      if (cached && isMounted.current) {
        setDtrData(JSON.parse(cached));
      }
    } catch {}

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<DTRReportData>('reports/dtr/', {
        params: { month: dtrMonth, year: dtrYear },
      });
      if (isMounted.current) {
        setDtrData(res.data);
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(res.data));
      }
    } catch {
      // Retain cached state
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current && !silent) {
        setDtrLoading(false);
      }
    }
  }, [dtrMonth, dtrYear]);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMounted.current = true;
    if (activeTab === 'dtr') {
      fetchDTR(false);
    }
    return () => {
      isMounted.current = false;
    };
  }, [activeTab, fetchDTR]);

  const handleSubmitDtr = async () => {
    if (!dtrData) return;
    setDtrSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await apiClient.post(`reports/dtr/${dtrData.faculty_id}/`, {
        action: 'SUBMIT_DTR',
        faculty_id: dtrData.faculty_id,
        month: dtrData.month_number,
        year: dtrData.year,
        regular_days_hours: dtrData.regular_days_hours,
        saturdays_hours: dtrData.saturdays_hours,
      });
      Alert.alert('Success', 'DTR submitted to your Department Head.');
      fetchDTR(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      Alert.alert('Error', err?.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to submit DTR.');
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setDtrSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  // Reusable sub-header with Back Arrow Button for all Menu tabs
  const renderSubHeader = (title: string, onBackAction?: () => void) => (
    <View style={[styles.subScreenHeader, { paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top, 12) }]}>
      <TouchableOpacity
        style={styles.backBtn}
        onPress={onBackAction || (() => setActiveTab('menu'))}
        activeOpacity={0.7}
        hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
      >
        <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={22} color="#004b87" />
        <Text style={styles.backBtnText}>Menu</Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>
      <Text style={styles.subScreenTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
        {title}
      </Text>
      <View style={{ width: 68 }} />
    </View>
  );

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <View style={styles.screenContainer}>
        {/* Core Screen: Dashboard */}
        {activeTab === 'home' && <DashboardScreen />}

        {/* Menu Tab 1: Gate Passes & Exit Clearance */}
        {activeTab === 'gate_pass' && (
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <GatePassScreen onBack={() => setActiveTab('menu')} />
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Core Screen: Scanner */}
        {activeTab === 'scanner' && <ScannerScreen />}

        {/* Core Screen: Reports */}
        {activeTab === 'reports' && <ReportsScreen />}

        {/* Menu Screen */}
        {activeTab === 'menu' && <MenuScreen onSelectScreen={setActiveTab} />}

        {/* Menu Tab 2: Academic Calendar */}
        {activeTab === 'calendar' && (
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            {renderSubHeader('Academic Calendar')}
            <CalendarScreen />
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Menu Tab 3: Profile Settings */}
        {activeTab === 'settings' && (
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            {renderSubHeader('Profile Settings')}
            <SettingsScreen />
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Menu Tab 4: Civil Service Form No. 48 (DTR) */}
        {activeTab === 'dtr' && (
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            {renderSubHeader('Civil Service Form No. 48')}
            <ScrollView
              contentContainerStyle={styles.subScreenScroll}
              showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
              refreshControl={
                <RefreshControl
                  refreshing={dtrLoading}
                  onRefresh={() => fetchDTR(false)}
                  colors={[COLORS.primary]}
                />
              }
            >
              {dtrLoading && !dtrData ? (
                <View style={styles.loadingBox}>
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={COLORS.primary} />
                  <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Loading DTR records...</Text>
                </View>
              ) : (
                <DTRReportView
                  data={dtrData}
                  selectedMonth={dtrMonth}
                  selectedYear={dtrYear}
                  onMonthChange={(m, y) => {
                    setDtrMonth(m);
                    setDtrYear(y);
                  }}
                  submitting={dtrSubmitting}
                  onSubmit={handleSubmitDtr}
                  onRefreshNeeded={() => fetchDTR(true)}
                />
              )}
            </ScrollView>
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Menu Tab 5: SMS Delivery Logs */}
        {activeTab === 'sms' && (
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            {renderSubHeader('SMS Delivery Logs')}
            <SmsLogsScreen />
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Menu Tab 6: Class List */}
        {activeTab === 'class_list' && (
          <View style={styles.placeholderContainer}>
            {renderSubHeader('Class List')}
            <View style={styles.placeholderBody}>
              <Ionicons name="people-circle-outline" size={64} color="#004b87" />
              <Text style={styles.placeholderTitle}>Class List</Text>
              <Text style={styles.placeholderText}>
                Active learners, guardian contacts, and classroom attendance.
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Menu Tab 7: Department Employee Attendance */}
        {activeTab === 'dept_monitor' && (
          <View style={styles.placeholderContainer}>
            {renderSubHeader('Employee Attendance')}
            <View style={styles.placeholderBody}>
              <Ionicons name="analytics-outline" size={64} color="#004b87" />
              <Text style={styles.placeholderTitle}>Employee Attendance</Text>
              <Text style={styles.placeholderText}>
                Department employee attendance inside campus geofence.
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Menu Tab 8: Gate Scanner Feed */}
        {activeTab === 'gate_feed' && (
          <View style={styles.placeholderContainer}>
            {renderSubHeader('Gate Scanner Feed')}
            <View style={styles.placeholderBody}>
              <Ionicons name="videocam-outline" size={64} color="#004b87" />
              <Text style={styles.placeholderTitle}>Gate Scanner Feed</Text>
              <Text style={styles.placeholderText}>
                Live RFID and QR taps at Campus Gate Kiosks.
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Menu Tab 9: Device Approvals */}
        {activeTab === 'device_approval' && (
          <View style={styles.placeholderContainer}>
            {renderSubHeader('Device Approvals')}
            <View style={styles.placeholderBody}>
              <Ionicons name="shield-checkmark-outline" size={64} color="#004b87" />
              <Text style={styles.placeholderTitle}>Device Approvals</Text>
              <Text style={styles.placeholderText}>
                Manage verified employee mobile devices.
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Menu Tab 10: Gate Kiosk Health */}
        {activeTab === 'kiosk_health' && (
          <View style={styles.placeholderContainer}>
            {renderSubHeader('Gate Kiosk Health')}
            <View style={styles.placeholderBody}>
              <Ionicons name="hardware-chip-outline" size={64} color="#004b87" />
              <Text style={styles.placeholderTitle}>Gate Kiosk Health</Text>
              <Text style={styles.placeholderText}>
                Scanner online status, ping latency, and offline queue logs.
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Bottom Navigation Bar */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.bottomBar,
          {
            paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 8),
            height: 60 + Math.max(insets.bottom, 8),
          },
        ]}
      >
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.tabItem}
          onPress={() => setActiveTab('home')}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
        >
          <Ionicons
            name={activeTab === 'home' ? 'grid' : 'grid-outline'}
            size={20}
            color={activeTab === 'home' ? COLORS.primary : COLORS.textMuted}
          />
          <Text
            style={[styles.tabLabel, activeTab === 'home' && styles.tabLabelActive]}
            numberOfLines={1}
          >
            Dashboard
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.tabItem}
          onPress={() => setActiveTab('gate_pass')}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
        >
          <Ionicons
            name={activeTab === 'gate_pass' ? 'ticket' : 'ticket-outline'}
            size={20}
            color={activeTab === 'gate_pass' ? COLORS.primary : COLORS.textMuted}
          />
          <Text
            style={[styles.tabLabel, activeTab === 'gate_pass' && styles.tabLabelActive]}
            numberOfLines={1}
          >
            Gate Pass
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.centerFab}
          onPress={() => setActiveTab('scanner')}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
        >
          <View style={[styles.fabBox, activeTab === 'scanner' && styles.fabBoxActive]}>
            <Ionicons name="qr-code" size={24} color="#ffffff" />
          </View>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.tabItem}
          onPress={() => setActiveTab('reports')}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
        >
          <Ionicons
            name={activeTab === 'reports' ? 'document-text' : 'document-text-outline'}
            size={20}
            color={activeTab === 'reports' ? COLORS.primary : COLORS.textMuted}
          />
          <Text
            style={[styles.tabLabel, activeTab === 'reports' && styles.tabLabelActive]}
            numberOfLines={1}
          >
            Reports
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.tabItem}
          onPress={() => setActiveTab('menu')}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
        >
          <Ionicons
            name={
              [
                'menu',
                'settings',
                'sms',
                'calendar',
                'class_list',
                'dtr',
                'dept_monitor',
                'gate_feed',
                'device_approval',
                'kiosk_health',
              ].includes(activeTab)
                ? 'menu'
                : 'menu-outline'
            }
            size={22}
            color={
              [
                'menu',
                'settings',
                'sms',
                'calendar',
                'class_list',
                'dtr',
                'dept_monitor',
                'gate_feed',
                'device_approval',
                'kiosk_health',
              ].includes(activeTab)
                ? COLORS.primary
                : COLORS.textMuted
            }
          />
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.tabLabel,
              [
                'menu',
                'settings',
                'sms',
                'calendar',
                'class_list',
                'dtr',
                'dept_monitor',
                'gate_feed',
                'device_approval',
                'kiosk_health',
              ].includes(activeTab) && styles.tabLabelActive,
            ]}
            numberOfLines={1}
          >
            Menu
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

/**
 * AttendSure V3 - Mobile Advisory Subject Attendance Monitor
 * File: mobile/src/screens/AdvisoryMatrixScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  FlatList,
  TouchableOpacity,
  TextInput,
  ActivityIndicator,
  RefreshControl,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { COLORS } from '../constants/theme';

interface SubjectStatus {
  schedule_id: number;
  subject_code: string;
  status: 'PRESENT' | 'LATE' | 'CUTTING' | 'UNVERIFIED' | 'UNSCANNED';
  time: string;
}

interface AdviseeRow {
  number: number;
  student_id: number;
  student_name: string;
  lrn: string;
  photo_url?: string | null;
  gate_status: string;
  gate_direction: 'IN' | 'OUT' | 'NONE';
  attended_count: number;
  cutting_count: number;
  total_subjects: number;
  compliance_rate: number;
  subjects: SubjectStatus[];
}

export const AdvisoryMatrixScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [filterMode, setFilterMode] = useState<'ALL' | 'CUTTING'>('ALL');

  const [hasAdvisoryClass, setHasAdvisoryClass] = useState(false);
  const [sectionName, setSectionName] = useState('');
  const [gradeLevel, setGradeLevel] = useState('');
  const [matrix, setMatrix] = useState<AdviseeRow[]>([]);
  const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const fetchMatrixData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get('classroom/adviser-matrix/');
      if (res.data?.has_advisory_class) {
        setHasAdvisoryClass(true);
        setSectionName(res.data.section_name || '');
        setGradeLevel(res.data.grade_level || '');
        setMatrix(res.data.matrix || []);
      } else {
        setHasAdvisoryClass(false);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load mobile advisory matrix:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchMatrixData();
  }, [fetchMatrixData]);

  const onRefresh = () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    fetchMatrixData();
  };

  const filteredMatrix = useMemo(() => {
    return matrix.filter((row) => {
      const matchSearch =
        row.student_name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
        row.lrn.includes(search);

      if (!matchSearch) return false;
      if (filterMode === 'CUTTING') return row.cutting_count > 0;
      return true;
    });
  }, [matrix, search, filterMode]);

  const summary = useMemo(() => {
    const total = matrix.length;
    const cutting = matrix.filter((m) => m.cutting_count > 0).length;
    return { total, cutting };
  }, [matrix]);

  const getInitials = (name: string) => {
    const parts = name.replace(/,/g, '').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    if (parts.length >= 2) return `${parts[0][0]}${parts[1][0]}`.toUpperCase();
    return name.slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
  };

  const renderSubjectBadge = (item: SubjectStatus) => {
    let bg = '#f1f5f9';
    let textCol = '#64748b';

    if (item.status === 'PRESENT') {
      bg = '#ecfdf5';
      textCol = '#059669';
    } else if (item.status === 'LATE') {
      bg = '#fffbeb';
      textCol = '#d97706';
    } else if (item.status === 'CUTTING') {
      bg = '#fef2f2';
      textCol = '#dc2626';
    }

    return (
      <View key={item.schedule_id} style={[styles.subPill, { backgroundColor: bg }]}>
        <Text style={[styles.subCode, { color: textCol }]}>{item.subject_code}</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.subStatus, { color: textCol }]}>
          {item.status === 'UNSCANNED' ? '—' : item.status}
        </Text>
      </View>
    );
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.centerBox}>
        <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={COLORS.primary} />
        <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Loading advisory records...</Text>
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (!hasAdvisoryClass) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.emptyStateBox}>
          <Ionicons name="information-circle-outline" size={48} color="#94a3b8" />
          <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>No Advisory Section</Text>
          <Text style={styles.emptySub}>
            Your faculty account is not registered as an official Class Adviser.
          </Text>
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top']}>
      {/* Header Strip */}
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <View>
          <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Advisory Subject Monitor</Text>
          <Text style={styles.headerSubtitle}>
            {gradeLevel} - {sectionName} &bull; {summary.total} Advisees
          </Text>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.filterRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.filterChip, filterMode === 'ALL' && styles.filterChipActive]}
            onPress={() => setFilterMode('ALL')}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.filterText, filterMode === 'ALL' && styles.filterTextActive]}>
              All ({summary.total})
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.filterChip, filterMode === 'CUTTING' && styles.filterChipDangerActive]}
            onPress={() => setFilterMode('CUTTING')}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.filterText, filterMode === 'CUTTING' && styles.filterTextDangerActive]}>
              Cutting ({summary.cutting})
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Search Field */}
      <View style={styles.searchContainer}>
        <Ionicons name="search" size={16} color="#94a3b8" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
        <TextInput
          style={styles.searchInput}
          placeholder="Search advisee name or LRN..."
          value={search}
          onChangeText={setSearch}
        />
        {search.length > 0 && (
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSearch('')}>
            <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={18} color="#94a3b8" />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Advisee List */}
      <FlatList
        data={filteredMatrix}
        keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.student_id)}
        contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 12, paddingBottom: 28 }}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} />}
        renderItem={({ item }) => {
          const isExpanded = expandedId === item.student_id;
          const isCutting = item.cutting_count > 0;

          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.card, isCutting && styles.cardCutting]}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
              onPress={() => setExpandedId(isExpanded ? null : item.student_id)}
            >
              <View style={styles.cardHeader}>
                {/* Number Badge */}
                <View style={styles.numberBadge}>
                  <Text style={styles.numberText}>{item.number}</Text>
                </View>

                {/* Profile Photo */}
                <View style={styles.avatarWrap}>
                  {item.photo_url ? (
                    <Image source={{ uri: item.photo_url }} style={styles.avatarImg} />
                  ) : (
                    <Text style={styles.initialsText}>{getInitials(item.student_name)}</Text>
                  )}
                </View>

                {/* Name & LRN */}
                <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 8 }}>
                  <Text style={styles.nameText} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {item.student_name}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.lrnText}>LRN: {item.lrn}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.gateText}>
                    Gate: <Text style={{ fontWeight: '700', color: item.gate_direction === 'IN' ? '#059669' : '#dc2626' }}>{item.gate_status}</Text>
                  </Text>
                </View>

                {/* Compliance Badge */}
                <View style={styles.complianceBadge}>
                  <Text style={[styles.complianceText, { color: item.compliance_rate >= 80 ? '#059669' : '#dc2626' }]}>
                    {item.attended_count}/{item.total_subjects}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.complianceSub}>{item.compliance_rate}%</Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              {/* Subject Badges Summary */}
              <View style={styles.subjectsStrip}>
                {item.subjects.map((sub) => renderSubjectBadge(sub))}
              </View>

              {/* Expand Hint */}
              {isCutting && (
                <View style={styles.warningStrip}>
                  <Ionicons name="warning" size={13} color="#dc2626" />
                  <Text style={styles.warningText}>
                    Missing {item.cutting_count} subject period(s) today
                  </Text>
                </View>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        }}
        ListEmptyComponent={
          <View style={styles.emptyListBox}>
            <Text style={styles.emptyListText}>No advisee records match the current filter.</Text>
          </View>
        }
      />
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#f8fafc' },
  centerBox: { flex: 1, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center' },
  loadingText: { marginTop: 8, fontSize: 13, color: '#64748b' },
  header: {
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingVertical: 12,
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderBottomColor: '#e2e8f0',
  },
  headerTitle: { fontSize: 17, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a' },
