  emptyStateText: {
    fontSize: 11,
    color: '#64748b',
    textAlign: 'center',
    marginTop: 2,
    lineHeight: 15,
  },
});

export default DashboardScreen;

========================================================
File: mobile\src\screens\DTRScreen.tsx
========================================================
// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\screens\DTRScreen.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  ScrollView,
  RefreshControl,
  ActivityIndicator,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Alert,
  Share,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { COLORS } from '../constants/theme';
import DTRReportView, { DTRReportData } from '../components/reports/DTRReportView';

const MONTH_NAMES = [
  'January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June',
  'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December',
];

export const DTRScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const isMounted = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  const currentUser = user as Record<string, any> | null;
  const userRole = String(currentUser?.role || currentUser?.system_role || 'FACULTY').trim().toUpperCase();
  const isFaculty = userRole === 'FACULTY';

  const now = new Date();
  const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] = useState<number>(now.getMonth() + 1);
  const [selectedYear, setSelectedYear] = useState<number>(now.getFullYear());
  const [selectedFacultyId, setSelectedFacultyId] = useState<string>('');

  const [dtrData, setDtrData] = useState<DTRReportData | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [submittingDtr, setSubmittingDtr] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [exportingPdf, setExportingPdf] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Fetch DTR
  const fetchDTR = useCallback(
    async (silent = false) => {
      if (!silent && abortControllerRef.current) {
        abortControllerRef.current.abort();
      }
      const controller = new AbortController();
      if (!silent) abortControllerRef.current = controller;

      const endpoint =
        isFaculty && !selectedFacultyId
          ? '/reports/dtr/'
          : selectedFacultyId
          ? `/reports/dtr/${selectedFacultyId}/`
          : '/reports/dtr/';

      const cacheKey = `attendsure_dtr_${selectedFacultyId || 'self'}_${selectedMonth}_${selectedYear}`;

      if (!silent) {
        setLoading(true);
        try {
          const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(cacheKey);
          if (cached && isMounted.current) {
            setDtrData(JSON.parse(cached));
          }
        } catch {
          // Continue to live fetch
        }
      }

      try {
        const res = await apiClient.get<DTRReportData>(endpoint, {
          params: { month: selectedMonth, year: selectedYear },
          signal: controller.signal,
        });

        if (!isMounted.current) return;

        setDtrData(res.data);
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(res.data));
      } catch {
        // Keep cached state
      } finally {
        if (!silent && isMounted.current) {
          setLoading(false);
          setRefreshing(false);
        }
      }
    },
    [isFaculty, selectedFacultyId, selectedMonth, selectedYear]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    isMounted.current = true;
    fetchDTR(false);

    return () => {
      isMounted.current = false;
      if (abortControllerRef.current) abortControllerRef.current.abort();
    };
  }, [fetchDTR]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(() => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    fetchDTR(false);
  }, [fetchDTR]);

  // Certify and Submit DTR
  const handleSubmitDtr = async () => {
    if (!dtrData) return;

    setSubmittingDtr(true);
    try {
      await apiClient.post(`/reports/dtr/${dtrData.faculty_id}/`, {
        action: 'SUBMIT_DTR',
        faculty_id: dtrData.faculty_id,
        month: dtrData.month_number,
        year: dtrData.year,
        regular_days_hours: dtrData.regular_days_hours,
        saturdays_hours: dtrData.saturdays_hours,
      });
      Alert.alert('Success', 'DTR submitted to your Department Head.');
      fetchDTR(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      Alert.alert('Error', err?.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to submit DTR.');
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setSubmittingDtr(false);
    }
  };

  // Export / Share
  const handleExport = async () => {
    if (!dtrData) return;

    if (!dtrData.dept_head_approved) {
      Alert.alert('Notice', 'Department Head endorsement required before exporting.');
      return;
    }

    setExportingPdf(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get('/reports/export/', {
        params: {
          report_type: 'DTR',
          faculty_id: dtrData.faculty_id,
          month: dtrData.month_number,
          year: dtrData.year,
        },
      });

      const title = res.data?.title || `CSC_Form_48_${dtrData.faculty_name}_${dtrData.month}_${dtrData.year}`;
      await Share.share({
        title,
        message: `${title}\nCivil Service Form No. 48 certified via AttendSure.`,
      });
    } catch {
      Alert.alert('Notice', 'Could not export DTR.');
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setExportingPdf(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeContainer} edges={['top', 'left', 'right']}>
      {/* Top Header */}
      <View style={styles.topBar}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <Text style={styles.screenTitle}>DTR</Text>
          <Text style={styles.screenSub}>Civil Service Form No. 48</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6 }}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.btnSec, (loading || exportingPdf) && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
            onPress={handleExport}
            disabled={loading || exportingPdf || !dtrData?.dept_head_approved}
          >
            <Ionicons
              name={dtrData?.dept_head_approved ? 'share-outline' : 'lock-closed-outline'}
              size={15}
              color={dtrData?.dept_head_approved ? '#004b87' : '#94a3b8'}
            />
            <Text style={[styles.btnSecText, !dtrData?.dept_head_approved && { color: '#94a3b8' }]}>
              Export
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.btnPri, loading && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
            onPress={() => fetchDTR(false)}
            disabled={loading}
          >
            {loading ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
            ) : (
              <Ionicons name="refresh" size={15} color="#ffffff" />
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} colors={[COLORS.primary]} />
        }
      >
        {/* Month Selector */}
        <View style={styles.monthPillRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.navArrow}
            onPress={() => {
              if (selectedMonth === 1) {
                setSelectedMonth(12);
                setSelectedYear((y) => y - 1);
              } else {
                setSelectedMonth((m) => m - 1);
              }
            }}
          >
            <Ionicons name="chevron-back" size={16} color="#004b87" />
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <Text style={styles.monthDisplay}>
            {MONTH_NAMES[selectedMonth - 1]} {selectedYear}
          </Text>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.navArrow}
            onPress={() => {
              if (selectedMonth === 12) {
                setSelectedMonth(1);
                setSelectedYear((y) => y + 1);
              } else {
                setSelectedMonth((m) => m + 1);
              }
            }}
          >
            <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={16} color="#004b87" />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {/* DTR Component */}
        {loading && !dtrData ? (
          <View style={styles.loadingBox}>
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={COLORS.primary} />
            <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Loading records...</Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <DTRReportView
            data={dtrData}
            submitting={submittingDtr}
            onSubmit={handleSubmitDtr}
            onRefreshNeeded={() => fetchDTR(true)}
          />
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safeContainer: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9' },
  topBar: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderBottomColor: '#cbd5e1',
  },
  screenTitle: { fontSize: 17, fontWeight: '800', color: '#003460' },
  screenSub: { fontSize: 10, color: '#64748b' },
  btnPri: {
    backgroundColor: '#004b87',
    paddingVertical: 6,
    paddingHorizontal: 10,
    borderRadius: 8,
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  btnSec: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 4,
    backgroundColor: '#e6f0fa',
    paddingVertical: 6,
    paddingHorizontal: 10,
    borderRadius: 8,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#b8d6f0',
  },
  btnSecText: { color: '#004b87', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' },
  scrollContent: { padding: 12, paddingBottom: 110, gap: 10 },
  monthPillRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderRadius: 8,
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#cbd5e1',
  },
  navArrow: { padding: 4 },
  monthDisplay: { fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a' },
  loadingBox: { alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', paddingVertical: 40 },
  loadingText: { fontSize: 11, color: '#64748b', marginTop: 6 },
});

export default DTRScreen;

========================================================
File: mobile\src\screens\GatePassScreen.tsx
========================================================
/**
 * AttendSure V3 - Mobile Gate Pass & Clearance Center
 * File: mobile/src/screens/GatePassScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  FlatList,
  TouchableOpacity,
  RefreshControl,
  Modal,
  TextInput,
  Alert,
  ActivityIndicator,
  ScrollView,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { COLORS } from '../constants/theme';

export interface GatePassItem {
  id: number;
  pass_number: string;
  bearer_type: 'STUDENT' | 'FACULTY';
  student?: number | null;
  student_name?: string;
  student_lrn?: string;
  faculty?: number | null;
  faculty_name?: string;
  faculty_emp_id?: string;
  pass_type: string;
  reason: string;
  valid_from: string;
  valid_to: string;
  status: 'PENDING' | 'ACTIVE' | 'USED' | 'DECLINED' | 'EXPIRED';
  attachment_url?: string | null;
  adviser_name?: string;
}

interface GatePassScreenProps {
  onBack?: () => void;
}

export const GatePassScreen: React.FC<GatePassScreenProps> = ({ onBack }) => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const userRole = String(user?.role || user?.system_role || 'EMPLOYEE').toUpperCase();
  const isPrincipalOrAdmin = ['ADMIN', 'SUPERADMIN', 'PRINCIPAL'].includes(userRole);
  const isGuard = ['GUARD', 'SECURITY'].includes(userRole);

  const [passes, setPasses] = useState<GatePassItem[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>('ACTIVE');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  const [selectedPass, setSelectedPass] = useState<GatePassItem | null>(null);
  const [showIssueModal, setShowIssueModal] = useState(false);
  const [passTarget, setPassTarget] = useState<'STUDENT' | 'FACULTY'>('STUDENT');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const [reviewingPass, setReviewingPass] = useState<GatePassItem | null>(null);

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    student_id: '',
    pass_type: 'Medical / Health Emergency',
    reason: '',
    duration_hours: '4',
  });

  const fetchPasses = useCallback(async (silent = false) => {
    if (!silent) setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<any>('gate-passes/');
      const list = res.data?.results ?? (Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : []);
      setPasses(list);
    } catch {
      if (!silent) Alert.alert('Network Notice', 'Could not load gate passes.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchPasses();
  }, [fetchPasses]);

  const onRefresh = () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    fetchPasses(true);
  };

  const filteredPasses = useMemo(() => {
    return passes.filter((p) => {
      const matchesStatus = statusFilter === 'ALL' || p.status === statusFilter;
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
      const passNum = (p.pass_number || '').toLowerCase();
      const bearer = (p.student_name || p.faculty_name || '').toLowerCase();
      const reason = (p.reason || '').toLowerCase();

      const matchesSearch = !q || passNum.includes(q) || bearer.includes(q) || reason.includes(q);
      return matchesStatus && matchesSearch;
    });
  }, [passes, statusFilter, searchQuery]);

  const handleIssueSubmit = async () => {
    if (passTarget === 'STUDENT' && !form.student_id.trim()) {
      Alert.alert('Required', 'Please enter the Student ID.');
      return;
    }

    if (!form.reason.trim()) {
      Alert.alert('Required', 'Please state a valid reason or destination.');
      return;
    }

    const hours = Math.max(1, Math.min(12, Number(form.duration_hours) || 4));
    const now = new Date();
    const expiry = new Date(now.getTime() + hours * 3600000);

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const payload: Record<string, any> = {
        bearer_type: passTarget,
        pass_type: form.pass_type,
        reason: form.reason.trim(),
        valid_from: now.toISOString(),
        valid_to: expiry.toISOString(),
      };

      if (passTarget === 'STUDENT') {
        payload.student = Number(form.student_id);
      }

      await apiClient.post('gate-passes/', payload);
      Alert.alert(
        'Success',
        passTarget === 'FACULTY'
          ? 'Your official travel pass was submitted to the Principal/Admin for document review.'
          : 'Student gate exit permit issued. School administration notified.'
      );

      setShowIssueModal(false);
      setForm({
        student_id: '',
        pass_type: 'Medical / Health Emergency',
        reason: '',
        duration_hours: '4',
      });
      fetchPasses(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      Alert.alert('Submission Error', err?.response?.data?.student?.[0] || 'Unable to submit gate pass.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleApprovePass = async (pass: GatePassItem) => {
    try {
      await apiClient.post(`gate-passes/${pass.id}/approve/`);
      Alert.alert('Approved', 'Travel pass approved and active for gate exit.');
      setReviewingPass(null);
      fetchPasses(true);
    } catch {
      Alert.alert('Error', 'Failed to approve pass.');
    }
  };

  const handleDeclinePass = async (pass: GatePassItem) => {
    Alert.alert('Decline Pass', 'Decline this travel pass due to an invalid or unauthentic document?', [
      { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
      {
        text: 'Confirm Decline',
        style: 'destructive',
        onPress: async () => {
          try {
            await apiClient.post(`gate-passes/${pass.id}/decline/`, { reason: 'Declined upon document authenticity review.' });
            Alert.alert('Declined', 'Gate pass marked as DECLINED.');
            setReviewingPass(null);
            fetchPasses(true);
          } catch {
            Alert.alert('Error', 'Failed to decline pass.');
          }
        },
      },
    ]);
  };

  const handleConfirmExit = async (pass: GatePassItem) => {
    try {
      await apiClient.post(`gate-passes/${pass.id}/mark-used/`);
      Alert.alert('Exit Logged', 'Bearer departure recorded. Pass marked as USED.');
      setSelectedPass(null);
      fetchPasses(true);
    } catch {
      Alert.alert('Error', 'Failed to confirm exit.');
    }
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top']}>
      {/* Header with Back Arrow Button */}
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <View style={styles.headerLeft}>
          {onBack && (
            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.backBtn} onPress={onBack}>
              <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color="#004b87" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
          <View>
            <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Gate Clearances</Text>
            <Text style={styles.headerSubtitle}>Student Permits &amp; Travel Orders</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {!isGuard && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.newBtn}
            onPress={() => {
              setPassTarget('STUDENT');
              setShowIssueModal(true);
            }}
          >
            <Ionicons name="add" size={16} color="#ffffff" />
            <Text style={styles.newBtnText}>New Pass</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Search Input */}
      <View style={styles.searchBox}>
        <Ionicons name="search" size={16} color={COLORS.textMuted} />
        <TextInput
          style={styles.searchInput}
          placeholder="Search pass #, name, reason..."
          placeholderTextColor={COLORS.textMuted}
          value={searchQuery}
          onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
        />
      </View>

      {/* Filter Row */}
      <View style={styles.filterRow}>
        <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 6 }}>
          {['ACTIVE', 'PENDING', 'ALL', 'USED', 'DECLINED'].map((st) => (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={st}
              style={[styles.filterChip, statusFilter === st && styles.filterChipActive]}
              onPress={() => setStatusFilter(st)}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.filterChipText, statusFilter === st && styles.filterChipTextActive]}>
                {st === 'PENDING' ? 'Pending Review' : st}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ))}
        </ScrollView>
      </View>

      {/* Passes List */}
      {loading ? (
        <View style={styles.centerBox}>
          <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={COLORS.primary} />
          <Text style={styles.centerText}>Loading gate passes...</Text>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <FlatList
          data={filteredPasses}
          keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
          contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
          refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} colors={[COLORS.primary]} />}
          renderItem={({ item }) => {
            const bearer = item.bearer_type === 'STUDENT' ? item.student_name : item.faculty_name;
            const isPending = item.status === 'PENDING';

            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.card}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
                onPress={() => (isPending && isPrincipalOrAdmin ? setReviewingPass(item) : setSelectedPass(item))}
              >
                <View style={styles.cardTop}>
                  <View>
                    <Text style={styles.passCode}>{item.pass_number}</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.bearerType}>{item.bearer_type} CLEARANCE</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.statusPill,
                      {
                        backgroundColor:
                          item.status === 'ACTIVE'
                            ? 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12)'
                            : item.status === 'PENDING'
                            ? 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15)'
                            : item.status === 'DECLINED'
                            ? 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.12)'
                            : '#f1f5f9',
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.statusPillText,
                        {
                          color:
                            item.status === 'ACTIVE'
                              ? '#10b981'
                              : item.status === 'PENDING'
                              ? '#d97706'
                              : item.status === 'DECLINED'
                              ? '#ef4444'
                              : '#64748b',
                        },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {item.status}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                <Text style={styles.bearerName}>{bearer || 'Authorized Bearer'}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.reasonText} numberOfLines={2}>{item.reason}</Text>

                <View style={styles.cardBottom}>
                  <Text style={styles.dateText}>
                    Until: {new Date(item.valid_to).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                  </Text>
                  {isPending && isPrincipalOrAdmin ? (
                    <Text style={[styles.cardAction, { color: '#d97706' }]}>Review Document &gt;</Text>
                  ) : (
                    <Text style={styles.cardAction}>View Slip &gt;</Text>
                  )}
                </View>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* MODAL 1: Create Pass with Proof Document */}
      <Modal visible={showIssueModal} transparent animationType="fade">
        <View style={styles.modalBg}>
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <Text style={styles.modalHeading}>Create Gate Clearance</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setShowIssueModal(false)}>
                <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color="#0f172a" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
              <View style={styles.switchRow}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.switchBtn, passTarget === 'STUDENT' && styles.switchBtnActive]}
                  onPress={() => setPassTarget('STUDENT')}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.switchText, passTarget === 'STUDENT' && styles.switchTextActive]}>
                    Student Exit (Adviser)
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.switchBtn, passTarget === 'FACULTY' && styles.switchBtnActive]}
                  onPress={() => setPassTarget('FACULTY')}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.switchText, passTarget === 'FACULTY' && styles.switchTextActive]}>
                    Faculty Official Travel
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              {passTarget === 'STUDENT' ? (
                <>
                  <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Student Database ID *</Text>
                  <TextInput
                    style={styles.inputField}
                    placeholder="Enter Student ID"
                    keyboardType="numeric"
                    value={form.student_id}
                    onChangeText={(val) => setForm({ ...form, student_id: val })}
                  />
                </>
              ) : (
                <Text style={styles.noticeText}>
                  Your travel pass will be submitted to the School Principal / Admin for document review.
                </Text>
              )}

              <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Valid Reason &amp; Destination *</Text>
              <TextInput
                style={[styles.inputField, { height: 60 }]}
                placeholder="State destination and purpose..."
                multiline
                value={form.reason}
                onChangeText={(val) => setForm({ ...form, reason: val })}
              />

              <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Supporting Proof File *</Text>
              <View style={styles.proofBox}>
                <Ionicons name="document-attach-outline" size={18} color="#004b87" />
                <Text style={styles.proofText}>Proof document selected (Required)</Text>
              </View>

              <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Allowed Duration (Hours)</Text>
              <TextInput
                style={styles.inputField}
                placeholder="4"
                keyboardType="numeric"
                value={form.duration_hours}
                onChangeText={(val) => setForm({ ...form, duration_hours: val })}
              />

              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.submitBtn} onPress={handleIssueSubmit} disabled={submitting}>
                {submitting ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
                ) : (
                  <Text style={styles.submitBtnText}>Submit Gate Clearance</Text>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </ScrollView>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* MODAL 2: Principal / Admin Review */}
      <Modal visible={Boolean(reviewingPass)} transparent animationType="fade">
        <View style={styles.modalBg}>
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <Text style={styles.modalHeading}>Inspect Proof Document</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setReviewingPass(null)}>
                <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color="#0f172a" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            {reviewingPass && (
              <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
                <Text style={styles.detailTitle}>Applicant: {reviewingPass.faculty_name}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.detailDesc}>Reason: {reviewingPass.reason}</Text>

                <View style={styles.docBox}>
                  <Text style={styles.docBoxLabel}>ATTACHED PROOF DOCUMENT</Text>
                  {reviewingPass.attachment_url ? (
                    <Text style={styles.docBoxStatus}>Proof document attached on record.</Text>
                  ) : (
                    <Text style={[styles.docBoxStatus, { color: '#ef4444' }]}>No attachment provided!</Text>
                  )}
                </View>

                <View style={styles.btnRow}>
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[styles.actionBtn, { backgroundColor: '#fee2e2' }]}
                    onPress={() => handleDeclinePass(reviewingPass)}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.actionBtnText, { color: '#ef4444' }]}>Decline (Unverified)</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>

                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[styles.actionBtn, { backgroundColor: '#10b981' }]}
                    onPress={() => handleApprovePass(reviewingPass)}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.actionBtnText, { color: '#ffffff' }]}>Approve Pass</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              </ScrollView>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* MODAL 3: Pass QR Slip */}
      <Modal visible={Boolean(selectedPass)} transparent animationType="slide">
        <View style={styles.modalBg}>
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <Text style={styles.modalHeading}>Gate Clearance Slip</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSelectedPass(null)}>
                <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color="#0f172a" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            {selectedPass && (
              <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
                <View style={styles.qrArea}>
                  <Image
                    source={{
                      uri: `https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=180x180&data=${encodeURIComponent(
                        `ATTENDSURE-GATEPASS:${selectedPass.pass_number}`
                      )}`,
                    }}
                    style={styles.qrImage}
                    contentFit="contain"
                  />
                  <Text style={styles.qrHint}>Present to Gate Guard upon exit.</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.infoRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>Pass Serial:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.infoVal}>{selectedPass.pass_number}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.infoRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>Bearer:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.infoVal}>
                    {selectedPass.bearer_type === 'STUDENT' ? selectedPass.student_name : selectedPass.faculty_name}
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.infoRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>Adviser / Issuer:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.infoVal}>{selectedPass.adviser_name || 'Class Adviser'}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.infoRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>Reason:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.infoVal}>{selectedPass.reason}</Text>
                </View>

                {isGuard && selectedPass.status === 'ACTIVE' && (
                  <TouchableOpacity style={styles.confirmExitBtn} onPress={() => handleConfirmExit(selectedPass)}>
                    <Ionicons name="checkmark-circle" size={16} color="#ffffff" />
                    <Text style={styles.confirmExitBtnText}>Confirm Gate Exit</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
              </ScrollView>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: COLORS.background },
  header: {
    flexDirection: 'row',

File: mobile\src\screens\LoginScreen.tsx

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\screens\LoginScreen.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  ScrollView,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { getServerBaseUrl } from '../services/configService';
import { registerPushTokenWithBackend } from '../services/pushService';
import { startPassivePresenceTracking } from '../services/backgroundLocation';
import { getOrCreateDeviceUuid } from '../services/deviceService';

// Direct SVG vector assets
const attendsureLogoSvg = require('../../assets/attendsure-logo.svg');
const techblazerSvg = require('../../assets/techblazer.svg');
const CACHED_SCHOOL_PROFILE_KEY = 'attendsure_cached_school_profile';

// Cybersecurity: Brute-Force lockout configuration
const MAX_FAILED_ATTEMPTS = 5;
const LOCKOUT_DURATION_MS = 30000;

interface PublicSchoolProfile {
  school_id?: string;
  school_name?: string;
  division?: string;
  region?: string;
  school_logo?: string | null;
  left_logo?: string | null;
}

const showAlert = (title: string, message: string) => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.alert(`${title}: ${message}`);
  } else {
    Alert.alert(title, message);
  }
};

export const LoginScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const isMounted = useRef<boolean>(true);
  
  // Concrete reference typed to prevent TS2339 & TS2322 in generated definitions
  const passwordInputRef = useRef<any>(null);

  // Form State
  const [username, setUsername] = useState<string>('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState<string>('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Live System & Network State
  const [schoolInfo, setSchoolInfo] = useState<PublicSchoolProfile | null>(null);
  const [serverBase, setServerBase] = useState<string>('');
  const [serverOnline, setServerOnline] = useState<'ONLINE' | 'OFFLINE' | 'CONNECTING'>('CONNECTING');
  const [logoFailed, setLogoFailed] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [deviceUuid, setDeviceUuid] = useState<string>('');
  const [devicePlatform] = useState<string>(
    `${Platform.OS.toUpperCase()} (${Platform.Version || 'OS'})`
  );

  // Brute-force attempt counters
  const failedAttemptsRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const lockoutUntilRef = useRef<number>(0);

  // Helper: Sanitize relative media paths to valid server URLs
  const resolvePhotoUrl = useCallback((path: string | null | undefined, base: string) => {
    if (!path) return null;
    if (path.startsWith('http://') || path.startsWith('https://') || path.startsWith('data:')) {
      return path;
    }
    const cleanBase = base.replace(/\/+$/, '');
    const cleanPath = path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`;
    return `${cleanBase}${cleanPath}`;
  }, []);

  // 1. Parallel Data Initialization (AsyncStorage + Live Backend Health)
  const initializeLoginData = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isMounted.current) {
      setServerOnline('CONNECTING');
    }

    try {
      const [cachedProfile, uuid, activeBase] = await Promise.all([
        AsyncStorage.getItem(CACHED_SCHOOL_PROFILE_KEY),
        getOrCreateDeviceUuid().catch(() => ''),
        getServerBaseUrl().catch(() => ''),
      ]);

      if (isMounted.current) {
        if (cachedProfile) {
          try {
            setSchoolInfo(JSON.parse(cachedProfile));
          } catch {
            // Ignore malformed cache
          }
        }
        if (uuid) setDeviceUuid(uuid);
        if (activeBase) setServerBase(activeBase);
      }
    } catch {
      // Continue to live check
    }

    // 3-second live server check
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3000);

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get('/settings/school/', {
        signal: controller.signal,
      });

      if (isMounted.current && res.data) {
        setSchoolInfo(res.data);
        setServerOnline('ONLINE');
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(CACHED_SCHOOL_PROFILE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.data));
      }
    } catch {
      if (isMounted.current) {
        setServerOnline('OFFLINE');
      }
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMounted.current = true;
    initializeLoginData();

    return () => {
      isMounted.current = false;
    };
  }, [initializeLoginData]);

  // 2. Authentication with Input Sanitization & Anti-Brute-Force Guard
  const handleLogin = async () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now < lockoutUntilRef.current) {
      const remainingSec = Math.ceil((lockoutUntilRef.current - now) / 1000);
      showAlert('Account Locked', `Too many failed attempts. Please wait ${remainingSec} seconds.`);
      return;
    }

    // Strip CRLF control characters to prevent header injection
    const cleanUsername = username.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, '').trim();
    const cleanPassword = password.replace(/[\r\n]/g, '').trim();

    if (!cleanUsername || !cleanPassword) {
      showAlert('Required', 'Please enter your Employee ID or Username and Password.');
      return;
    }

    if (cleanUsername.length > 150 || cleanPassword.length > 128) {
      showAlert('Invalid Input', 'Credentials exceed the maximum permitted length.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await login(cleanUsername, cleanPassword);
      failedAttemptsRef.current = 0;

      // Start passive background services quietly
      startPassivePresenceTracking().catch(() => {});
      registerPushTokenWithBackend().catch(() => {});
    } catch (err: any) {
      failedAttemptsRef.current += 1;
      if (failedAttemptsRef.current >= MAX_FAILED_ATTEMPTS) {
        lockoutUntilRef.current = Date.now() + LOCKOUT_DURATION_MS;
      }

      const rawError = String(err?.response?.data?.error || err?.response?.data?.detail || '');
      const isInternalLeak =
        rawError.includes('Traceback') ||
        rawError.includes('django') ||
        rawError.includes('psycopg') ||
        rawError.includes('SyntaxError');

      const safeMessage = isInternalLeak
        ? 'Authentication error. Please contact your school administrator.'
        : rawError ||
          (err?.code === 'ECONNABORTED' || err?.message?.includes('Network Error')
            ? 'Cannot reach school server. Please verify your Wi-Fi or network connection.'
            : 'Invalid username or password.');

      showAlert('Login Failed', safeMessage);
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) {
        setSubmitting(false);
      }
    }
  };

  const rawLogoPath = schoolInfo?.school_logo || schoolInfo?.left_logo;
  const resolvedLogoUri = resolvePhotoUrl(rawLogoPath, serverBase);
  const logoSource = (resolvedLogoUri && !logoFailed) ? { uri: resolvedLogoUri } : attendsureLogoSvg;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container}>
      {/* Correct Expo StatusBar usage: avoids TS2769 */}
      <StatusBar style="dark" />

      <KeyboardAvoidingView
        style={{ flex: 1 }}
        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
      >
        <ScrollView
          contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        >
          {/* Top Status Badge (Tappable to reconnect) */}
          <View style={styles.topUtilityBar}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              activeOpacity={0.75}
              onPress={initializeLoginData}
              style={[
                styles.connectionPill,
                serverOnline === 'ONLINE'
                  ? styles.pillOnline
                  : serverOnline === 'OFFLINE'
                  ? styles.pillOffline
                  : styles.pillChecking,
              ]}
            >
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.statusDot,
                  {
                    backgroundColor:
                      serverOnline === 'ONLINE'
                        ? '#16a34a'
                        : serverOnline === 'OFFLINE'
                        ? '#dc2626'
                        : '#d97706',
                  },
                ]}
              />
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.connectionPillText,
                  {
                    color:
                      serverOnline === 'ONLINE'
                        ? '#15803d'
                        : serverOnline === 'OFFLINE'
                        ? '#b91c1c'
                        : '#b45309',
                  },
                ]}
              >
                {serverOnline === 'ONLINE'
                  ? 'SERVER ONLINE'
                  : serverOnline === 'OFFLINE'
                  ? 'OFFLINE • TAP TO RETRY'
                  : 'CONNECTING...'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {/* School Header with Circular SVG Logo */}
          <View style={styles.brandSection}>
            <View style={styles.circleLogoContainer}>
              <Image
                source={logoSource}
                style={styles.logoImage}
                contentFit="contain"
                onError={() => setLogoFailed(true)}
              />
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.schoolNameText}>
              {schoolInfo?.school_name || 'Lapasan National High School'}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.schoolSubText}>
              {[
                schoolInfo?.school_id ? `School ID: ${schoolInfo.school_id}` : 'School ID: 55555',
                schoolInfo?.division || 'Cagayan de Oro City',
              ]
                .filter(Boolean)
                .join(' • ')}
            </Text>
          </View>

          {/* Form Card */}
          <View style={styles.loginCard}>
            <Text style={styles.cardHeaderTitle}>SIGN IN</Text>

            {/* Username Input */}
            <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
              <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>Employee ID / Username</Text>
              <View style={styles.inputBox}>
                <Ionicons name="person-outline" size={18} color="#64748b" style={{ marginRight: 10 }} />
                <TextInput
                  style={styles.textInput}
                  placeholder="e.g., FACULTY or Employee ID"
                  placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
                  value={username}
                  onChangeText={setUsername}
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  autoCorrect={false}
                  maxLength={150}
                  returnKeyType="next"
                  onSubmitEditing={() => passwordInputRef.current?.focus()}
                  blurOnSubmit={false}
                />
              </View>
            </View>

            {/* Password Input */}
            <View style={styles.fieldGroup}>
              <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>Password</Text>
              <View style={styles.inputBox}>
                <Ionicons
                  name="lock-closed-outline"
                  size={18}
                  color="#64748b"
                  style={{ marginRight: 10 }}
                />
                <TextInput
                  ref={passwordInputRef}
                  style={styles.textInput}
                  placeholder="Enter your password"
                  placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
                  value={password}
                  onChangeText={setPassword}
                  secureTextEntry={!showPassword}
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  maxLength={128}
                  returnKeyType="go"
                  onSubmitEditing={handleLogin}
                />
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => setShowPassword((prev) => !prev)}
                  hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
                >
                  <Ionicons
                    name={showPassword ? 'eye-off-outline' : 'eye-outline'}
                    size={18}
                    color="#64748b"
                  />
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </View>

            {/* Submit Button */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.loginBtn, submitting && { opacity: 0.65 }]}
              onPress={handleLogin}
              disabled={submitting}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
            >
              {submitting ? (
                <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Ionicons name="log-in-outline" size={18} color="#ffffff" />
                  <Text style={styles.loginBtnText}>Sign In</Text>
                </>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {/* Security & TechBlazer Footer Card */}
          <View style={styles.hardwareCard}>
            <View style={styles.hardwareHeader}>
              <Ionicons name="shield-checkmark-outline" size={14} color="#004b87" />
              <Text style={styles.hardwareTitle}>DEVICE LOCK & TELEMETRY</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.hardwareRow}>
              <Text style={styles.hardwareKey}>Platform:</Text>
              <Text style={styles.hardwareVal}>{devicePlatform}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.hardwareRow}>
              <Text style={styles.hardwareKey}>Hardware UUID:</Text>
              <Text style={styles.hardwareVal} numberOfLines={1}>{deviceUuid || 'Bound'}</Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.brandingDivider} />
            <View style={styles.techblazerBranding}>
              <Text style={styles.techblazerText}>System by</Text>
              <Image
                source={techblazerSvg}
                style={styles.techblazerLogoSmall}
                contentFit="contain"
              />
            </View>
          </View>
        </ScrollView>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#f8fafc' },
  scrollContent: {
    flexGrow: 1,
    paddingHorizontal: 20,
    paddingVertical: 16,
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  topUtilityBar: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    marginBottom: 16,
  },
  connectionPill: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingVertical: 4,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    borderRadius: 14,
    borderWidth: 1,
    gap: 6,
  },
  pillOnline: { backgroundColor: '#dcfce7', borderColor: '#bbf7d0' },
  pillOffline: { backgroundColor: '#fef2f2', borderColor: '#fecaca' },
  pillChecking: { backgroundColor: '#fffbeb', borderColor: '#fde68a' },
  statusDot: { width: 6, height: 6, borderRadius: 3 },
  connectionPillText: { fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '800', letterSpacing: 0.4 },

  brandSection: { alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 20 },
  circleLogoContainer: {
    width: 104,
    height: 104,
    borderRadius: 52,
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    borderWidth: 1.5,
    borderColor: '#e2e8f0',
    shadowColor: '#004b87',
    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 },
    shadowOpacity: 0.12,
    shadowRadius: 10,
    elevation: 4,
    marginBottom: 12,
    overflow: 'hidden',
  },
  logoImage: { width: 70, height: 70 },
  schoolNameText: {
    fontSize: 18,
    fontWeight: '900',
    color: '#003460',
    textAlign: 'center',
    letterSpacing: -0.3,
  },
  schoolSubText: { fontSize: 11, color: '#64748b', textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 3 },

  loginCard: {
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderRadius: 16,

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\screens\MenuScreen.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
  Image,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { TabType } from '../navigation/MainTabNavigator';

interface MenuScreenProps {
  onSelectScreen: (screen: TabType) => void;
}

// Cybersecurity: Validate avatar URL against injection and unsafe URI protocols
const isSafeImageUrl = (url?: string | null): url is string => {
  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return false;
  const clean = url.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!clean || clean === 'null' || clean === 'undefined' || clean === 'none') {
    return false;
  }
  if (clean.startsWith('javascript:') || clean.startsWith('vbscript:')) {
    return false;
  }
  if (clean.startsWith('data:') && !clean.startsWith('data:image/')) {
    return false;
  }
  return (
    clean.startsWith('http://') ||
    clean.startsWith('https://') ||
    clean.startsWith('/') ||
    clean.startsWith('data:image/') ||
    clean.startsWith('blob:')
  );
};

export const MenuScreen: React.FC<MenuScreenProps> = ({ onSelectScreen }) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const [photoError, setPhotoError] = useState(false);

  const userRole = String(user?.role || user?.system_role || 'EMPLOYEE').toUpperCase();
  const isAdmin = userRole === 'ADMIN' || userRole === 'SUPERADMIN' || Boolean(user?.is_superuser);
  const isDeptHead = userRole === 'DEPT_HEAD' || isAdmin;
  const isGuard = userRole === 'GUARD' || userRole === 'SECURITY';

  const displayName =
    user?.full_name ||
    `${user?.first_name || ''} ${user?.last_name || ''}`.trim() ||
    user?.username ||
    'Employee';

  const employeeId = user?.employee_id || user?.faculty_id || user?.username || 'ID Pending';

  // Strict string type narrowing: prevents null assignment to Image source.uri
  const photoUri: string | null =
    typeof user?.photo === 'string' && isSafeImageUrl(user.photo) && !photoError
      ? user.photo
      : null;

  return (
    <ScrollView
      style={styles.container}
      contentContainerStyle={[
        styles.scrollContent,
        { paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top, 16), paddingBottom: 110 },
      ]}
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
    >
      {/* Employee Profile Header Card */}
      <View style={styles.userCard}>
        <View style={styles.userRow}>
          {photoUri ? (
            <Image
              source={{ uri: photoUri }}
              style={styles.avatarImage}
              onError={() => setPhotoError(true)}
            />
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.avatarFallback}>
              <Ionicons name="person" size={26} color="#ffffff" />
            </View>
          )}
          <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 12 }}>
            <Text style={styles.userName} numberOfLines={1}>
              {displayName}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.userMeta}>
              {userRole} &bull; Employee ID: {employeeId}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Section 1: Academics, Clearances & Attendance */}
      <Text style={styles.sectionHeader}>ACADEMICS &amp; CLEARANCES</Text>
      <View style={styles.menuGroup}>
        {/* Gate Passes & Exit Clearance */}
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.menuItem}
          onPress={() => onSelectScreen('gate_pass')}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
        >
          <View style={[styles.iconWrap, { backgroundColor: '#e0f2fe' }]}>
            <Ionicons name="ticket-outline" size={19} color="#004b87" />
          </View>
          <View style={styles.itemBody}>
            <Text style={styles.itemTitle}>Gate Passes &amp; Exit Slips</Text>
            <Text style={styles.itemSub}>Issue, view, and verify student exit clearances</Text>
          </View>
          <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color="#94a3b8" />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        {/* Civil Service Form No. 48 (DTR) */}
        {!isGuard && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.menuItem}
            onPress={() => onSelectScreen('dtr')}
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            <View style={[styles.iconWrap, { backgroundColor: '#dcfce7' }]}>
              <Ionicons name="time-outline" size={19} color="#16a34a" />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.itemBody}>
              <Text style={styles.itemTitle}>Daily Time Record (Form 48)</Text>
              <Text style={styles.itemSub}>Monthly hours summary and submission</Text>
            </View>
            <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color="#94a3b8" />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}

        {/* Academic Calendar */}
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.menuItem}
          onPress={() => onSelectScreen('calendar')}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
        >
          <View style={[styles.iconWrap, { backgroundColor: '#fef3c7' }]}>
            <Ionicons name="calendar-outline" size={19} color="#d97706" />
          </View>
          <View style={styles.itemBody}>
            <Text style={styles.itemTitle}>Academic Calendar</Text>
            <Text style={styles.itemSub}>Quarter schedules, exam dates, and holidays</Text>
          </View>
          <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color="#94a3b8" />

      </View>

      {/* Section 2: Department Head Oversight */}
      {isDeptHead && (
        <>
          <Text style={styles.sectionHeader}>DEPARTMENT SUPERVISION</Text>
          <View style={styles.menuGroup}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.menuItem}
              onPress={() => onSelectScreen('dept_monitor')}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
            >
              <View style={[styles.iconWrap, { backgroundColor: '#ffedd5' }]}>
                <Ionicons name="stats-chart-outline" size={18} color="#ea580c" />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.itemBody}>

      {/* Section 3: Campus Hardware & Security */}

File: mobile\src\screens\ProfileSettingsScreen.tsx

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\screens\ProfileSettingsScreen.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  ScrollView,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  RefreshControl,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useNavigation } from '@react-navigation/native';
import QRCode from 'react-native-qrcode-svg';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';

interface LiveFacultyProfile {
  id: number;
  employee_id: string;
  first_name: string;
  last_name: string;
  full_name: string;
  position: string;
  department: string;
  phone_number?: string;
  email?: string;
  photo_url?: string | null;
  photo?: string | null;
  bound_device_id?: string | null;
  device_bound_at?: string | null;
}

interface ExtendedSessionUser {
  id?: number | string;
  username?: string;
  name?: string;
  first_name?: string;
  last_name?: string;
  employee_id?: string;
  role?: string;
  device_id?: string;
  bound_device_id?: string;
}

export const ProfileSettingsScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const navigation = useNavigation();
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();

  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<LiveFacultyProfile | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [qrTimestamp, setQrTimestamp] = useState<number>(Date.now());

  // Fixed endpoint: points to verified 'me/' route instead of non-existent 'faculty/me/'
  const fetchProfile = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<LiveFacultyProfile>('me/', { timeout: 8000 });
      if (res.data) {
        setProfile(res.data);
      }
    } catch {
      // Safe fallback to existing session
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchProfile();
  }, [fetchProfile]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setQrTimestamp(Date.now());
    }, 60000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  const onRefresh = () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    setQrTimestamp(Date.now());
    fetchProfile();
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    Alert.alert('Sign Out', 'Sign out of AttendSure on this device?', [
      { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
      { text: 'Sign Out', style: 'destructive', onPress: async () => await logout() },
    ]);
  };

  const sessionUser = user as ExtendedSessionUser | null;
  const displayName = profile?.full_name || sessionUser?.name || sessionUser?.username || 'Employee';
  const displayId = profile?.employee_id || sessionUser?.employee_id || 'ID Pending';
  const displayPosition = profile?.position || sessionUser?.role || 'Academic Staff';
  const displayDept = profile?.department || 'Department Unassigned';
  const boundDeviceId = profile?.bound_device_id || sessionUser?.device_id || sessionUser?.bound_device_id || null;

  const gatePassPayload = JSON.stringify({
    app: 'ATTENDSURE_GATE_V3',
    emp_id: displayId,
    name: displayName,
    ts: qrTimestamp,
    dev_id: boundDeviceId ? boundDeviceId.slice(0, 8) : 'UNBOUND',
  });

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top']}>
      <View style={styles.appBar}>
        <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => navigation.goBack()} style={styles.backButton}>
          <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={22} color="#0f172a" />
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <Text style={styles.appBarTitle}>Faculty Profile &amp; Pass</Text>
        <View style={{ width: 40 }} />
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} colors={['#004b87']} />}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      >
        <View style={styles.profileCard}>
          <View style={styles.avatarContainer}>
            {profile?.photo_url || profile?.photo ? (
              <Image source={{ uri: profile.photo_url || profile.photo || '' }} style={styles.avatarImage} contentFit="cover" />
            ) : (
              <Ionicons name="person" size={40} color="#004b87" />
            )}
          </View>

          <Text style={styles.profileName}>{displayName}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.profileRole}>{displayPosition}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.profileDept}>{displayDept}</Text>

          <View style={styles.badgeRow}>
            <View style={styles.idBadge}>
              <Ionicons name="card-outline" size={13} color="#004b87" />
              <Text style={styles.idBadgeText}>ID: {displayId}</Text>
            </View>

            <View style={[styles.statusBadge, boundDeviceId ? styles.statusActive : styles.statusPending]}>
              <Ionicons
                name={boundDeviceId ? 'shield-checkmark' : 'shield-outline'}
                size={13}
                color={boundDeviceId ? '#059669' : '#d97706'}
              />
              <Text style={[styles.statusBadgeText, { color: boundDeviceId ? '#059669' : '#d97706' }]}>
                {boundDeviceId ? 'Hardware Paired' : 'Unbound'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.qrCard}>
          <View style={styles.qrHeader}>
            <Ionicons name="qr-code-outline" size={20} color="#004b87" />
            <Text style={styles.qrCardTitle}>Official Gate Clearance Pass</Text>
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.qrSubtitle}>
            Present this dynamic QR code to campus gate kiosks for physical check-in and check-out.
          </Text>

          <View style={styles.qrWrapper}>
            {loading ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="large" color="#004b87" />
            ) : (
              <QRCode value={gatePassPayload} size={180} color="#004b87" backgroundColor="#ffffff" />
            )}
          </View>

          <View style={styles.qrFooter}>
            <Ionicons name="sync-outline" size={13} color="#64748b" />
            <Text style={styles.qrFooterText}>Auto-refreshes every 60s to prevent screenshot reuse</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.logoutButton} onPress={handleLogout} activeOpacity={0.8}>
          <Ionicons name="log-out-outline" size={18} color="#dc2626" />
          <Text style={styles.logoutText}>Sign Out of Device</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};
