    paddingVertical: 12,
    borderRadius: 10,
    backgroundColor: '#fee2e2',
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#fca5a5',
    marginTop: 4,
  },
  logoutText: { fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '800', color: '#dc2626' },
});

export default ProfileSettingsScreen;

========================================================
File: mobile\src\screens\ReportsScreen.tsx
========================================================
// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\screens\ReportsScreen.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
  RefreshControl,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Platform,
  TextInput,
  Share,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { COLORS } from '../constants/theme';

// Standalone Report Components
import DTRReportView from '../components/reports/DTRReportView';
import SF1ReportView from '../components/reports/SF1ReportView';
import SF2ReportView from '../components/reports/SF2ReportView';
import SF4ReportView from '../components/reports/SF4ReportView';

type ReportType = 'DTR' | 'SF1' | 'SF2' | 'SF4';

interface AssignedSection {
  section_id: number;
  section_name: string;
  grade_level?: string;
}

const FULL_MONTHS = [
  'January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June',
  'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December',
];

const SHORT_MONTHS = [
  'Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun',
  'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec',
];

const showAlert = (title: string, message: string) => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.alert(`${title}: ${message}`);
  } else {
    Alert.alert(title, message);
  }
};

const triggerBrowserPrint = (htmlContent: string) => {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return;

  const frameId = 'attendsure-print-frame';
  const existing = document.getElementById(frameId);
  if (existing?.parentNode) existing.parentNode.removeChild(existing);

  const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
  iframe.id = frameId;
  iframe.style.position = 'fixed';
  iframe.style.top = '-10000px';
  iframe.style.left = '-10000px';
  iframe.style.width = '1200px';
  iframe.style.height = '900px';
  iframe.style.border = '0';
  document.body.appendChild(iframe);

  const win = iframe.contentWindow;
  const doc = win?.document;
  if (doc && win) {
    doc.open();
    doc.write(htmlContent);
    doc.close();
    setTimeout(() => {
      win.focus();
      win.print();
    }, 350);
  }
};

export const ReportsScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const now = new Date();
  const isMounted = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  // 1. Role-Based Permissions
  const currentUser = user as Record<string, any> | null;
  const userRole = String(
    currentUser?.role || currentUser?.system_role || 'EMPLOYEE'
  ).trim().toUpperCase();

  const isAdmin = ['ADMIN', 'SUPERADMIN', 'PRINCIPAL', 'REGISTRAR'].includes(userRole) || Boolean(currentUser?.is_superuser);
  const isFaculty = ['FACULTY', 'FACULTY'].includes(userRole);
  const isStaffOnly = ['STAFF', 'GUARD', 'SECURITY'].includes(userRole);

  const availableTabs = useMemo(() => {
    const tabs: Array<{ key: ReportType; label: string; icon: keyof typeof Ionicons.glyphMap }> = [];

    // Form 48 for all staff
    tabs.push({ key: 'DTR', label: 'DTR', icon: 'time-outline' });

    // Classroom Registers (SF1 & SF2)
    if (isAdmin || isFaculty) {
      tabs.push({ key: 'SF1', label: 'SF1', icon: 'people-outline' });
      tabs.push({ key: 'SF2', label: 'SF2', icon: 'calendar-outline' });
    }

    // Consolidated Movement (SF4)
    if (isAdmin) {
      tabs.push({ key: 'SF4', label: 'SF4', icon: 'stats-chart-outline' });
    }

    return tabs;
  }, [isAdmin, isFaculty]);

  const [selectedTab, setSelectedTab] = useState<ReportType>(() => {
    if (isAdmin || isFaculty) return 'SF1';
    return 'DTR';
  });

  const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] = useState<number>(now.getMonth() + 1);
  const [selectedYear, setSelectedYear] = useState<number>(now.getFullYear());
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');

  const [sections, setSections] = useState<AssignedSection[]>([]);
  const [selectedSectionId, setSelectedSectionId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  // Active Payloads
  const [dtrData, setDtrData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [sf1Data, setSf1Data] = useState<any>(null);
  const [sf2Data, setSf2Data] = useState<any>(null);
  const [sf4Data, setSf4Data] = useState<any>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [submittingDtr, setSubmittingDtr] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [exportingPdf, setExportingPdf] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [printingDoc, setPrintingDoc] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Fixed: typed as any to eliminate TS2339 / TS2322 ref errors
  const monthScrollRef = useRef<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const isCurrentTabAllowed = availableTabs.some((t) => t.key === selectedTab);
    if (!isCurrentTabAllowed && availableTabs.length > 0) {
      setSelectedTab(availableTabs[0].key);
    }
  }, [availableTabs, selectedTab]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (monthScrollRef.current && selectedMonth > 3) {
        monthScrollRef.current.scrollTo({ x: Math.max(0, (selectedMonth - 2) * 82), animated: true });
      }
    }, 200);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [selectedMonth, selectedTab]);

  const loadSections = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isStaffOnly) return;

    try {
      const [classListRes, secRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
        apiClient.get('classroom/student_list/').catch(() => apiClient.get('classroom/class_list/')),
        apiClient.get('sections/'),
      ]);

      const map = new Map<number, AssignedSection>();

      if (classListRes.status === 'fulfilled' && Array.isArray(classListRes.value?.data)) {
        classListRes.value.data.forEach((item: any) => {
          if (item?.section_id && !map.has(item.section_id)) {
            map.set(item.section_id, {
              section_id: item.section_id,
              section_name: item.section_name,
            });
          }
        });
      }

      if (map.size === 0 && secRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
        const raw = Array.isArray(secRes.value?.data)
          ? secRes.value.data
          : secRes.value?.data?.results || [];
        raw.forEach((s: any) => {
          if (s?.id && !map.has(s.id)) {
            map.set(s.id, {
              section_id: s.id,
              section_name: s.name,
              grade_level: s.grade_level,
            });
          }
        });
      }

      const list = Array.from(map.values());
      setSections(list);
      if (list.length > 0) {
        setSelectedSectionId((prev) => prev ?? list[0].section_id);
      }
    } catch {
      setSections([]);
    }
  }, [isStaffOnly]);

  const loadReportData = useCallback(async () => {
    if (abortControllerRef.current) {
      abortControllerRef.current.abort();
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortControllerRef.current = controller;

    setLoading(true);
    const monthName = FULL_MONTHS[selectedMonth - 1] || 'October';

    try {
      if (selectedTab === 'DTR') {
        const res = await apiClient.get<any>('reports/dtr/', {
          params: { month: selectedMonth, year: selectedYear },
          signal: controller.signal,
        }).catch(() => apiClient.get<any>('dtr/', {
          params: { month: selectedMonth, year: selectedYear },
          signal: controller.signal,
        }));
        if (isMounted.current) setDtrData(res.data || null);
      } else if (selectedTab === 'SF1' && selectedSectionId && (isAdmin || isFaculty)) {
        const res = await apiClient.get(`reports/sf1/${selectedSectionId}/`, {
          signal: controller.signal,
        });
        if (isMounted.current) setSf1Data(res.data || null);
      } else if (selectedTab === 'SF2' && selectedSectionId && (isAdmin || isFaculty)) {
        const res = await apiClient.get(`reports/sf2/${selectedSectionId}/`, {
          params: { month: monthName, year: selectedYear },
          signal: controller.signal,
        });
        if (isMounted.current) setSf2Data(res.data || null);
      } else if (selectedTab === 'SF4' && isAdmin) {
        const res = await apiClient.get('reports/sf4/', {
          params: { month: monthName, year: selectedYear },
          signal: controller.signal,
        });
        if (isMounted.current) setSf4Data(res.data || null);
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore request cancellations
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) {
        setLoading(false);
        setRefreshing(false);
      }
    }
  }, [selectedTab, selectedMonth, selectedYear, selectedSectionId, isAdmin, isFaculty]);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMounted.current = true;
    loadSections();
    return () => {
      isMounted.current = false;
      if (abortControllerRef.current) abortControllerRef.current.abort();
    };
  }, [loadSections]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadReportData();
  }, [loadReportData]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(() => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    loadReportData();
  }, [loadReportData]);

  const handleSubmitDtr = async () => {
    if (!dtrData) return;
    setSubmittingDtr(true);
    try {
      const facultyId = dtrData.faculty_id || currentUser?.id;
      const payload = {
        action: 'SUBMIT_DTR',
        faculty_id: facultyId,
        month: dtrData.month_number || selectedMonth,
        year: dtrData.year || selectedYear,
        regular_days_hours: dtrData.regular_days_hours || '8:00 AM - 12:00 PM / 1:00 PM - 5:00 PM',
        saturdays_hours: dtrData.saturdays_hours || 'As Required',
      };

      await apiClient.post(`reports/dtr/${facultyId}/`, payload)
        .catch(() => apiClient.post('dtr/', payload));

      showAlert('Success', 'DTR submitted to your Department Head.');
      await loadReportData();
    } catch (err: any) {
      showAlert('Error', err?.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to submit DTR.');
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setSubmittingDtr(false);
    }
  };

  const fetchReportExportPayload = async () => {
    const monthName = FULL_MONTHS[selectedMonth - 1] || 'October';
    const payload = {
      report_type: selectedTab,
      section_id: selectedTab === 'SF4' ? undefined : selectedSectionId || undefined,
      month: selectedTab === 'DTR' ? selectedMonth : monthName,
      year: selectedYear,
    };

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.post('reports/export/', payload);
      return res.data;
    } catch (postErr: any) {
      if (postErr?.response?.status === 405) {
        const res = await apiClient.get('reports/export/', { params: payload });
        return res.data;
      }
      throw postErr;
    }
  };

  const handlePrint = async () => {
    if ((selectedTab === 'SF1' || selectedTab === 'SF2') && !selectedSectionId) {
      showAlert('Required', 'Please select a class section.');
      return;
    }

    setPrintingDoc(true);
    try {
      const data = await fetchReportExportPayload();
      const html = data?.html || '';

      if (Platform.OS === 'web' && html) {
        triggerBrowserPrint(html);
      } else {
        try {
          const Print = require('expo-print');
          await Print.printAsync({ html });
        } catch {
          await Share.share({
            title: `Print_${selectedTab}`,
            message: `Official ${selectedTab} Attendance Document ready for printing.`,
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      showAlert('Notice', err?.response?.data?.error || 'Could not send document to printer.');
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setPrintingDoc(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDownloadPDF = async () => {
    if ((selectedTab === 'SF1' || selectedTab === 'SF2') && !selectedSectionId) {
      showAlert('Required', 'Please select a class section.');
      return;
    }

    setExportingPdf(true);
    try {
      const data = await fetchReportExportPayload();
      const html = data?.html || '';
      const title = data?.title || `${selectedTab}_Report`;

      if (Platform.OS === 'web' && html) {
        triggerBrowserPrint(html);
      } else {
        try {
          const Print = require('expo-print');
          const Sharing = require('expo-sharing');
          const { uri } = await Print.printToFileAsync({ html });
          await Sharing.shareAsync(uri, { UTI: '.pdf', mimeType: 'application/pdf' });
        } catch {
          await Share.share({
            title,
            message: `${title}\n\nAttendSure Official PDF Document.`,
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      showAlert('Notice', err?.response?.data?.error || 'Could not export PDF report.');
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setExportingPdf(false);
    }
  };

  const filteredSf1 = useMemo(() => {
    const raw = sf1Data?.learners || sf1Data?.students || [];
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) return raw;
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    return raw.filter(
      (st: any) =>
        String(st.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        String(st.lrn || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [sf1Data, searchQuery]);

  const filteredSf2 = useMemo(() => {
    if (!sf2Data) return [];
    const raw = [
      ...(Array.isArray(sf2Data.males) ? sf2Data.males : []),
      ...(Array.isArray(sf2Data.females) ? sf2Data.females : []),
      ...(Array.isArray(sf2Data.learners) ? sf2Data.learners : []),
    ];
    const seen = new Set();
    const unique = raw.filter((i) => {
      const k = i.id || i.lrn || i.name;
      if (seen.has(k)) return false;
      seen.add(k);
      return true;
    });
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) return unique;
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    return unique.filter(
      (st: any) =>
        String(st.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        String(st.lrn || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [sf2Data, searchQuery]);

  const filteredSf4 = useMemo(() => {
    const groups = sf4Data?.grade_groups || [];
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) return groups;
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    return groups.filter((g: any) => String(g.grade_level || '').toLowerCase().includes(q));
  }, [sf4Data, searchQuery]);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top', 'left', 'right']}>
      {/* Top Header */}
      <View style={styles.topHeader}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <Text style={styles.screenTitle}>Reports</Text>
          <Text style={styles.screenSub}>Export &amp; Print</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6 }}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.btnSec, (loading || printingDoc) && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
            onPress={handlePrint}
            disabled={loading || printingDoc}
          >
            {printingDoc ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#004b87" />
            ) : (
              <>
                <Ionicons name="print-outline" size={15} color="#004b87" />
                <Text style={styles.btnSecText}>Print</Text>
              </>
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.btnPri, (loading || exportingPdf) && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
            onPress={handleDownloadPDF}
            disabled={loading || exportingPdf}
          >
            {exportingPdf ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
            ) : (
              <>
                <Ionicons name="download-outline" size={15} color="#ffffff" />
                <Text style={styles.btnPriText}>PDF</Text>
              </>
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Role-Filtered Tab Switcher */}
      <View style={styles.tabRow}>
        {availableTabs.map((tab) => {
          const active = selectedTab === tab.key;
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={tab.key}
              style={[styles.tabBtn, active && styles.tabBtnActive]}
              onPress={() => {
                setSelectedTab(tab.key);
                setSearchQuery('');
              }}
            >
              <Ionicons name={tab.icon} size={13} color={active ? '#ffffff' : '#475569'} />
              <Text style={[styles.tabBtnText, active && styles.tabBtnTextActive]}>
                {tab.label}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} colors={[COLORS.primary]} />
        }
      >
        {/* Month Selector for DTR, SF2, SF4 */}
        {selectedTab !== 'SF1' && (
          <ScrollView
            ref={monthScrollRef}
            horizontal
            showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
            style={styles.monthStrip}
          >
            {SHORT_MONTHS.map((mName, idx) => {
              const mNum = idx + 1;
              const active = selectedMonth === mNum;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={mName}
                  style={[styles.monthChip, active && styles.monthChipActive]}
                  onPress={() => setSelectedMonth(mNum)}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.monthChipText, active && { color: '#ffffff' }]}>
                    {mName} {selectedYear}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </ScrollView>
        )}

        {/* Section Selector for SF1 & SF2 */}
        {(selectedTab === 'SF1' || selectedTab === 'SF2') && (
          <View style={styles.card}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionHeaderTitle}>SECTION</Text>
            {sections.length === 0 ? (
              <Text style={styles.emptyText}>No assigned sections found.</Text>
            ) : (
              <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}>
                {sections.map((sec) => {
                  const active = sec.section_id === selectedSectionId;
                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      key={String(sec.section_id)}
                      style={[styles.secPill, active && styles.secPillActive]}
                      onPress={() => setSelectedSectionId(sec.section_id)}
                    >
                      <Ionicons
                        name="school-outline"
                        size={13}
                        color={active ? '#ffffff' : '#004b87'}
                      />
                      <Text style={[styles.secPillText, active && { color: '#ffffff' }]}>
                        {sec.section_name}
                      </Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                })}
              </ScrollView>
            )}
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Quick Search */}
        {selectedTab !== 'DTR' && (
          <View style={styles.searchBox}>
            <Ionicons name="search-outline" size={16} color="#64748b" />
            <TextInput
              style={styles.searchInput}
              value={searchQuery}
              onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
              placeholder={
                selectedTab === 'SF4' ? 'Filter grade level...' : 'Search learner or LRN...'
              }
              placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
            />
            {searchQuery.length > 0 && (
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSearchQuery('')}>
                <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={16} color="#94a3b8" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Dynamic Report Content */}
        {loading ? (
          <View style={styles.card}>
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={COLORS.primary} style={{ marginVertical: 24 }} />
            <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Loading {selectedTab} records...</Text>
          </View>
        ) : selectedTab === 'DTR' ? (
          <DTRReportView
            data={dtrData}
            selectedMonth={selectedMonth}
            selectedYear={selectedYear}
            onMonthChange={(m, y) => {
              setSelectedMonth(m);
              setSelectedYear(y);
            }}
            submitting={submittingDtr}
            onSubmit={handleSubmitDtr}
            onRefreshNeeded={() => loadReportData()}
          />
        ) : selectedTab === 'SF1' ? (
          <SF1ReportView data={sf1Data} learners={filteredSf1} />
        ) : selectedTab === 'SF2' ? (
          <SF2ReportView data={sf2Data} learners={filteredSf2} />
        ) : selectedTab === 'SF4' ? (
          <SF4ReportView data={sf4Data} gradeGroups={filteredSf4} />
        ) : null}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#f8fafc' },
  topHeader: {
    flexDirection: 'row',

/**
 * AttendSure V3 - Mobile Classroom Attendance Camera Scanner
 * File: mobile/src/screens/ScannerScreen.tsx
 * Optical camera only. OTG barcode gun handling is reserved for the web application.
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Alert,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Animated,
  Vibration,
  Platform,
  FlatList,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { CameraView, useCameraPermissions } from 'expo-camera';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { COLORS } from '../constants/theme';

interface VerifiedStudent {
  student_id: number;
  student_name: string;
  lrn: string;
  section: string;
  authorization_role: string;
  scanned_at: string;
  photo_url?: string | null;
  gate_status_note?: string;
  is_duplicate?: boolean;
}

export const ScannerScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [permission, requestPermission] = useCameraPermissions();

  const [torchOn, setTorchOn] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [activeStudent, setActiveStudent] = useState<VerifiedStudent | null>(null);
  const [scannedHistory, setScannedHistory] = useState<VerifiedStudent[]>([]);
  const [showHistorySheet, setShowHistorySheet] = useState(false);

  // Fast scanning locks: prevent redundant camera frame triggers
  const isProcessingRef = useRef(false);
  const lastScannedCodeRef = useRef('');
  const lastScannedTimeRef = useRef(0);
  const laserAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  // Viewfinder laser line animation
  useEffect(() => {
    const loop = Animated.loop(
      Animated.sequence([
        Animated.timing(laserAnim, {
          toValue: 1,
          duration: 1800,
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
        Animated.timing(laserAnim, {
          toValue: 0,
          duration: 1800,
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
      ])
    );
    loop.start();
    return () => loop.stop();
  }, [laserAnim]);

  // Cybersecurity: Clean and validate QR code data before dispatching
  const sanitizeScanInput = (raw: string): string | null => {
    if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'string') return null;
    const clean = raw.trim().replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, '');

    // Allow only alphanumeric identifiers, dashes, and underscores (3 to 64 characters)
    const validPattern = /^[a-zA-Z0-9\-_:]{3,64}$/;
    if (!validPattern.test(clean)) return null;
    return clean;
  };

  const handleProcessCode = useCallback(
    async (rawCode: string) => {
      const cleanCode = sanitizeScanInput(rawCode);
      const now = Date.now();

      // Drop malicious, malformed, or rapid duplicate camera triggers
      if (!cleanCode) return;
      if (isProcessingRef.current) return;
      if (cleanCode === lastScannedCodeRef.current && now - lastScannedTimeRef.current < 4000) {
        return;
      }

      isProcessingRef.current = true;
      lastScannedCodeRef.current = cleanCode;
      lastScannedTimeRef.current = now;
      setLoading(true);

      try {
        const deviceUuid = `MOBILE-${Platform.OS}-${user?.username || 'STAFF'}`;
        const res = await apiClient.post('academics/classroom-scan/', {
          code: cleanCode,
          device_uuid: deviceUuid,
        });

        if (res.data?.success) {
          const student: VerifiedStudent = {
            student_id: res.data.student_id,
            student_name: res.data.student_name,
            lrn: res.data.lrn,
            section: res.data.section,
            authorization_role: res.data.authorization_role || 'Faculty',
            scanned_at: res.data.scanned_at,
            photo_url: res.data.photo_url || null,
            gate_status_note: res.data.gate_status_note,
            is_duplicate: Boolean(res.data.is_duplicate),
          };

          setActiveStudent(student);

          if (student.is_duplicate) {
            Vibration.vibrate(120);
          } else {
            Vibration.vibrate([0, 50, 40, 50]);
            setScannedHistory((prev) => [
              student,
              ...prev.filter((item) => item.lrn !== student.lrn),
            ]);
          }
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        Vibration.vibrate(250);
        const errorMessage =
          err?.response?.data?.error ||
          'Attendance could not be recorded. Ensure the student is enrolled in this section.';
        Alert.alert('Attendance Alert', errorMessage);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
        // Small delay before unlocking camera frames to keep processing smooth
        setTimeout(() => {
          isProcessingRef.current = false;
        }, 1200);
      }
    },
    [user]
  );

  if (!permission) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.centerBox}>
        <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={COLORS.primary} />
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (!permission.granted) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.permissionBox}>
          <Ionicons name="camera-outline" size={56} color={COLORS.primary} />
          <Text style={styles.permissionTitle}>Camera Access Required</Text>
          <Text style={styles.permissionDesc}>
            The mobile application uses your phone camera to scan student ID cards for classroom attendance.
          </Text>
          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.primaryBtn} onPress={requestPermission}>
            <Text style={styles.btnTextWhite}>Allow Camera Access</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }

  const laserTranslate = laserAnim.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 1],
    outputRange: [0, 200],
  });

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top']}>
      {/* Header Bar */}
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <View>
          <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Classroom Scanner</Text>
          <Text style={styles.headerSubtitle}>Optical Camera Attendance Mode</Text>
        </View>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.actionIconBtn, torchOn && styles.actionIconBtnActive]}
          onPress={() => setTorchOn(!torchOn)}
        >
          <Ionicons
            name={torchOn ? 'flash' : 'flash-outline'}
            size={18}
            color={torchOn ? '#f59e0b' : '#334155'}
          />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {/* Main Camera Viewport */}
      <View style={styles.cameraContainer}>
        <CameraView
          style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
          facing="back"
          enableTorch={torchOn}
          barcodeScannerSettings={{
            barcodeTypes: ['qr', 'code128', 'ean13'],
          }}
          onBarcodeScanned={({ data }) => {
            if (data) {
              handleProcessCode(data);
            }
          }}
        />

        {/* Viewfinder Target */}
        <View style={styles.viewfinderCenter}>
          <View style={styles.targetWindow}>
            <Animated.View
              style={[
                styles.laserLine,
                { transform: [{ translateY: laserTranslate }] },
              ]}
            />
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.viewfinderHint}>Align Student QR Code inside frame</Text>
        </View>

        {loading && (
          <View style={styles.loadingOverlay}>
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
            <Text style={styles.loadingOverlayText}>Checking record...</Text>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Verified Student Confirmation Card */}
      {activeStudent && (
        <View style={styles.verifiedCard}>
          <View style={styles.verifiedRow}>
            <View style={styles.avatarWrap}>
              {activeStudent.photo_url ? (
                <Image source={{ uri: activeStudent.photo_url }} style={styles.avatarImg} />
              ) : (
                <Ionicons name="person" size={24} color="#ffffff" />
              )}
            </View>

            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <View style={styles.verifiedHeaderLine}>
                <Text style={styles.studentName} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {activeStudent.student_name}
                </Text>
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.statusBadge,
                    { backgroundColor: activeStudent.is_duplicate ? '#fef3c7' : '#ecfdf5' },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.statusBadgeText,
                      { color: activeStudent.is_duplicate ? '#d97706' : '#059669' },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {activeStudent.is_duplicate ? 'ALREADY SCANNED' : 'PRESENT'}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <Text style={styles.sectionText}>
                LRN: {activeStudent.lrn} &bull; {activeStudent.section}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.authBadge}>
                Recorded by {activeStudent.authorization_role} at {activeStudent.scanned_at}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setActiveStudent(null)} style={{ padding: 4 }}>
              <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={22} color="#94a3b8" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      )}

      {/* Bottom Bar: Scanned Count & Class List */}
      <View style={styles.bottomBar}>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
          <Ionicons name="people-outline" size={16} color="#64748b" />
          <Text style={styles.bottomBarText}>
            Class List Scanned Today:{' '}
            <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800', color: COLORS.primary }}>
              {scannedHistory.length}
            </Text>
          </Text>
        </View>

        {scannedHistory.length > 0 && (
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setShowHistorySheet(!showHistorySheet)}>
            <Text style={styles.viewHistoryText}>
              {showHistorySheet ? 'Hide List' : 'View Class List'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Class List Drawer */}
      {showHistorySheet && (
        <View style={styles.historyDrawer}>
          <Text style={styles.drawerTitle}>Class List Scans ({scannedHistory.length})</Text>
          <FlatList
            data={scannedHistory}
            keyExtractor={(item) => item.lrn}
            style={{ maxHeight: 180 }}
            renderItem={({ item }) => (
              <View style={styles.historyItem}>
                <Text style={styles.historyName}>{item.student_name}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.historyTime}>{item.scanned_at}</Text>
              </View>
            )}
          />
        </View>
      )}
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#ffffff' },
  centerBox: { flex: 1, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center' },
  header: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderBottomColor: '#e2e8f0',
  },
  headerTitle: { fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '800', color: '#003460' },
  headerSubtitle: { fontSize: 11, color: '#64748b' },
  actionIconBtn: {
    width: 36,
    height: 36,
    borderRadius: 8,
    backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  actionIconBtnActive: { backgroundColor: '#fef3c7' },
  cameraContainer: { flex: 1, position: 'relative', backgroundColor: '#000000' },
  viewfinderCenter: {
    flex: 1,
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)',
  },
  targetWindow: {
    width: 220,
    height: 220,
    borderWidth: 2,
    borderColor: '#38bdf8',
    borderRadius: 16,
    overflow: 'hidden',
    position: 'relative',
  },
  laserLine: {
    width: '100%',
    height: 2,
    backgroundColor: '#38bdf8',
    shadowColor: '#38bdf8',
    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 0 },
    shadowOpacity: 0.9,
    shadowRadius: 6,
  },
  viewfinderHint: {
    color: '#ffffff',
    fontSize: 11.5,
    fontWeight: '600',
    marginTop: 12,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.65)',
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 4,
    borderRadius: 12,
  },
  loadingOverlay: {
    position: 'absolute',
    top: 20,
    alignSelf: 'center',
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 8,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.75)',
    paddingHorizontal: 14,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    borderRadius: 20,
  },
  loadingOverlayText: { color: '#ffffff', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '700' },
  verifiedCard: {
    position: 'absolute',
    bottom: 52,
    left: 12,
    right: 12,
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderRadius: 14,
    padding: 12,
    shadowColor: '#000000',
    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 },
    shadowOpacity: 0.15,
    shadowRadius: 10,
    elevation: 8,
    borderLeftWidth: 4,
    borderLeftColor: '#10b981',
  },
  verifiedRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 },
  avatarWrap: {
    width: 44,
    height: 44,
    borderRadius: 22,
    backgroundColor: '#004b87',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    overflow: 'hidden',
  },
  avatarImg: { width: 44, height: 44 },
  verifiedHeaderLine: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  studentName: { fontSize: 14, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a', flex: 1 },
  statusBadge: { paddingHorizontal: 6, paddingVertical: 2, borderRadius: 6 },
  statusBadgeText: { fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '800' },
  sectionText: { fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '700', color: COLORS.primary, marginTop: 1 },
  authBadge: { fontSize: 10, color: '#64748b', marginTop: 2 },
  bottomBar: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderTopWidth: 1,
    borderTopColor: '#e2e8f0',
  },
  bottomBarText: { fontSize: 12, color: '#64748b' },
  viewHistoryText: { fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '700', color: COLORS.primary },
  historyDrawer: {
    backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
    padding: 12,
    borderTopWidth: 1,
    borderTopColor: '#e2e8f0',
  },
  drawerTitle: { fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '800', color: '#64748b', marginBottom: 6 },
  historyItem: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    paddingVertical: 6,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderBottomColor: '#f1f5f9',
  },
  historyName: { fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '600', color: '#0f172a' },
  historyTime: { fontSize: 11, color: '#94a3b8' },
  permissionBox: { flex: 1, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', padding: 24 },
  permissionTitle: { fontSize: 17, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a', marginTop: 14 },
  permissionDesc: {
    fontSize: 12.5,
    color: '#64748b',

    paddingHorizontal: 20,
    borderRadius: 8,
    marginTop: 18,
  },
  btnTextWhite: { color: '#ffffff', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '700' },
});

export default ScannerScreen;

========================================================
File: mobile\src\screens\SettingsScreen.tsx
========================================================
/**
 * AttendSure - User Profile & Application Settings Screen
 * File: mobile/src/screens/SettingsScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import QRCode from 'react-native-qrcode-svg';
import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { getServerBaseUrl } from '../services/configService';

export interface ExtendedUserProfile {
  id?: number | string;
  username?: string;
  first_name?: string;
  last_name?: string;
  middle_name?: string;
  suffix?: string;
  full_name?: string;
  email?: string;
  contact_number?: string;
  phone?: string;
  position?: string;
  department?: string;
  role?: string;
  employee_id?: string;
  faculty_id?: string | number;
  photo?: string | null;
  photo_url?: string | null;
}

export const SettingsScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, logout, updateUser } = useAuth();

  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<ExtendedUserProfile | null>(user as ExtendedUserProfile | null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [serverBase, setServerBase] = useState<string>('');

  // Editable user fields
  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState<string>(user?.first_name || '');
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState<string>(user?.last_name || '');
  const [contactNumber, setContactNumber] = useState<string>(user?.contact_number || '');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState<string>(user?.email || '');
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState<string>('');

  // Profile photo state
  const [newPhotoBase64, setNewPhotoBase64] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [photoPreview, setPhotoPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const resolvePhotoUri = useCallback(
    (url?: string | null): string | undefined => {
      if (!url) return undefined;
      if (url.startsWith('http://') || url.startsWith('https://') || url.startsWith('data:image/')) {
        return url;
      }
      return `${serverBase.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/${url.replace(/^\/+/, '')}`;
    },
    [serverBase]
  );

  // Automatically fetch profile information in the background
  const fetchProfile = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<ExtendedUserProfile>('me/');
      if (res.data) {
        setProfile(res.data);
        setFirstName(res.data.first_name || '');
        setLastName(res.data.last_name || '');
        setContactNumber(res.data.contact_number || res.data.phone || '');
        setEmail(res.data.email || '');
      }
    } catch {
      // Keep session state intact
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    getServerBaseUrl().then((url) => setServerBase(url));
    fetchProfile();
  }, [fetchProfile]);

  // Pick new profile picture with size validation
  const handlePickPhoto = async () => {
    try {
      const { status } = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();
      if (status !== 'granted') {
        Alert.alert('Permission Needed', 'Please allow photo library access in phone settings.');
        return;
      }

      const result = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
        mediaTypes: ['images'],
        allowsEditing: true,
        aspect: [1, 1],
        quality: 0.6,
        base64: true,
      });

      if (!result.canceled && result.assets && result.assets[0]) {
        const asset = result.assets[0];

        // Cybersecurity check: file size limit 5 MB
        if (asset.fileSize && asset.fileSize > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
          Alert.alert('File Too Large', 'Please select a photo smaller than 5 MB.');
          return;
        }

        if (asset.base64) {
          setNewPhotoBase64(`data:image/jpeg;base64,${asset.base64}`);
          setPhotoPreview(asset.uri);
        } else {
          setPhotoPreview(asset.uri);
        }
      }
    } catch {
      Alert.alert('Photo Error', 'Unable to open photo picker.');
    }
  };

  // Save personal updates to the database
  const handleSaveProfile = async () => {
    if (!firstName.trim() || !lastName.trim()) {
      Alert.alert('Missing Name', 'First name and last name are required.');
      return;
    }

    if (newPassword.trim() && newPassword.trim().length < 8) {
      Alert.alert('Weak Password', 'Password must be at least 8 characters long.');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const payload: Record<string, string> = {
        first_name: firstName.trim(),
        last_name: lastName.trim(),
        contact_number: contactNumber.trim(),
        email: email.trim(),
      };

      if (newPassword.trim()) {
        payload.password = newPassword.trim();
      }

      if (newPhotoBase64) {
        payload.photo = newPhotoBase64;
      }

      const res = await apiClient.put('me/', payload);
      if (res.data) {
        setProfile(res.data);
        updateUser(res.data);
      }

      setNewPassword('');
      setNewPhotoBase64(null);
      Alert.alert('Success', 'Your personal information has been saved.');
    } catch (err: any) {
      const msg = err?.response?.data?.error || err?.response?.data?.detail || 'Failed to update personal details.';
      Alert.alert('Update Failed', msg);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const qrPayload = JSON.stringify({
    app: 'ATTENDSURE_PASS',
    id: profile?.employee_id || profile?.username || 'USER',
    name: profile?.full_name || `${firstName} ${lastName}`.trim(),
    role: profile?.role || 'FACULTY',
  });

  const displayPhoto = photoPreview || resolvePhotoUri(profile?.photo_url || profile?.photo);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['left', 'right']}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        {/* Profile Header & Photo Manager */}
        <View style={styles.profileHeaderCard}>
          <View style={styles.avatarWrapper}>
            <View style={styles.avatarCircle}>
              {displayPhoto ? (
                <Image source={{ uri: displayPhoto }} style={styles.avatarImg} contentFit="cover" />
              ) : (
                <Ionicons name="person" size={40} color="#004b87" />
              )}
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.cameraBadgeBtn}
              onPress={handlePickPhoto}
              accessibilityLabel="Change Photo"
            >
              <Ionicons name="camera" size={15} color="#ffffff" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <Text style={styles.profileName}>
            {profile?.full_name || `${firstName} ${lastName}`.trim() || 'User Account'}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.profileRole}>{profile?.position || profile?.role || 'Staff Member'}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.profileDept}>{profile?.department || 'Academic Department'}</Text>

          <View style={styles.badgeRow}>
            <View style={styles.idChip}>
              <Ionicons name="id-card-outline" size={12} color="#004b87" />
              <Text style={styles.idChipText}>ID: {profile?.employee_id || profile?.username || '—'}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.idChip}>
              <Ionicons name="shield-checkmark-outline" size={12} color="#059669" />
              <Text style={[styles.idChipText, { color: '#059669' }]}>Verified Account</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Digital School Clearance Pass */}
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Digital Gate Pass</Text>
          <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>Scan this QR code at campus gate kiosks for check-in and check-out.</Text>
          <View style={styles.qrContainer}>
            <QRCode value={qrPayload} size={140} color="#004b87" backgroundColor="#ffffff" />
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* User Information Form */}
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Personal Information</Text>
          <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>Manage your name, contact phone, email, and password.</Text>

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>First Name</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={firstName}
            onChangeText={setFirstName}
            placeholder="Enter first name"
            placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
          />

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Last Name</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={lastName}
            onChangeText={setLastName}
            placeholder="Enter last name"
            placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
          />

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Email Address</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={email}
            onChangeText={setEmail}
            keyboardType="email-address"
            autoCapitalize="none"
            placeholder="name@school.edu"
            placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
          />

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Mobile Phone Number</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={contactNumber}
            onChangeText={setContactNumber}
            keyboardType="phone-pad"
            placeholder="09XXXXXXXXX"
            placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
          />

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>New Password (leave blank to keep current)</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={newPassword}
            onChangeText={setNewPassword}
            secureTextEntry
            placeholder="Minimum 8 characters"
            placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
          />

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.saveBtn, saving && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
            onPress={handleSaveProfile}
            disabled={saving}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            {saving ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
            ) : (
              <Text style={styles.saveBtnText}>Save Changes</Text>
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {/* Sign Out Button */}
        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.logoutBtn} onPress={logout} activeOpacity={0.8}>
          <Ionicons name="log-out-outline" size={18} color="#dc2626" />
          <Text style={styles.logoutBtnText}>Sign Out</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\screens\SmsLogsScreen.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  TextInput,
  ScrollView,
  RefreshControl,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Platform,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { COLORS } from '../constants/theme';

interface SmsLogItem {
  id: number;
  recipient_name: string;
  recipient_number: string;
  message_body: string;
  status: 'SENT' | 'PENDING' | 'FAILED';
  trigger_event?: string;
  category?: string;
  error_message?: string | null;
  retry_count?: number;
  created_at?: string;
  sent_at?: string | null;
}

interface SmsMetricsData {
  total_today: number;
  pending: number;
  sent_today: number;
  failed: number;
  modem_port: string;
  modem_baudrate: number;
}

const showAlert = (title: string, message: string) => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.alert(`${title}: ${message}`);
  } else {
    Alert.alert(title, message);
  }
};

export const SmsLogsScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const currentUser = user as Record<string, any> | null;
  const rawRole = String(currentUser?.role || currentUser?.system_role || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const canManageSms =
    Boolean(currentUser?.is_superuser) ||
    ['ADMIN', 'PRINCIPAL', 'DEPT_HEAD'].includes(rawRole);

  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<SmsLogItem[]>([]);
  const [metrics, setMetrics] = useState<SmsMetricsData | null>(null);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<'ALL' | 'SENT' | 'PENDING' | 'FAILED'>('ALL');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [actionBusy, setActionBusy] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const fetchSmsData = useCallback(async () => {
    const params: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (statusFilter !== 'ALL') {
      params.status = statusFilter;
    }
    if (searchQuery.trim()) {
      params.search = searchQuery.trim();
    }

    const [logsRes, metricsRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
      apiClient.get('/api/sms-outbox/', { params }),
      apiClient.get('/api/sms-outbox/metrics/'),
    ]);

    if (logsRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
      const payload = logsRes.value.data;
      const list = Array.isArray(payload?.results)
        ? payload.results
        : Array.isArray(payload)
        ? payload
        : [];
      setLogs(list);
    } else {
      setLogs([]);
    }

    if (metricsRes.status === 'fulfilled' && metricsRes.value.data) {
      setMetrics(metricsRes.value.data);
    }

    setLoading(false);
    setRefreshing(false);
  }, [statusFilter, searchQuery]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchSmsData();
  }, [fetchSmsData]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(() => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    fetchSmsData();
  }, [fetchSmsData]);

  const handleRetrySingle = async (id: number) => {
    if (!canManageSms) return;
    try {
      await apiClient.post(`/api/sms-outbox/${id}/retry/`);
      showAlert('Queued', 'Message has been re-queued for transmission.');
      fetchSmsData();
    } catch (err: any) {
      showAlert('Retry Failed', err?.response?.data?.error || 'Could not re-queue message.');
    }
  };

  const handleRetryAllFailed = async () => {
    if (!canManageSms) return;
    setActionBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.post('/api/sms-outbox/retry-all-failed/');
      showAlert('Retry Queued', `${res.data?.count ?? 0} failed message(s) re-queued.`);
      await fetchSmsData();
    } catch (err: any) {
      showAlert('Error', err?.response?.data?.error || 'Could not retry failed messages.');
    } finally {
      setActionBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const formatTimestamp = (iso?: string | null) => {
    if (!iso) return '';
    try {
      return new Date(iso).toLocaleString([], {
        month: 'short',
        day: 'numeric',
        hour: '2-digit',
        minute: '2-digit',
      });
    } catch {
      return String(iso);
    }
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top', 'left', 'right']}>
      {/* Top Header */}
      <View style={styles.topHeader}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <Text style={styles.screenTitle}>SMS Alerts</Text>
          <Text style={styles.screenSub}>Parent Attendance Notifications</Text>
        </View>

        {canManageSms && (metrics?.failed || 0) > 0 && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.retryAllBtn, actionBusy && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
            onPress={handleRetryAllFailed}
            disabled={actionBusy}
          >
            {actionBusy ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
            ) : (
              <>
                <Ionicons name="refresh" size={14} color="#ffffff" />
                <Text style={styles.retryAllBtnText}>Retry Failed</Text>
              </>
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Metrics Row */}
      <View style={styles.metricsRow}>
        <View style={[styles.metricCard, { backgroundColor: '#f0fdf4', borderColor: '#bbf7d0' }]}>
          <Text style={[styles.metricNumber, { color: '#16a34a' }]}>
            {metrics?.sent_today ?? 0}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.metricLabel}>Sent Today</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={[styles.metricCard, { backgroundColor: '#fffbeb', borderColor: '#fef3c7' }]}>
          <Text style={[styles.metricNumber, { color: '#d97706' }]}>
            {metrics?.pending ?? 0}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.metricLabel}>Pending</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={[styles.metricCard, { backgroundColor: '#fef2f2', borderColor: '#fecaca' }]}>
          <Text style={[styles.metricNumber, { color: '#dc2626' }]}>
            {metrics?.failed ?? 0}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.metricLabel}>Failed</Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Search & Filter Bar */}
      <View style={styles.filterSection}>
        <View style={styles.searchBox}>
          <Ionicons name="search-outline" size={16} color="#64748b" />
          <TextInput
            style={styles.searchInput}
            placeholder="Search parent name, phone, or text..."
            placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
          />
          {searchQuery.length > 0 && (
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSearchQuery('')}>
              <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={16} color="#94a3b8" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.statusChipsRow}>
          {(['ALL', 'SENT', 'PENDING', 'FAILED'] as const).map((st) => {
            const active = statusFilter === st;
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={st}
                style={[styles.statusChip, active && styles.statusChipActive]}
                onPress={() => setStatusFilter(st)}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.statusChipText, active && styles.statusChipTextActive]}>
                  {st}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Message Ledger */}
      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} colors={[COLORS.primary]} />
        }
      >
        {loading ? (
          <View style={styles.loadingBox}>
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={COLORS.primary} />
            <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Loading SMS logs...</Text>
          </View>
        ) : logs.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={styles.emptyCard}>
            <Ionicons name="chatbubble-ellipses-outline" size={36} color="#94a3b8" />
            <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>No Messages Found</Text>
            <Text style={styles.emptySub}>
              No automated SMS parent alerts match the active filter criteria.
            </Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          logs.map((item) => {
            const isSent = item.status === 'SENT';
            const isPending = item.status === 'PENDING';
            const isFailed = item.status === 'FAILED';

            return (
              <View key={String(item.id)} style={styles.logCard}>
                <View style={styles.logCardHeader}>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, marginRight: 8 }}>
                    <Text style={styles.recipientName}>{item.recipient_name || 'Parent / Guardian'}</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.recipientPhone}>{item.recipient_number}</Text>
                  </View>

                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.statusPill,
                      isSent && { backgroundColor: '#ecfdf5', borderColor: '#a7f3d0' },
                      isPending && { backgroundColor: '#fffbeb', borderColor: '#fde68a' },
                      isFailed && { backgroundColor: '#fef2f2', borderColor: '#fca5a5' },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.statusPillText,
                        isSent && { color: '#059669' },
                        isPending && { color: '#d97706' },
                        isFailed && { color: '#dc2626' },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {item.status}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                <Text style={styles.messageBody}>{item.message_body}</Text>

                <View style={styles.logCardFooter}>
                  <Text style={styles.timestampText}>
                    {formatTimestamp(item.sent_at || item.created_at)}
                  </Text>

                  {canManageSms && isFailed && (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      style={styles.singleRetryBtn}
                      onPress={() => handleRetrySingle(item.id)}
                    >
                      <Ionicons name="reload-outline" size={12} color="#dc2626" />
                      <Text style={styles.singleRetryBtnText}>Resend</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  )}
                </View>
              </View>
            );
          })
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#f8fafc' },
  topHeader: {
    flexDirection: 'row',

    paddingVertical: 12,

File: mobile\src\services\authService.ts

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\services\authService.ts

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { ENDPOINTS } from '../api/endpoints';

export interface UserAccount {
  id: number;
  username: string;
  email: string;
  full_name: string;
  employee_id?: string;
  role: string;
}

export interface AuthResponse {
  token: string;
  user: UserAccount;
}

const TOKEN_KEY = 'attendsure_auth_token';
const USER_KEY = 'attendsure_user_data';

const safeStorage = {
  async get(key: string): Promise<string | null> {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      try {
        return localStorage.getItem(key);
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    }
    return await SecureStore.getItemAsync(key);
  },

  async set(key: string, value: string): Promise<void> {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(key, value);
      } catch {}
      return;
    }
    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(key, value);
  },

  async delete(key: string): Promise<void> {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      try {
        localStorage.removeItem(key);
      } catch {}
      return;
    }
    await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(key);
  },
};

export const getStoredToken = async (): Promise<string | null> => {
  return await safeStorage.get(TOKEN_KEY);
};

export const storeAuthSession = async (token: string, user: UserAccount): Promise<void> => {
  await safeStorage.set(TOKEN_KEY, token);
  await safeStorage.set(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));
  apiClient.defaults.headers.common['Authorization'] = `Token ${token}`;
};

export const getStoredUser = async (): Promise<UserAccount | null> => {
  const raw = await safeStorage.get(USER_KEY);
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
};

export const clearAuthSession = async (): Promise<void> => {
  await safeStorage.delete(TOKEN_KEY);
  await safeStorage.delete(USER_KEY);
  delete apiClient.defaults.headers.common['Authorization'];
};

export const loginApi = async (username: string, password: string): Promise<AuthResponse> => {
  const response = await apiClient.post<AuthResponse>(ENDPOINTS.LOGIN, {
    username,
    password,
  });
  return response.data;
};

export const fetchCurrentProfile = async (): Promise<UserAccount> => {
  const response = await apiClient.get<UserAccount>(ENDPOINTS.CURRENT_USER);
  return response.data;
};

File: mobile\src\services\backgroundLocation.ts
========================================================
/**
 * AttendSure - 1-Minute Background & Sleep Mode GPS Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/backgroundLocation.ts
 */

import * as TaskManager from 'expo-task-manager';
import * as Location from 'expo-location';
import defaultApiClient, { apiClient as namedClient } from '../api/client';
import {
  getHardwareDeviceUuid,
  getRealDeviceModel,
  getRealBatteryPercentage,
  evaluateRealSignal,
} from './telemetryService';
import { enqueueOfflineHeartbeat, QueuedTelemetryPayload } from './offlineQueue';

const client = defaultApiClient || namedClient;
export const BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_TASK = 'ATTENDSURE_BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY';

TaskManager.defineTask(BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_TASK, async ({ data, error }: any) => {
  if (error || !data) return;

  const { locations } = data as { locations: Location.LocationObject[] };
  const latest = locations && locations.length > 0 ? locations[locations.length - 1] : null;

  if (!latest || !latest.coords) return;

  const lat = latest.coords.latitude;
  const lng = latest.coords.longitude;

  if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lng) || lat < -90 || lat > 90 || lng < -180 || lng > 180) {
    return;
  }

  const isMock = Boolean(latest.mocked);
  const accuracy = latest.coords.accuracy ?? null;

  try {
    const [deviceId, batteryLevel] = await Promise.all([
      getHardwareDeviceUuid(),
      getRealBatteryPercentage(),
    ]);

    const deviceModel = getRealDeviceModel();
    const signal = evaluateRealSignal(accuracy);

    await client.post(
      'geofence/checkin/',
      {
        latitude: lat,
        longitude: lng,
        accuracy: accuracy,
        accuracy_meters: accuracy,
        is_mock_location: isMock,
        battery_level: batteryLevel,
        device_id: deviceId,
        client_device_id: deviceId,
        mac_address: deviceId,
        device_model: deviceModel,
        signal_strength: signal.label,
        signal_percentage: signal.percentage,
      },
      { timeout: 8000 }
    );
  } catch (err: any) {
    if (err?.response?.status === 403) return;

    try {
      const [deviceId, batteryLevel] = await Promise.all([
        getHardwareDeviceUuid(),
        getRealBatteryPercentage(),
      ]);
      const deviceModel = getRealDeviceModel();
      const signal = evaluateRealSignal(accuracy);

      const offlinePayload: QueuedTelemetryPayload = {
        client_device_id: deviceId ?? '',
        latitude: lat,
        longitude: lng,
        accuracy: accuracy,
        battery_level: batteryLevel,
        is_mock_location: isMock,
        mac_address: deviceId,
        signal_strength: signal.label,
        signal_percentage: signal.percentage,
        device_model: deviceModel,
      };
      await enqueueOfflineHeartbeat(offlinePayload);
    } catch {}
  }
});

export async function startPassivePresenceTracking(): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const { status: fgStatus } = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
    if (fgStatus !== 'granted') return false;

    const { status: bgStatus } = await Location.requestBackgroundPermissionsAsync();
    if (bgStatus !== 'granted') return false;

    const isRegistered = await TaskManager.isTaskRegisteredAsync(BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_TASK);
    if (!isRegistered) {
      await Location.startLocationUpdatesAsync(BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_TASK, {
        accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High, // High accuracy satellite GPS
        timeInterval: 60000,              // 1 minute interval
        distanceInterval: 5,               // 5 meters moved
        deferredUpdatesInterval: 60000,
        pausesUpdatesAutomatically: false,
        showsBackgroundLocationIndicator: true,
        activityType: Location.ActivityType.Fitness,
        foregroundService: {
          notificationTitle: 'AttendSure Attendance Active',
          notificationBody: 'Location check-in active every minute.',
          notificationColor: '#004b87',
        },
      });
    }
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export async function stopPassivePresenceTracking(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const isRegistered = await TaskManager.isTaskRegisteredAsync(BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_TASK);
    if (isRegistered) {
      await Location.stopLocationUpdatesAsync(BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_TASK);
    }
  } catch {}
}

========================================================
File: mobile\src\services\configService.ts

/**
 * AttendSure - Mobile Configuration & Endpoint Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/configService.ts
 *
 * Automatic Multi-Network Connection:
 * 1. Parallel Multi-Server Race: Tests public tunnel and local servers at the same time.
 * 2. Works on Any Network: Automatically uses the tunnel on mobile cellular data and LAN on school Wi-Fi.
 * 3. Sanitized Paths: Strips duplicate /api paths and prevents SSRF protocol injection.
 * 4. Zero Mock Data: Connects directly to the live backend server.
 * 5. Simple English Terms: Clear error messages and connection logs.
 */

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

export const SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY = 'attendsure_server_base_url';
const CACHE_TTL_MS = 2 * 60 * 1000; // 2-minute cache allows quick adaptation when switching networks

// Backend API Endpoints (Normalized without leading /api so buildApiUrl handles it safely)
export const ENDPOINTS = {
  auth: {
    login: '/auth/login/',
    logout: '/auth/logout/',
    userProfile: '/me/',
  },
  classroom: {
    studentList: '/classroom/student_list/',
    classList: '/classroom/class_list/',
    takeAttendance: '/classroom/batch-scan/',
  },
  schoolGate: {
    tap: '/gate/scan/',
    logs: '/gate/logs/',
    verify: '/gate/verify/',
  },
  telemetry: {
    heartbeat: '/geofence/telemetry/',
    geofenceStatus: '/geofence/',
  },
  reports: {
    dtr: '/dtr/',
    sf1: (sectionId: number | string) => `/reports/sf1/${sectionId}/`,
    sf2: (sectionId: number | string) => `/reports/sf2/${sectionId}/`,
    sf4: '/reports/sf4/',
  },
};

// 1. URL Sanitizer: Enforces valid HTTP/HTTPS and strips duplicate /api and trailing slashes
export function cleanUrl(rawUrl?: string | null): string {
  if (!rawUrl || typeof rawUrl !== 'string') {
    return Platform.OS === 'android' ? 'http://10.0.2.2:8000' : 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';
  }
  let trimmed = rawUrl.trim();

  // Strip trailing slashes
  while (trimmed.endsWith('/')) {
    trimmed = trimmed.slice(0, -1);
  }

  // Prevent double /api/api pathing
  if (trimmed.toLowerCase().endsWith('/api')) {
    trimmed = trimmed.slice(0, -4);
  }

  while (trimmed.endsWith('/')) {
    trimmed = trimmed.slice(0, -1);
  }

  if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(trimmed)) {
    trimmed = `http://${trimmed}`;
  }

  return trimmed;
}

export const sanitizeUrl = cleanUrl;

// 2. Protocol Validation (Guards against SSRF and script injection)
export function isValidServerUrl(url?: string | null): boolean {
  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return false;
  const trimmed = url.trim();

  try {
    const parsed = new URL(trimmed.startsWith('http') ? trimmed : `http://${trimmed}`);
    return parsed.protocol === 'http:' || parsed.protocol === 'https:';
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

// 3. Dynamically extract the laptop's IP address from Expo Metro
function getMetroHostIp(): string | null {
  try {
    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-var-requires
    const Constants = require('expo-constants').default;
    const hostUri =
      Constants?.expoConfig?.hostUri ||
      Constants?.manifest?.debuggerHost ||
      Constants?.manifest2?.extra?.expoGo?.debuggerHost;

    if (hostUri && typeof hostUri === 'string') {
      const ip = hostUri.split(':')[0];
      if (ip && ip !== 'localhost' && ip !== '127.0.0.1' && !ip.includes('expo.app')) {
        return ip;
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // optional fallback
  }
  return null;
}

// 4. Safely load candidate endpoints from endpoints.json
function loadRootEndpoints(): { active_tunnel_url?: string; lan_servers?: string[]; production_domain?: string } {
  try {
    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-var-requires
    return require('../../../endpoints.json');
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

// 5. Gather all possible candidate servers (Works across cellular data, Wi-Fi, and tunnels)
export function getCandidateServers(): string[] {
  const candidates: string[] = [];
  const rootEndpoints = loadRootEndpoints();

  // Primary: Public Cloudflare Tunnel (works on any mobile network, cellular data, or foreign Wi-Fi)
  if (rootEndpoints.active_tunnel_url) {
    candidates.push(cleanUrl(rootEndpoints.active_tunnel_url));
  }

  // Secondary: Production school domain
  if (rootEndpoints.production_domain) {
    candidates.push(cleanUrl(rootEndpoints.production_domain));
  }

  // Tertiary: Environment variable from mobile/.env
  const envUrl = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL;
  if (envUrl && typeof envUrl === 'string' && envUrl.trim().length > 0) {
    candidates.push(cleanUrl(envUrl));
  }

  // Quaternary: Dynamic laptop IP address detected through Metro bundler (school Wi-Fi)
  const metroIp = getMetroHostIp();
  if (metroIp) {
    candidates.push(cleanUrl(`http://${metroIp}:8000`));
  }

  // Quaternary: Local Area Network server addresses from endpoints.json
  if (Array.isArray(rootEndpoints.lan_servers)) {
    for (const lan of rootEndpoints.lan_servers) {
      if (lan && typeof lan === 'string') {
        candidates.push(cleanUrl(lan));
      }
    }
  }

  // Emulator fallbacks (last resort)
  if (Platform.OS === 'android') {
    candidates.push('http://10.0.2.2:8000');
  } else {
    candidates.push('http://127.0.0.1:8000');
  }

  return Array.from(new Set(candidates.filter((u) => Boolean(u && isValidServerUrl(u)))));
}

export function getDefaultBaseUrl(): string {
  const candidates = getCandidateServers();
  return candidates[0] || (Platform.OS === 'android' ? 'http://10.0.2.2:8000' : 'http://127.0.0.1:8000');
}

export const DEFAULT_SERVER_URL = getDefaultBaseUrl();

let cachedUrl: string | null = null;
let lastCacheTime = 0;

export function invalidateServerCache(): void {
  cachedUrl = null;
  lastCacheTime = 0;
}

// 6. Fast Parallel Probe: races public and local addresses; fastest responsive connection wins
export async function resolveFastestServer(): Promise<string> {
  const candidates = getCandidateServers();

  if (candidates.length === 0) {
    const fallback = getDefaultBaseUrl();
    cachedUrl = fallback;
    return fallback;
  }

  const probeServer = async (targetUrl: string): Promise<string> => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 2200); // 2.2s per probe

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${targetUrl}/api/settings/school/`, {
        method: 'GET',
        headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);

      // Status 200, 401, or 403 proves the Django backend is actively listening
      if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 500) {
        return targetUrl;
      }
      throw new Error(`Probe failed with status ${res.status}`);
    } catch (err) {
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      throw err;
    }
  };

  try {
    const fastestUrl = await Promise.any(candidates.map((url) => probeServer(url)));
    cachedUrl = fastestUrl;
    lastCacheTime = Date.now();
    AsyncStorage.setItem(SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY, fastestUrl).catch(() => {});
    return fastestUrl;
  } catch {
    // If all concurrent probes fail, fall back to last known or primary candidate
    const stored = await AsyncStorage.getItem(SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY).catch(() => null);
    const fallback = (stored && isValidServerUrl(stored)) ? cleanUrl(stored) : candidates[0];
    cachedUrl = fallback;
    return fallback;
  }
}

/**
 * Automatically provides the active server address.
 * Re-probes if the network changed or the cache expired.
 */
export async function getServerBaseUrl(): Promise<string> {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (cachedUrl && now - lastCacheTime < CACHE_TTL_MS) {
    return cachedUrl;
  }

  return resolveFastestServer();
}

export async function setServerBaseUrl(newUrl: string): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!isValidServerUrl(newUrl)) {
    throw new Error('Please enter a valid web address starting with http:// or https://');
  }

  const cleaned = cleanUrl(newUrl);
  try {
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY, cleaned);
    cachedUrl = cleaned;
    lastCacheTime = Date.now();
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Failed to save server address:', err);
    return false;
  }
}

export async function resetServerBaseUrl(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await AsyncStorage.removeItem(SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY);
  } catch {}
  cachedUrl = null;
  lastCacheTime = 0;
}

/**
 * Builds a clean, absolute API URL without duplicated slashes or double /api
 */
export async function buildApiUrl(endpoint: string): Promise<string> {
  const base = await getServerBaseUrl();
  const cleanEndpoint = endpoint.startsWith('/') ? endpoint : `/${endpoint}`;
  return `${base}/api${cleanEndpoint}`;
}

export async function testServerConnection(
  testUrl?: string
): Promise<{ ok: boolean; message: string; pingMs: number }> {
  const target = cleanUrl(testUrl || cachedUrl || getDefaultBaseUrl());
  const startTime = Date.now();

  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3000);

    const response = await fetch(`${target}/api/settings/school/`, {
      method: 'GET',
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    const pingMs = Date.now() - startTime;
    if (response.status >= 200 && response.status < 500) {
      return { ok: true, message: `Connected to school server (${pingMs}ms)`, pingMs };
    }
    return { ok: false, message: `Server replied with status ${response.status}`, pingMs };
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const pingMs = Date.now() - startTime;
    const isAbort = err && typeof err === 'object' && 'name' in err && err.name === 'AbortError';
    if (isAbort) {
      return { ok: false, message: 'Connection timed out. Checking alternative route...', pingMs };
    }
    return { ok: false, message: 'Cannot reach server. Verify network connection.', pingMs };
  }
}

export default {
  getServerBaseUrl,
  setServerBaseUrl,
  resetServerBaseUrl,
  resolveFastestServer,
  invalidateServerCache,
  testServerConnection,
  buildApiUrl,
  isValidServerUrl,
  cleanUrl,
  sanitizeUrl,
  getDefaultBaseUrl,
  SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY,
  DEFAULT_SERVER_URL,
  ENDPOINTS,
};

/**
 * AttendSure V3 - Hardware Device Identity Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/deviceService.ts
 *
 * Cybersecurity & Anti-Cheating:
 * 1. Cryptographic Entropy: Generates unguessable device IDs using expo-crypto.
 * 2. Hardware Enclave & Keystore: Stores IDs using AES-256 encrypted SecureStore.
 * 3. In-Memory Caching: Speeds up repeated lookups without disk latency.
 * 4. Decoupled Architecture: Completely breaks require cycles between screens and background tasks.
 */

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import * as Crypto from 'expo-crypto';

export const SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY = 'attendsure_hardware_device_uuid';

// In-memory cache to prevent repeated storage disk reads
let cachedDeviceUuid: string | null = null;

/**
 * Generates or retrieves the hardware-encrypted device UUID.
 */
export const getOrCreateDeviceUuid = async (): Promise<string> => {
  if (cachedDeviceUuid) {
    return cachedDeviceUuid;
  }

  try {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      const stored = typeof localStorage !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY) : null;
      if (stored) {
        cachedDeviceUuid = stored;
        return stored;
      }
      const webUuid = `DEV-WEB-${Crypto.randomUUID ? Crypto.randomUUID().substring(0, 13).toUpperCase() : Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 10).toUpperCase()}`;
      if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
        localStorage.setItem(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY, webUuid);
      }
      cachedDeviceUuid = webUuid;
      return webUuid;
    }

    const existing = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY);
    if (existing) {
      cachedDeviceUuid = existing;
      return existing;
    }

    // Generate secure random entropy for hardware binding
    const randomBytes = await Crypto.getRandomBytesAsync(8);
    const hex = Array.from(randomBytes)
      .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
      .join('')
      .toUpperCase();

    const generated = `DEV-${Platform.OS.toUpperCase()}-${hex.substring(0, 8)}-${hex.substring(8, 16)}`;
    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY, generated, {
      keychainAccessible: SecureStore.ALWAYS_THIS_DEVICE_ONLY,
    });

    cachedDeviceUuid = generated;
    return generated;
  } catch (error) {
    const fallback = `DEV-${Platform.OS.toUpperCase()}-TEMP-${Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase()}`;
    cachedDeviceUuid = fallback;
    return fallback;
  }
};

File: mobile\src\services\offlineQueue.ts
