/**
 * AttendSure - Offline Storage & Sync Queue Engine
 * File: mobile/src/services/offlineQueue.ts
 */

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';

const OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY = 'attendsure_offline_queue';

export interface QueuedTelemetryPayload {
  client_device_id?: string | null;
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
  accuracy?: number | null;
  battery_level?: number | null;
  is_mock_location?: boolean;
  ip_address?: string | null;
  mac_address?: string | null;
  signal_strength?: string | null;
  signal_percentage?: number | null;
  device_model?: string | null;
}

export interface OfflineQueueItem {
  id: string;
  endpoint: string;
  method: 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH';
  payload: any;
  timestamp: number;
  retryCount?: number;
}

/**
 * Returns the total number of records waiting to be uploaded.
 */
export async function getUnsyncedCount(): Promise<number> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return 0;
    const items: OfflineQueueItem[] = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(items) ? items.length : 0;
  } catch {
    return 0;
  }
}

/**
 * Queues an offline telemetry heartbeat with automatic deduplication.
 * Replaces older heartbeats so breadcrumbs do not stack up during long offline periods.
 */
export async function enqueueOfflineHeartbeat(payload: QueuedTelemetryPayload): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY);
    let queue: OfflineQueueItem[] = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];

    // Remove any previous pending heartbeat to keep only the newest location
    queue = queue.filter(
      (item) => !item.endpoint.includes('checkin') && !item.endpoint.includes('heartbeat')
    );

    queue.push({
      id: `hb_${Date.now()}`,
      endpoint: 'geofence/checkin/',
      method: 'POST',
      payload: {
        ...payload,
        device_id: payload.client_device_id ?? null,
        accuracy_meters: payload.accuracy ?? null,
      },
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      retryCount: 0,
    });

    await AsyncStorage.setItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify(queue));
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[OfflineQueue] Save notice:', err);
  }
}

/**
 * Generic queue action for class roll calls and gate logs.
 */
export async function queueOfflineAction(
  endpoint: string,
  payload: any,
  method: 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH' = 'POST'
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY);
    let queue: OfflineQueueItem[] = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];

    if (endpoint.includes('checkin') || endpoint.includes('heartbeat')) {
      queue = queue.filter(
        (item) => !item.endpoint.includes('checkin') && !item.endpoint.includes('heartbeat')
      );
    }

    queue.push({
      id: `${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`,
      endpoint,
      method,
      payload,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      retryCount: 0,
    });

    await AsyncStorage.setItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify(queue));
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[OfflineQueue] Save notice:', err);
  }
}

/**
 * Uploads all queued offline records to the school server.
 */
export async function syncOfflineQueue(): Promise<{ synced: number; remaining: number }> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return { synced: 0, remaining: 0 };

    const queue: OfflineQueueItem[] = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!Array.isArray(queue) || queue.length === 0) {
      return { synced: 0, remaining: 0 };
    }

    const remaining: OfflineQueueItem[] = [];
    let synced = 0;

    for (const item of queue) {
      // Discard location pings older than 24 hours
      if (
        (item.endpoint.includes('checkin') || item.endpoint.includes('heartbeat')) &&
        Date.now() - item.timestamp > 86400000
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      try {
        if (item.method === 'PUT') {
          await apiClient.put(item.endpoint, item.payload, { timeout: 7000 });
        } else {
          await apiClient.post(item.endpoint, item.payload, { timeout: 7000 });
        }
        synced += 1;
      } catch (err: any) {
        const status = err?.response?.status;
        // If server rejected the record (bad payload or 403 lock), remove it from queue
        if (status === 400 || status === 403 || status === 409) {
          continue;
        }

        // Retry temporary network errors up to 8 times
        item.retryCount = (item.retryCount || 0) + 1;
        if ((item.retryCount || 0) < 8) {
          remaining.push(item);
        }
      }
    }

    await AsyncStorage.setItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify(remaining));
    return { synced, remaining: remaining.length };
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[OfflineQueue] Sync notice:', err);
    return { synced: 0, remaining: await getUnsyncedCount() };
  }
}

/**
 * Clears the offline queue.
 */
export async function clearOfflineQueue(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await AsyncStorage.removeItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY);
  } catch {}
}

File: mobile\src\services\pushService.ts

/**
 * AttendSure V3 - Safe Push & Wake-Up Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/pushService.ts
 *
 * Cybersecurity & Performance:
 * 1. Expo Go Detection: Safely skips remote push registration in the Expo Go sandbox to eliminate SDK 53 console warnings.
 * 2. Standalone APK Ready: Enables high-priority wake-up push notifications in production builds.
 * 3. LogBox Cleanliness: Filters known sandbox notices from polluting the terminal.
 */

import { Platform, LogBox } from 'react-native';
import Constants, { ExecutionEnvironment } from 'expo-constants';
import defaultApiClient, { apiClient as namedClient } from '../api/client';
import {
  startPassivePresenceTracking,
  stopPassivePresenceTracking,
} from './backgroundLocation';

// Safely resolve HTTP client
const client = defaultApiClient || namedClient;

export const BACKGROUND_NOTIFICATION_TASK = 'ATTENDSURE_BACKGROUND_PUSH_TASK';

// Ignore the Expo Go sandbox push warning in development
LogBox.ignoreLogs([
  '[expo-notifications] Push notifications (remote notifications) are unavailable in Expo Go',
]);

// Check if running inside Expo Go sandbox
const isRunningInExpoGo =
  Constants.executionEnvironment === ExecutionEnvironment.StoreClient;

let Notifications: any = null;
let TaskManager: any = null;

// Only initialize remote push listeners if NOT in Expo Go sandbox
if (!isRunningInExpoGo) {
  try {
    Notifications = require('expo-notifications');
    TaskManager = require('expo-task-manager');

    if (Notifications?.setNotificationHandler) {
      Notifications.setNotificationHandler({
        handleNotification: async () => ({
          shouldShowAlert: false,
          shouldPlaySound: false,
          shouldSetBadge: false,
          shouldShowBanner: false,
          shouldShowList: false,
        }),
      });
    }

    if (TaskManager?.defineTask) {
      TaskManager.defineTask(BACKGROUND_NOTIFICATION_TASK, async ({ data, error }: any) => {
        if (error) return;
        const action = data?.notification?.data?.action?.toUpperCase();
        if (action === 'START_TRACKING_IN') {
          await startPassivePresenceTracking();
        } else if (action === 'STOP_TRACKING_OUT') {
          await stopPassivePresenceTracking();
        }
      });

      Notifications.registerTaskAsync(BACKGROUND_NOTIFICATION_TASK).catch(() => {});
    }
  } catch (err) {
    // Suppress if native dependencies are absent
  }
}

/**
 * Registers device push token with the backend.
 * Safely returns null in Expo Go and uses background GPS telemetry instead.
 */
export async function registerPushTokenWithBackend(): Promise<string | null> {
  if (isRunningInExpoGo || !Notifications?.getExpoPushTokenAsync) {
    console.log('[PushService] Operating in Expo Go: using automated background GPS telemetry.');
    return null;
  }

  try {
    const { status: existingStatus } = await Notifications.getPermissionsAsync();
    let finalStatus = existingStatus;

    if (existingStatus !== 'granted') {
      const { status } = await Notifications.requestPermissionsAsync();
      finalStatus = status;
    }

    if (finalStatus !== 'granted') {
      return null;
    }

    const pushTokenData = await Notifications.getExpoPushTokenAsync();
    const token = pushTokenData?.data;

    if (!token) return null;

    const targetUrl = client?.defaults?.baseURL?.endsWith('/api')
      ? '/users/register-push-token/'
      : '/api/users/register-push-token/';

    await client.post(targetUrl, {
      push_token: token,
      platform: Platform.OS,
    });

    console.log('[PushService] Production push token bound successfully.');
    return token;
  } catch (err: any) {
    return null;
  }
}

// mobile/src/services/soundService.ts

import { Vibration, Platform } from 'react-native';

let AudioModule: any = null;
let FileSystemModule: any = null;
let isAudioAvailable = false;

try {
  const expoAv = require('expo-av');
  if (expoAv && expoAv.Audio) {
    AudioModule = expoAv.Audio;
    isAudioAvailable = true;
  }
} catch {
  isAudioAvailable = false;
}

try {
  FileSystemModule = require('expo-file-system');
} catch {
  FileSystemModule = null;
}

let isAudioConfigured = false;

const configureAudio = async () => {
  if (!isAudioAvailable || !AudioModule || isAudioConfigured) return;
  try {
    await AudioModule.setAudioModeAsync({
      playsInSilentModeIOS: true,
      staysActiveInBackground: false,
      shouldDuckAndroid: true,
      playThroughEarpieceAndroid: false, // Forces audio to external speaker
    });
    isAudioConfigured = true;
  } catch {
    // Ignore audio config error
  }
};

// Reliable CDN Fallback Audio for Android MediaPlayer
const SOUND_URLS = {
  success: 'https://actions.google.com/sounds/v1/communication/beep_short.ogg',
  warning: 'https://actions.google.com/sounds/v1/alarms/alarm_clock.ogg',
  error: 'https://actions.google.com/sounds/v1/cartoon/cartoon_boing.ogg',
};

// Base64 WAV buffers for offline local caching
const BASE64_TONES = {
  success:
    'UklGRjQBAABXQVZFZm10IBAAAAABAAEAQB8AAEAfAAABAAgAZGF0YRABAACAgICAgICAgICAgICAf39/f3+AgICAgIB/f39/f4CAgICAgH9/f39/gICAgICAf39/f3+AgICAgIB/f39/f4CAgICAgH9/f39/gICAgICAf39/f3+AgICAgIB/f39/f4CAgICAgH9/f39/gICAgICAf39/f3+AgICAgIB/f39/f4CAgICAgH9/f39/gICAgICA',
  warning:
    'UklGRjQBAABXQVZFZm10IBAAAAABAAEAQB8AAEAfAAABAAgAZGF0YRABAADAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMC/v7+/v8DAwMDAwL+/v7+/wMDAwMDAv7+/v7/AwMDAwMC/v7+/v8DAwMDAwL+/v7+/wMDAwMDAv7+/v7/AwMDAwMC/v7+/v8DAwMDAwL+/v7+/wMDAwMDAv7+/v7/AwMDAwMC/v7+/v8DAwMDAwL+/v7+/wMDAwMDAv7+/v7/AwMDAwMA',
  error:
    'UklGRjQBAABXQVZFZm10IBAAAAABAAEAQB8AAEAfAAABAAgAZGF0YRABAADg4ODg4ODg4ODg4ODg39/f39/g4ODg4ODf39/f3+Dg4ODg4N/f39/f4ODg4ODg39/f39/g4ODg4ODf39/f3+Dg4ODg4N/f39/f4ODg4ODg39/f39/g4ODg4ODf39/f3+Dg4ODg4N/f39/f4ODg4ODg39/f39/g4ODg4ODf39/f3+Dg4ODg4N/f39/f4ODg4ODg',
};

const getPlayableUri = async (type: 'success' | 'warning' | 'error'): Promise<string> => {
  if (FileSystemModule && FileSystemModule.cacheDirectory) {
    try {
      const fileUri = `${FileSystemModule.cacheDirectory}attendsure_${type}.wav`;
      const fileInfo = await FileSystemModule.getInfoAsync(fileUri);
      if (!fileInfo.exists) {
        await FileSystemModule.writeAsStringAsync(fileUri, BASE64_TONES[type], {
          encoding: FileSystemModule.EncodingType.Base64,
        });
      }
      return fileUri;
    } catch {
      // Fallback to URL
    }
  }
  return SOUND_URLS[type];
};

const playSoundSafely = async (
  type: 'success' | 'warning' | 'error',
  vibratePattern: number | number[]
) => {
  // Always trigger vibration as tactile backup
  Vibration.vibrate(vibratePattern);

  if (!isAudioAvailable || !AudioModule) return;

  try {
    await configureAudio();
    const sourceUri = await getPlayableUri(type);
    const { sound } = await AudioModule.Sound.createAsync(
      { uri: sourceUri },
      { shouldPlay: true, volume: 1.0 }
    );
    sound.setOnPlaybackStatusUpdate((status: any) => {
      if (status.isLoaded && status.didJustFinish) {
        sound.unloadAsync();
      }
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn(`[Audio Notice] Sound playback skipped: ${err}`);
  }
};

export const playScanSuccessSound = async () => {
  await playSoundSafely('success', 70);
};

export const playScanWarningSound = async () => {
  await playSoundSafely('warning', [0, 150, 80, 150]);
};

export const playScanErrorSound = async () => {
  await playSoundSafely('error', [0, 200, 100, 200]);
};

/**
 * AttendSure - Zero-Mock Foreground Telemetry Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/telemetryService.ts
 */

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import * as Location from 'expo-location';
import * as Battery from 'expo-battery';
import * as Application from 'expo-application';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { enqueueOfflineHeartbeat, QueuedTelemetryPayload } from './offlineQueue';

const DEVICE_UUID_STORAGE_KEY = 'attendsure_hardware_device_uuid';
let lastSentTimestamp = 0;
const ONE_MINUTE_MS = 60000;

export interface HeartbeatResponse {
  status?: string;
  device_bound?: boolean;
  distance?: string;
  distance_meters?: number;
  verified_inside?: boolean;
  is_inside_perimeter?: boolean;
  server_time?: string;
  isQueuedOffline?: boolean;
}

export interface CoordinatesPayload {
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
  accuracy?: number | null;
  mocked?: boolean;
  timestamp?: string;
}

export const getHardwareDeviceUuid = async (): Promise<string | null> => {
  try {
    const existing = await AsyncStorage.getItem(DEVICE_UUID_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (existing && existing.trim().length > 0) {
      return existing.trim();
    }

    let realHardwareId: string | null = null;
    if (Platform.OS === 'android') {
      realHardwareId = Application.getAndroidId();
    } else if (Platform.OS === 'ios') {
      realHardwareId = await Application.getIosIdForVendorAsync();
    }

    if (realHardwareId && realHardwareId.trim().length > 0) {
      const cleanId = `DEV-${Platform.OS.toUpperCase()}-${realHardwareId.trim().toUpperCase()}`;
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(DEVICE_UUID_STORAGE_KEY, cleanId);
      return cleanId;
    }
  } catch {}
  return null;
};

export const getOrCreateDeviceUuid = getHardwareDeviceUuid;

export const getRealDeviceModel = (): string | null => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'android') {
    const constants = Platform.constants as Record<string, any>;
    const brand = constants?.Brand || constants?.brand || '';
    const model = constants?.Model || constants?.model || '';
    const full = `${brand} ${model}`.trim();
    return full.length > 0 ? full : null;
  }
  if (Platform.OS === 'ios') {
    return 'Apple iOS Device';
  }
  return null;
};

export const getRealBatteryPercentage = async (): Promise<number | null> => {
  try {
    const level = await Battery.getBatteryLevelAsync();
    if (typeof level === 'number' && level >= 0 && !isNaN(level)) {
      return Math.round(level * 100);
    }
  } catch {}
  return null;
};

export const evaluateRealSignal = (
  accuracyMeters: number | null | undefined
): { label: string | null; percentage: number | null } => {
  if (accuracyMeters === null || accuracyMeters === undefined || accuracyMeters < 0 || isNaN(accuracyMeters)) {
    return { label: null, percentage: null };
  }
  if (accuracyMeters <= 15) {
    return { label: 'Strong', percentage: 95 };
  } else if (accuracyMeters <= 30) {
    return { label: 'Good', percentage: 75 };
  } else if (accuracyMeters <= 60) {
    return { label: 'Fair', percentage: 50 };
  } else {
    return { label: 'Weak', percentage: 25 };
  }
};

export const format12HourTime = (date: Date = new Date()): string => {
  let hours = date.getHours();
  const minutes = String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
  const seconds = String(date.getSeconds()).padStart(2, '0');
  const ampm = hours >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
  hours = hours % 12;
  hours = hours ? hours : 12;
  const strHours = String(hours).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${strHours}:${minutes}:${seconds} ${ampm}`;
};

export const sendLiveHeartbeat = async (
  coordsOrIdentifier?: CoordinatesPayload | string
): Promise<HeartbeatResponse> => {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - lastSentTimestamp < ONE_MINUTE_MS && lastSentTimestamp !== 0) {
    return { status: 'THROTTLED' };
  }

  let lat: number | undefined;
  let lng: number | undefined;
  let acc: number | null = null;
  let isMock = false;

  if (coordsOrIdentifier && typeof coordsOrIdentifier === 'object') {
    lat = coordsOrIdentifier.latitude;
    lng = coordsOrIdentifier.longitude;
    acc = coordsOrIdentifier.accuracy ?? null;
    isMock = Boolean(coordsOrIdentifier.mocked);
  }

  if (lat === undefined || lng === undefined) {
    const { status } = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
    if (status !== 'granted') {
      throw new Error('Location permission is required.');
    }
    const loc = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({ accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High });
    lat = loc.coords.latitude;
    lng = loc.coords.longitude;
    acc = loc.coords.accuracy ?? null;
    isMock = Boolean(loc.mocked);
  }

  if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lng) || lat < -90 || lat > 90 || lng < -180 || lng > 180) {
    throw new Error('Invalid satellite coordinates.');
  }

  const [hardwareUuid, batteryLevel] = await Promise.all([
    getHardwareDeviceUuid(),
    getRealBatteryPercentage(),
  ]);

  const deviceModel = getRealDeviceModel();
  const signal = evaluateRealSignal(acc);

  const payload: QueuedTelemetryPayload = {
    client_device_id: hardwareUuid,
    latitude: lat,
    longitude: lng,
    accuracy: acc,
    battery_level: batteryLevel,
    is_mock_location: isMock,
    mac_address: hardwareUuid,
    signal_strength: signal.label,
    signal_percentage: signal.percentage,
    device_model: deviceModel,
  };

  try {
    const res = await apiClient.post(
      'geofence/checkin/',
      {
        client_device_id: hardwareUuid,
        device_id: hardwareUuid,
        mac_address: hardwareUuid,
        device_model: deviceModel,
        latitude: lat,
        longitude: lng,
        accuracy: acc,
        accuracy_meters: acc,
        battery_level: batteryLevel,
        is_mock_location: isMock,
        signal_strength: signal.label,
        signal_percentage: signal.percentage,
      },
      { timeout: 8000 }
    );

    lastSentTimestamp = now;
    return {
      ...res.data,
      isQueuedOffline: false,
    };
  } catch (err: any) {
    const httpStatus = err?.response?.status;
    const msg = err?.response?.data?.error || err?.message;

    if (httpStatus === 403) {
      throw new Error(msg || 'Device lock mismatch or mock location detected.');
    }

    await enqueueOfflineHeartbeat(payload);
    return {
      isQueuedOffline: true,
    };
  }
};

/**
 * AttendSure - Buffer Polyfill Shim
 * File: mobile/src/shims/buffer.js
 */

let BufferClass;

try {
  BufferClass = require('buffer/').Buffer;
} catch (e1) {
  try {
    BufferClass = require('buffer').Buffer;
  } catch (e2) {
    BufferClass = global.Buffer;
  }
}

if (!BufferClass) {
  BufferClass = class Buffer {
    constructor(arg) {
      this.data = arg;
    }
    static from(data) {
      return {
        toString: () => String(data),
      };
    }
    static isBuffer() {
      return false;
    }
  };
}

if (typeof global !== 'undefined' && !global.Buffer) {
  global.Buffer = BufferClass;
}

module.exports = {
  Buffer: BufferClass,
  default: { Buffer: BufferClass },
};

/**
 * AttendSure - React Native Internal Resolution Shim
 * File: mobile/src/shims/unstable-internals.js
 *
 * Resolves AssetSourceResolver for Expo Asset compatibility.
 */

let AssetSourceResolver;

try {
  AssetSourceResolver = require('react-native/Libraries/Image/AssetSourceResolver');
} catch (e1) {
  try {
    AssetSourceResolver = require('@react-native/assets-registry/AssetSourceResolver');
  } catch (e2) {
    AssetSourceResolver = class AssetSourceResolver {
      constructor(serverUrl, jsbundleUrl, asset) {
        this.serverUrl = serverUrl;
        this.jsbundleUrl = jsbundleUrl;
        this.asset = asset;
      }
      isLoadedFromServer() {
        return Boolean(this.serverUrl);
      }
      isLoadedFromFileSystem() {
        return Boolean(this.jsbundleUrl && this.jsbundleUrl.startsWith('file://'));
      }
      defaultAsset() {
        return this.asset;
      }
      scaledAssetURL(scale) {
        return this.asset;
      }
    };
  }
}

let resolveAssetSource;
try {
  resolveAssetSource = require('react-native/Libraries/Image/resolveAssetSource');
} catch (e) {
  resolveAssetSource = (source) => source;
}

const ResolvedResolver = AssetSourceResolver.default || AssetSourceResolver;
const ResolvedSource = resolveAssetSource.default || resolveAssetSource;

module.exports = {
  __esModule: true,
  default: {
    AssetSourceResolver: ResolvedResolver,
    resolveAssetSource: ResolvedSource,
  },
  AssetSourceResolver: ResolvedResolver,
  resolveAssetSource: ResolvedSource,
};

/**
 * Safely parses Django ISO, UTC, or SQLite date strings into human-readable 12-hour time.
 */
export function formatScanTime(rawDate?: string | null): string {
  if (!rawDate) return '--:--';

  try {
    // Replace space with 'T' to standardize SQLite timestamps: 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss' -> ISO
    const standardized = rawDate.includes('T') ? rawDate : rawDate.replace(' ', 'T');
    const parsed = new Date(standardized);

    if (!isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
      return parsed.toLocaleTimeString([], {
        hour: '2-digit',
        minute: '2-digit',
        hour12: true,
      });
    }

    // Fallback regex if phone runtime timezone fails to parse
    const timeMatch = rawDate.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
    if (timeMatch) {
      const hours = parseInt(timeMatch[1], 10);
      const minutes = timeMatch[2];
      const ampm = hours >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
      const formattedHours = hours % 12 || 12;
      return `${formattedHours}:${minutes} ${ampm}`;
    }
  } catch {
    // Return empty fallback instead of crash
  }

  return '--:--';
}

/**
 * Formats distance with appropriate units (meters vs kilometers).
 */
export function formatDistance(meters: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (meters === null || meters === undefined || isNaN(meters)) return 'Determining distance...';
  if (meters < 1000) {
    return `${Math.round(meters)} m`;
  }
  return `${(meters / 1000).toFixed(2)} km`;
}

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\App.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { View, StyleSheet, Platform } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaProvider } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { NavigationContainer } from '@react-navigation/native';
import * as NativeSplashScreen from 'expo-splash-screen';

import { AuthProvider } from './src/context/AuthContext';
import AppNavigator from './src/navigation/AppNavigator';
import { SplashScreen } from './src/components/SplashScreen';
import { resolveFastestServer } from './src/services/configService';

// Hold native splash screen so the app doesn't flash white before JS runs
if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
  NativeSplashScreen.preventAutoHideAsync().catch(() => {});
}

export default function App() {
  const [showSplash, setShowSplash] = useState<boolean>(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Faster Data Fetching: Pre-warm backend connection while radar animation plays
    resolveFastestServer().catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  const handleSplashFinish = () => {
    setShowSplash(false);
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaProvider>
      <View style={styles.container}>
        <AuthProvider>
          {showSplash ? (
            <SplashScreen onFinish={handleSplashFinish} />
          ) : (
            <NavigationContainer>
              <AppNavigator />
            </NavigationContainer>
          )}
        </AuthProvider>
      </View>
    </SafeAreaProvider>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
  },
});

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\metro.config.js

const path = require('path');
const { getDefaultConfig } = require('expo/metro-config');

const config = getDefaultConfig(__dirname);

// 1. Enable static SVG asset resolution for expo-image
const { assetExts, sourceExts } = config.resolver;
if (!assetExts.includes('svg')) {
  config.resolver.assetExts = [...assetExts, 'svg'];
}
config.resolver.sourceExts = sourceExts.filter((ext) => ext !== 'svg');

// 2. Custom module resolution shims
config.resolver.resolveRequest = (context, moduleName, platform) => {
  // 1. Safe web resolution for expo-splash-screen
  if (platform === 'web' && moduleName === 'expo-splash-screen') {
    return {
      filePath: path.resolve(__dirname, 'src/shims/splash-screen.web.js'),
      type: 'sourceFile',
    };
  }

  // 2. Resolve 'buffer' for react-native-svg & QR code generation
  if (moduleName === 'buffer') {
    return {
      filePath: path.resolve(__dirname, 'src/shims/buffer.js'),
      type: 'sourceFile',
    };
  }

  // 3. Resolve React Native internal exports for Expo Asset on Android / iOS
  if (moduleName.includes('unstable-internals-do-not-use')) {
    return {
      filePath: path.resolve(__dirname, 'src/shims/unstable-internals.js'),
      type: 'sourceFile',
    };
  }

  // 4. Resolve AssetRegistry for web platform
  if (platform === 'web' && moduleName.includes('AssetRegistry')) {
    try {
      return {
        filePath: require.resolve('react-native-web/dist/modules/AssetRegistry'),
        type: 'sourceFile',
      };
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  // 5. Resolve AssetRegistry for Android and iOS
  if (
    moduleName === 'react-native/asset-registry' ||
    moduleName === 'react-native/Libraries/Image/AssetRegistry' ||
    moduleName.endsWith('/asset-registry')
  ) {
    try {
      return {
        filePath: require.resolve('@react-native/assets-registry/registry.js'),
        type: 'sourceFile',
      };
    } catch (e) {
      try {
        return {
          filePath: require.resolve('react-native/Libraries/Image/AssetRegistry'),
          type: 'sourceFile',
        };
      } catch (err) {}
    }
  }

  return context.resolveRequest(context, moduleName, platform);
};

module.exports = config;
